paṇpāṭṭu varalāṟu
nūṟkaḷañciyam - tokuti mūṉṟu
tamiḻnāṭṭut tiruviḷakkukaḷ












cāttaṉkuḷam
a. irākavaṉ













amiḻtam patippakam

cāttaṉkuḷam a. irākavaṉ nūṟkaḷañciyam tokuti mūṉṟu
tamiḻnāṭṭut tiruviḷakkukaḷ | cāttaṉkuḷam a. irākavaṉ | 
patippāḷar : i. vaḷarmati | mutal patippu : 1964 | maṟu patippu : 2005 | 
tāḷ : 18.6 ki mēplittō | aḷavu : 1/8 temmi | eḻuttu : 10.5 puḷḷi | 
pakkam : 32+192 = 224 | nūl kaṭṭamippu : iyalpu (cātāraṇam) | 
vili : urupā. 210 | paṭikaḷ : 1000 | nūlākkam : caravaṇaṉ, aṭṭi vaṭivamippu : i. iṉiyaṉ, pāvāṇar kaṇiṉi, tiyākarāyar nakar, 
ceṉṉi - 17 | acciṭṭōr : veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ piriṇṭar āyiram viḷakku, ceṉṉi - 6 | veḷiyīṭu : amiḻtam patippakam, 
pi-11, kulmōkar kuṭiyiruppu, 15, teṟku pōkku cāli, tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi - 600 017 | kiṭikkumiṭam : tamiḻmaṇ patippakam, 2 ciṅkāravēlar teru, tiyākarāyarnakar ceṉṉi - 600 017, to.pē: 2433 9030
innūlākkattiṟku utaviyavarkaḷ :
pērā. vī. aracu maṟṟum āyvāḷar, ira. piruntāvati.

patippuri
tamiḻmoḻikkum tamiḻiṉattiṟkum, vaḷamum valimiyum cērkkiṉṟa nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭuvati nōkkamākak koṇṭu em patippakam toṭaṅkap paṭṭatu. tamiḻici aṟiñar āpirakām paṇṭitar, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar, paṉmoḻip pulavar appātturiyār, tamiḻīḻa aṟiñar na.ci. kantiyā, centamiḻ antaṇar irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār, paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpāliyar, muṉivar irā. iḷavaracu pōṉṟa aṟiñar perumakkaḷ eḻutiya nūl kaḷiyum, olkāp pukaḻ tolkāppiyattiyum oru cēra veḷiyiṭṭut tamiḻnūl patippil taṉimuttirip patittatit tamiḻulakam aṟiyum.
anta aṭiccuvaṭṭil tamiḻmoḻikkup perumi cērkkiṉṟa nūlkaḷi mīḷpatippu ceyvataṟku em paṇiyit toṭarnta nērattil nuṇkaliccelvar cāttaṉkuḷam a. irākavaṉ avarkaḷ eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum em patippakam veḷiyiṭṭāl moḻikkum iṉattiṟkum yām ituvariyilum ceyta paṇikku atu mēlum valimi cērkkum eṉṟum avaruṭiya nūlkaḷ veḷivaruvatu mikamika iṉṟi yamiyātatu eṉṟum ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka tamiḻilakkiyattuṟiyiṉ talivar pērā. vī. aracu avarkaḷ terivittār. avariṉ vaḻikāṭṭutaliṉ pēril innūlkaḷ veḷivarukiṉṟaṉa.
nuṇkalic celvar irākavaṉ avarkaḷ eḻuti avaruṭiya kālattil nūlkaḷāka veḷivantavaṟṟip poruḷ vāriyākap pirittu nūṟkaḷañciyamāka uṅkaḷ kikaḷil tavaḻaviṭṭuḷḷōm. marapu karuti mūla nūlil uḷḷavāṟē veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm. ivi maṭṭumaṉṟi kuṭiyaracu, jaṉacakti, aṟivu, tamiḻmuracu itaḻ kaḷil veḷivanta avaruṭiya kaṭṭurikaḷiyum tokuttu virivil veḷiyiṭavirukkiṟōm.
inta nūlkaḷ ceppamākavum nalla vaṭivamip pōṭum varuvataṟku uriya vaḻikāṭṭutal tantu pallāṟ ṟāṉum tuṇi iruntu utaviyavar pērā. vī. aracu āvār. mēlum, avarē innūlkaḷ ovvoṉṟukkum taṉittaṉiyē matippuri aḷittuc ciṟappu ceytuḷ ḷār. ivarukku em neñcārnta naṉṟi eṉṟum uriyatākum. celvi ira. piruntāvati, pērā. aracu avarkaḷiṉ āyvu māṇavar. ivar pērāciriyariṉ vaḻi kāṭṭutalōṭu palvēṟu vakiyil paṅkāṟṟiyum in nūlkaḷ piḻiyiṉṟi varuvataṟku meyppup pārttum utaviṉār. celvi piruntāvati avarkaḷi naṉṟi yuṇarvōṭu pārāṭṭukiṟēṉ.
tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattic cērnta pulavar mutturāmaliṅkam avarkaḷ pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭapōtu pallāṟṟāṉum tuṇi yirunta perumikkuriyavar. avar in nūlāciriyariṉ taṅki vīralakkumi ammiyāriṭamum, makaṉ irā. mativāṇa ṉiṭamum urimiyuri vāṅki utaviyatōṭu it tokutikaḷ veḷi varuvataṟkup peritum tuṇi iruntār. avarukkum em naṉṟi. innūl tokuti kaḷik kaṇiṉi ākkam ceytu utaviya tirumati. celvi (kuṭvil kaṇiṉi) avarkaḷukkum, meyppup pārttu utaviya ki. kuṇat tokiyaṉ, celvi piruntāvati, celvi kaliyaraci, celvi kōkilā ākiyōrkkum,  nūlkaḷ naṉmuṟiyil varuvataṟku mikavum utaviyāka irunta kumarēcaṉ, irāmaṉ, ciṟanta vakiyil vaṭivamittu oḻuṅkupaṭuttiya kaṇiṉi iyakkunar caravaṇaṉ, mēlaṭṭiyi aḻakuṟa vaṭivamippu ceyta iṉiyaṉ maṟṟum piṟa vakikaḷil tuṇi irunta veṅkaṭēcaṉ, taṉacēkaraṉ, cupramaṇiyaṉ āki yōrkku em naṉṟiyum, pārāṭṭum.
innūl tokutikaḷ tamiḻ āyvāḷarkaḷukkum tamiḻ uṇarvāḷarkaḷukkum peritum payaṉpaṭattakka ariya nūlkaḷākum. etirkālattil ippaṭippaṭṭa nūlkaḷit toṭarntu veḷiyiṭuvatil uṟutiyākap paṇiyāṟṟuvōm.

	patippāḷar

urimiyuri
nuṇkalic celvar cāttaṉkuḷam a. irākavaṉ avarkaḷ tamiḻkkalikaḷ paṟṟi virivāka āyvu 
ceytu nūlkaḷ eḻutiyavar. avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ ippoḻutu mīṇṭum accāvatu eṅkaḷukku mikunta makiḻcciyi aḷikkiṟatu. innūlkaḷ mīṇṭum accākumā? eṉṟa aiyattil irunta eṅkaḷukku ic ceyal mikunta makiḻcci aḷikkum ceyalākum. tamiḻar paṇpāṭṭu varalāṟu, kali varalāṟu, toḻil nuṭpa varalāṟu, tolporuḷāyvu varalāṟu ākiya pala tuṟikaḷil nuṇkalic celvar irākavaṉār eḻutiya nūlkaḷit tamiḻulakam pōṟṟip pārāṭṭum eṉṟa nampikki eṅkaḷukku uṇṭu.
pala nūlkaḷ kiṭittum cila nūlkaḷ kiṭik kāmalum iruntatik kaṇṭu kavali aṭinta eṅkaḷukku amiḻtam patippakattār mūlam in nūlkaḷ veḷi varuvatu eṅkaḷ kuṭumpattiṟkum tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkum perum ciṟappu eṉṟē karutukiṟōm. tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ avarkaḷukku eṅkaḷatu naṉṟi eṉṟum uriyatu. amiḻtam patippakattiṉ mūlam innūli veḷiyiṭum tiru i. iṉiyaṉ avarkaḷi nāṅkaḷ peritum pōṟṟip pārāṭṭukiṟōm. amiḻtam patippakattār nuṇkalic celvar nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭuvati vāḻtti varavēṟṟu makiḻkiṟōm.

irā. mativāṇaṉ
tirunelvēli	(aṟiñar a. irākavaṉiṉ makaṉ)
30.12.2005	kā. vīralaṭcumi ammiyār
(aṟiñar a. irākavaṉiṉ taṅki)

matippuri
caṭaṅku - ciṟpam- kali varalāṟu
makkaḷ tam muṉṉōr varalāṟṟi ārāyntu avarkaḷ peṟṟa veṟṟiyiyum tōlviyiyum naṉkuṇarntu tam vāḻkkiyil veṟṟi peṟumāṟu tam vāḻkki muṟikaḷi amittuk koḷvatē muṉṉēṟṟam aṭinta nāṭṭiluḷḷa aṟivuṭiya makkaḷ kaṇṭa anupavamuṟi; āṉāl tamiḻarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟi iṉṟum cīriya muṟiyil eḻuti muṭikkavilli eṉpati nāṉ naṉkuṇar vēṉ. eṉṟālum, tamiḻarkaḷ tamnāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟōṭu taṅkaḷ nāṭṭiṉ moḻi, ici, nāṭakam, nāṭṭiyam, aṇi, ālayam, āṭi, ilakkiyam, uṟiviṭam, kuṭi, ceruppu, camayam, kācu, viḷakku pōṉṟavikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟiyum naṉkuṇaravēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉatu pēravāvākum.
aṟiñar irākavaṉ avarkaḷ, innūliṉ tōṟṟuvāy eṉṉum pakutiyil mēṟkuṟittak karuttip pativu ceytuḷḷār. avar pativu ceytuḷḷavaṟṟil, ici, aṇi, kācu, viḷakku ākiya tuṟikaḷ cār varalāṟṟi avarē eḻutiyuḷḷār. ippiṉpulattil, viḷakkukaḷ kuṟitta varalāṟākat tamiḻnāṭṭut tiruviḷakkukaḷ eṉṉum innūl amikiṟatu.
maṉitarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ vāḻkkiyil payaṉpaṭuttak kaṟṟukkoṇṭa poruḷkaḷil, aṟputamāṉatu neruppu. iyaṟkiyil uḷḷa vētipporuṭkaḷ, tammuḷ ēṟpaṭuttik koḷḷum etirviṉikaḷ cārntē neruppu uṇṭākiṟatu. maṉitarkaḷ neruppi aṟiyāta kālam oṉṟiruntatu. neruppi aṟiyat toṭaṅkiya pōtutāṉ; iyaṟkip poruḷic ceyaṟkiyāka uruvākkum kaliyik kaṟṟuk koḷḷa muṭintatu. itil uṇavu camittal mutaliṭam peṟukiṟatu. neruppu mūlam kiṭikkum oḷiyi niliyākap piṭittu vittukkoḷḷa maṉitaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa muyaṟciyē viḷakku. tolpaḻam varalāṟṟut toṭarcciyuḷḷa tamiḻc camūkattil, neruppip payaṉpaṭuttum muṟikaḷil oṉṟāṉa viḷakkiṟkum toṭarcciyāṉa varalāṟu iruppatik kāṇkiṟōm. aimpūtaṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉa neruppu, maṉitarkaḷiṉ vaḻipaṭu poruḷāka vaṭivam peṟṟa pōtu, ataṉ viḷivākavē viḷakku vaṭivamikkap paṭṭatu. vaḻipāṭukaḷukku viḷakku payaṉpaṭuttuvataṟku muṉpu, maṉitarkaḷukku atu evvakiyil payaṉpaṭṭatu eṉpatu kuṟittu aṟivataṟku tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kāṭṭum tiṇi vāḻkki muṟi naṟcāṉṟāka amikiṟatu.
kuṟiñci maṟṟum mulli nilaṅkaḷil kallāl uruvākkappaṭṭa viḷakku; marutanilap pakutiyil maṇṇāl ceyyappaṭṭa viḷakku; neytal pakutiyil caṅkāl ceyyappaṭṭa viḷakku ākiyavi, puḻakkattil iruntami kuṟittu innūl ciṟappāka āyvu ceytiruppatik kāṇkiṟōm. maṉitarkaḷ tāṅkaḷ vāḻum pakutikaḷil kiṭikkum poruṭkaḷiyē taṅkaḷ tēvikaṭkup payaṉpaṭuttik koḷkiṟārkaḷ eṉpati itaṉ mūlam nām uṇara muṭikiṟatu. tamiḻ ilakkiyam kāṭṭum tiṇi cār vāḻkki marapu eṉpatu iyaṟki neṟi cār vāḻmuṟiyōṭu koṇ ṭuḷḷa uṟaviyum itaṉmūlam purintukoḷḷalām.
iyaṟkip poruḷāṉa neruppi, ceyaṟki yāka uruvākkappaṭṭa viḷakku eṉṟa amippōṭu maṉitarkaḷ iṇittirukkiṟārkaḷ. inta iṇippiṉ mūlam palvēṟu putiya vaḷarccikkum vaḻi kaṇṭirup patik kāṇkiṟōm. ivviḷakku maṉitarkaḷiṉ nampikkiyip pulappaṭuttum caṭaṅkukaḷāka vaṭivam peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. caṭaṅkukaḷāka vaṭivam peṟumpōtu, caṭaṅkupporuḷ, putīkap poruḷākavum urup peṟukiṟatu. appōtu uruvaṅkaḷ uruvākkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.  itaṉmūlam ōviyaṅkaḷum ciṟpaṅkaḷum uṟuppeṟukiṉṟaṉa. tirāviṭac ciṟpakkali vaḷarcci yil viḷakkukaḷ palvēṟu nuṭpaṅkaḷik koṇṭa tāka amintuḷḷaṉa. ivvakiyil, tamiḻakac ciṟpa vakikaḷil, viḷakkuc ciṟpattiṟku mutaṉmiyāṉa iṭam iruppati aṟikiṟōm. innūl iv varalāṟṟic ciṟappākap pativu ceytiruppatik kāṇ kiṟōm. kiviḷakkukaḷ, kuttu viḷakkukaḷ, tūkku viḷakkukaḷ, tūṇviḷakkukaḷ, pāvi viḷakkukaḷ ākiya piṟa viḷakkukaḷiṉ amippukaḷ, mēṟkuṟittap piṉpulattil purintu koḷḷa ētuvāka iruppatik kāṇkiṟōm.
maṉitarkaḷiṉ nampikki cārnta nilikaḷil oḷi x iruṭṭu eṉṟa irumi āḻamāka iṭampeṟ ṟiruppatik kāṇkiṟōm. maṉita vāḻkkiyiṉ kuṟiyīṭāka ivvirumip payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṟatu. paḻaṅkālattil iḷampeṇṇi viḷakku muṉ amara vittu, avaḷatu kaḻuttil tirumaṇa nāṇ pūṭṭi ṉārkaḷ. icceyal oḷikkum maṉita vāḻkkikku māṉa nampikki, maṉitarkaḷiṭam iṭam peṟṟati uṟutippaṭuttukiṟatu. ivvakiyāṉa, puritalukku innūlil kāṇappaṭum pala ceytikaḷ utavukiṉṟaṉa. iyaṟki nikaḻvu - maṉita nampikki maṟṟum caṭaṅku - neruppiṉ iṭam - maṉitarkaḷiṉ vāḻkki - itaṉ vaḻi kaṭṭamikkappaṭum paṇpāṭu eṉpatil viḷakkiṉ iṭatti nām purintu koḷḷalām.  
aṟiñar irākavaṉ, tamiḻakattil viḷakkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa muṟikaḷi varalāṟṟup pōkkil pativu ceytuḷḷār. ippativiṉ mūlam pala ceyti kaḷi nām purintu koḷḷa muṭikiṟatu. tiṇicār paṇpāṭṭu vaḷarcci, viḷakkil uruvākum cuṭar kaḷik koṇṭu vaḻipaṭum muṟi, oḷi vaḻipāṭu eṉpatu evvakiyil piṉṉar viḻāvāka vaṭivam peṟṟatu ākiya piṟa ceytikaḷi innūl virivāka vivātikkiṟatu. innūl pulappaṭuttum ceytikaḷik koṇṭu, evvakiyāṉa paṇpāṭṭu varalāṟṟik kaṭṭamikka muṭiyum eṉpatu innūṟ payaṉāka amiyum.
maṉita camūka varalāṟu; tol paḻam kāla maṉitarkaḷ, piṉṉar paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarcciyaṭiyum māṟṟaṅkaḷ, ataṉ mūlam ēṟpaṭum viḷivukaḷ eṉṟē amivatik kāṇkiṟōm. ippōkkil maṉitarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttum poruṭkaḷē, mēṟkuṟitta varalāṟṟi aṟivataṟku aṭippaṭikaḷāka amikiṉṟaṉa. ip poruṭkaḷ, aṭippaṭiyil iyaṟkiyil uḷḷa nilam, nīr, kāṟṟu, neruppu, vāṉam ākiyavaṟṟic cārntē amikiṉṟaṉa. ivi ovvoṉṟum maṉita camūkattiṉ varalāṟṟil, pal parimāṇa vaḷarcci peṟuvatik kāṇkiṟōm. innūl  avvakiyil, neruppu  kuṟitta varalāṟṟi aṟiyum aṭippaṭit taravukaḷik koṇṭiruppatik kāṇkiṟōm. it taravum tamiḻaka varalāṟṟup piṉpulatti aṭippaṭi yākak koṇṭē vivātikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ip poruḷi, ikkaṇṇōṭṭattil vivātikkum mutal nūlākavum innūl amikiṟatu. camūka varalāṟṟu māṇavarkaḷ, ciṟpikaḷ, ōviyarkaḷ, navīṉa iḷam aṟiviyal aṟiñarkaḷ ākiya paltuṟic cārnta varkaḷukkum utavum vakiyil innūl ceytikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. mēṟkuṟitta ovvoruvarum avaravar kōṇattil purintu koḷḷavum innūl utavum.
kuṟippiṭṭa tēcīya iṉattiṉ, paṇpāṭṭu varalāṟṟi aṟivataṟku, avviṉam vāḻum nilap pakutikaḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷa poruṭkaḷē mutaṉmit taravukaḷāka amikiṉṟaṉa. tirāviṭak kaṭṭiṭakkali marapi aṟivataṟkuc ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ palvēṟu kūṟukaḷ, aṭippaṭikaḷāka amikiṉṟaṉa. ciṟpaṅkaḷ uruvākkappaṭṭuḷḷa muṟimi eṉpatu oru puṟamirukka, ententa vaṭivaṅkaḷi, uruvaṅkaḷi ciṟpikaḷ taṅkaḷatu ciṟpanuṭpattik kāṭṭa eṭuttuk koḷkiṟārkaḷ eṉpatum mukkiyamāka amikiṟatu. tamiḻakattiṉ ciṟpaṅkaḷil mika virivāka iṭam peṟṟiruppatu viḷakkukaḷē ākum. kaṭavuḷarkaṭku koṭutta aṭimoḻiyāṉa tiru eṉpati viḷakku kaḷukkum koṭuttirukkum immarapil, avviḷakku kaḷic cetukkuvataṟku mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭa nuṭpaṅ kaḷum ciṟappāka amivatip purintu koḷḷa muṭikiṟatu. eṉavē, viḷakkukaḷ paṟṟiya innūliṉ vivātam eṉpatu, tavirkka iyalāta vakiyil, avaṟṟi uruvākkum vaṭivaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya āyvōṭum iṇintu koḷkiṟatu. ciṟpamum ōviyamum ivvaki yil mutaṉmiyāka amikiṉṟaṉa. maṉitarkaḷ, tam vāḻviṭaṅkaḷukkāṉa vaṭiva amippi uruvākkik koḷvatil palvēṟu muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟu kiṟārkaḷ. kāṟṟu, oḷi ākiyavi avviṭaṅkaḷil evvakiyil iṭampeṟa vēṇṭum eṉpatil akkaṟiyāka irukkiṟārkaḷ. itil iyaṟki oḷikkāṉa amippiyum, ceyaṟki oḷikkāṉa amippiyum vēṟupaṭutti amikkiṟārkaḷ. vāḻviṭa veḷikaḷil, viḷakku oḷi eppaṭi amiya vēṇṭum eṉpatiṉ vaṭivamākavē, viḷakkukaḷ amikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvakiyil, tēvi-poruḷiṉ vaṭivam-ciṟpattiṉ nuṭpam ākiyavi tamakkuḷ koṇṭuḷḷa uṟavi aṟiya muṭikiṟatu. innūlil  pēcappaṭṭuḷḷa palvēṟu viḷakkukaḷiṉ amippu muṟikaḷi, mēṟkuṟittap piṉpulattil aṇuka muṭiyum. avvitam aṇukum pōtu, tamiḻ nilappakutiyil vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ vāḻviṭa amippiṟkum avarkaḷāl uruvākkappaṭṭa ciṟpa amippiṟkumāṉa uṟavip purintu koḷḷa muṭiyum. ippuritalukku viḷakkukaḷ kuṟitta innūl takavalkaḷ peritum utavukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ ivvakiyāṉa maṟṟum pala puḻaṅku poruṭkaḷip purintu koḷḷa vum ivvaṭippaṭi utavakkūṭum.
tolpaḻaṅkālattil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa viḷakkukaḷiṉ marapu evvakiyil, navīṉa viḷakkukaḷ uruvākkattil utavukiṟatu eṉpatum mukkiyamākum. miṉcāram kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa piṟaku, aṇmik kālaṅkaḷil uruvākkappaṭum viḷakkukaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷ, tolmarapiṉ celvākki uḷvāṅkiyiruppatik kāṇkiṟōm. oḷiyi umiḻum iḻikaḷ oṉṟāka iruppiṉum, avviḻikaḷic cuṟṟi amikkappaṭum vaṭivaṅkaḷ, palvēṟu vakiyil iruppatik kāṇki ṟōm. ivi namatu ciṟpak kaliyiṉ toṭarcci yākavē uḷḷaṉa. ulakiṉ palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷa viḷakkukaḷiṉ amippiṟkum, atip payaṉpaṭuttiya makkaḷ kūṭṭattiṉ nampikki maṟṟum caṭaṅku cārnta uṟavukaḷukkum iṭiyē uḷḷa taṉmikaḷiyum māṉiṭaviyal kaṇṇōṭṭattil purintu koḷḷa viḷakkukaḷ utavukiṉṟaṉa. ivvakiyil, innūl taṉittaṉmiyuṭaṉ iruppatik kāṇkiṟōm.
kali varalāṟu paṟṟip pēcumpōtu, pēcappaṭum ceytikaḷukkāṉa taravukaḷik kāṭci vaṭivil anupavippatu avaciyam. itaṉi niṟivēṟṟum vakiyil innūlil amintirukkum paṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. tām 200 mēṟpaṭṭa viḷakku vakikaḷic cēkarittu vittiruntatākavum irākavaṉ kūṟukiṟār. mēṟkuṟitta ceytikku, icceyal mēlum valu vūṭṭum vakiyil amintuḷḷatu.
tamiḻnāṭṭut tiruviḷakkukaḷ eṉum innūl, iṉṟiya cūḻalil, ententak kūṟukaḷil, namatu kavaṉattukkuriyatāka amikiṟatu eṉpatum mukkiyam.
-	tamiḻarkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭṭu varalāṟṟil oḷikkum ataṉi veḷippaṭuttiya viḷakkukkum irunta mukkiyattuvatti aṟiya muṭikiṟatu. iyaṟki vaḻipāṭē tamiḻarkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭu eṉṟa karuttākkatti uṟuti ceytu koḷḷa innūl aṭippaṭiyāka amikiṟatu.
-	tamiḻarkaḷiṉ ciṟpa varalāṟṟil, avarkaḷatu nampikkikaḷukkum avi cārnta caṭaṅku kaḷukkum uḷḷa uṟavi aṟiyavum innūl peritum utavum.
aṟiñar irākavaṉ, tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kali varalāṟ ṟiṉ pala kūṟukaḷi āvaṇappaṭuttuvatil akkaṟi celuttiyuḷḷār. kali varalāṟṟiṉ mūlamē paṇ pāṭṭu varalāṟṟik kaṭṭamikka iyalum. ivvaki yil tamiḻar paṇpāṭṭu varalāṟṟi kaṭṭamittu, ataṉ mūlam tamiḻc camūka varalāṟu eḻuta utavum ariya taravukaḷil oṉṟāka innūl amikiṟatu.
innūli veḷiyiṭum amiḻtam patippakattiṉ muyaṟciyi peritum pārāṭṭa vēṇṭiya kaṭami namakku uṇṭu.

pērāciriyar, talivar	vī. aracu
tamiḻ ilakkiyat tuṟi	
ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻakam.


tōṟṟuvāy
viḷakku tamiḻarkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟōṭu toṭarpuṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷ. atu paṇṭu toṭṭu iṉṟuvari tamiḻarkaḷiṉ illattil oru matippiṟkuriya maṅkalap poru ḷāka matikkappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.
inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil tamiḻnāṭṭi luḷḷa ovvoru ciṟṟūrukkum miṉ viḷakkukaḷ vantuviṭṭaṉa. eṇṇey vārttu erikkum paḻiya viḷakkukaḷukku avaciya millāta nili ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭatu.
eṉṟālum iṉṟu ovvoru tamiḻariṉ vīṭṭilum paḻiya viḷakkukaḷ iruntē varukiṉṟaṉa. avikaḷ veṟum kāṭcip poruḷāka vikkappaṭavilli. ovvoru nāḷum māliyil avikaḷi naṉṟāy viḷakki eṇṇey vārttut tiriyiṭṭup pūviṭṭu neruppēṟṟi vaṇaṅkip pirārttik kiṉṟaṉar. ataṉ piṉṉartāṉ miṉ viḷakkēṟṟuvar. tiruviḷak kēṟṟiya piṉṉartāṉ piḷḷikaḷ pāṭam paṭikka ārampippar; viḷakkēṟṟiya piṉtāṉ vīṭṭiṉ talivi iravil ceyya vēṇṭiya taṉatu kāriyaṅkaḷik kavaṉippāḷ.
tamiḻarkaḷ perumpālum civarākavō viṇava rākavō iruppar. ivarkaḷ illaṅkaḷil civaṉuṭiya uruvaṅ kaḷō, tirumāliṉ uruvaṅkaḷō illāmal irukkalām; āṉāl tiruviḷakku illāmal oru illamum irātu.
tiruviḷakkic civarkaḷ civaṉuṭiya ciṉṉamākavum, viṇarkaḷ tirumāliṉ ciṉṉamākavum eṇṇi vaḻipaṭukiṉ ṟaṉar. āṉāl ellōrum tiruviḷakki, viḷakku nācciyār eṉṟum tīpalaṭcumi eṉṟum aḻikkiṉṟaṉar.
civarkaḷukkum viṇavarkaḷukkum potuvāṉa teyvamāka ari araṉ iruppatu pōl iru camayattārkkum tiruviḷakkup potuvāṉa teyvamāka iruntu varukiṟatu.
tamiḻarkaḷ viḷakkit teyvac ciṉṉamāka matittu vaḻi paṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. atōṭu mēṉāṭṭiṉar kaṇṭa meḻuku vartti viḷakkip pōl vikkātu atil kalinayam poliya aḻakuṟa amittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻ makkaḷ viḷakki tiruviḷakku eṉṟaṉar atil kali amcattip pukuttiṉar; pōṟṟiṉar; vaṇaṅkiṉar. āṉāl atip paṟṟi tamiḻarkaḷ ituvari oru nalla nūl iyaṟṟiṉar eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkilli.
eṉavē, nīṇṭa nāḷāka viḷakkip paṟṟi oru nalla ārāycci nūl veḷivara vēṇṭum eṉa ācippaṭṭuk koṇṭu iruntēṉ. ippoḻutu tāṉ ati niṟivēṟṟum vāyppuk kiṭittatu.
tiruviḷakkiṉ kalic ciṟappu
viḷakkiṉ virinta varalāṟāka nāṉ iṅku etuvum variya virumpavilli. viḷakkiṉ virivāṉa varalāṟṟi eḻuta eṇṇi ṉāl maṉitaṉ ceyaṟki muṟiyil neruppi uṇṭākkiya vitattiyum, appāl avaṉ eṇṇeyik kaṇṭa muṟiyi yum, appāl kalviḷakku, maṇ viḷakku, ulōka viḷakkup pōṉṟavikaḷi uruvākkiya muṟikaḷiyum ulakiluḷḷa ovvoru nāṭṭilum viḷakkup pariṇamitta vitaṅkaḷiyum viḷakka vēṇṭum.
nāṉ viḷakkiṉ tōṟṟattip paṟṟiyum neyyiṉ api viruttiyip paṟṟiyum oruvāṟu ārāyntuḷḷēṉ. eṉṟālum tamiḻk kaliyi vaḷarkka muṉ vantu niṟkum eṉakkut tiruviḷakkil kāṇum kali amcamē eṉṉip peritum kavarntuḷḷatu. eṉṟālum varalāṟum eṉṉi viṭṭu viṭavilli.
āṅkilattil, tēciya viḷakkukaḷ - atāvatu teru viḷakkukaḷ vanta varalāṟṟip paṟṟiyum aṉittulaka viḷakkiṉ katikaḷip paṟṟiyum pala kaṭṭurikaḷum nūṟkaḷum veḷi vantuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟi nāṉ naṉku paṭittuḷḷēṉ. eṉṟālum nāṉ aṟinta variyil āṅkilattil kūṭa niṟivuṭiya nūlkaḷ veḷivantiruppatākat teriyavilli.
tiru.em.ṭapiḷyū. irāpiṉ avarkaḷāl āṅkilattil eḻutappaṭṭa `viḷakkiṉ varalāṟum, meḻukuvartti viḷakku kaḷum eṉṟa oru nūlip pārttirukkiṉṟēṉ. annūlikkūṭa ellāk kālaṅkaḷilum ellā nāṭukaḷilum eḻunta ellā viḷakkukaḷiyum viḷakkēṟṟum cātaṉaṅkaḷiyum viññāṉa rītiyāka eḻutappaṭṭa oru niṟivāṉa viḷakkiṉ kalik kaḷañciyam eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātu.
eṉ vīṭṭil 200 viḷakkukaḷ
nāṉ eṉatu illattil cērttu vittirukkum 200kku mēṟpaṭṭa viḷakkukaḷiyum tamiḻnāṭu, kēraḷam, āntiram mutaliya nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa kōyilkaḷilum vīṭukaḷilum tol poruḷ kāṭcicālikaḷilumuḷḷa āyirakkaṇakkāṉa viḷakku kaḷiyum pārttu ārāyntuḷḷēṉ!
500kku mēṟpaṭṭa ariya viḷakkukaḷiṉ niḻaṟpaṭaṅ kaḷiyum cēkarittu vittuḷḷēṉ. ivikaḷip paṟṟit tamiḻilum āṅkilattilum eḻutiya pala kaṭṭurikaḷiyum aracāṅkat tol poruḷ kāṭcicāliyiliruntu veḷi yiṭappaṭṭa aṟikkikaḷiyum, viḷakkip paṟṟiya veḷi nāṭṭup pirayāṇikaḷ eḻutiya kuṟippukaḷiyum pala camayac cāṉṟōrkaḷ eḻutiya pāṭṭukaḷiyum viḷakkiṟkākap pala paktarkaḷum, maṉṉarkaḷum eḻutivitta cācaṉaṅkaḷiyum māṉiyaṅkaḷiyum paṟṟi ārāyntirukkiṉṟēṉ.
1955ām āṇṭu mutal viḷakkip paṟṟi oru nalla nūl eḻuta vēṇṭum eṉṟa avāvōṭu pala kuṟippukaḷi eḻuti vittuḷḷēṉ. eṉavē ituvari viḷakkip paṟṟi nāṉ palvēṟu camayaṅkaḷilum eḻuti vitta kuṟippukaḷiyum aṟiñar kaḷiṉ moḻikaḷiyum piṟa cātaṉaṅkaḷiyum ārāyntu viḷakkippaṟṟi oru nalla ārāycciyi iṅku eḻuta muṉ vantuḷḷēṉ.
muppatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ nāṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭup pakuttaṟivuc caṅka amiccarākavum `aṟivu eṉṟa pattiriki āciriyarākavum, cuya mariyāti iyakkap piramukarākavum iruntēṉ. aṉṟu eṉakkuk kali, kippāka iruntatu. kōyilkaḷ kuccuk kārikaḷiṉ illam pōl kāṇap paṭukiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiya vārttikaḷ cevik kamutāy iruntatu. camaya ñāṉikaḷellām cōṟṟukkākap pitaṟṟum pittar kaḷākak kāṇappaṭṭārkaḷ.
eṉṟālum vaṭalūr irāmaliṅkam piḷḷi eṉakku oru camaraca caṉmārkka ñāṉiyākavum uṇmiyi urikkum uyar neṟiyāḷarākavum uttamarākavum kāṇappaṭṭār.
eṉavē eṉatu uḻuvalaṉpar cāmi citamparaṉār. eṅkaḷ karuttukkicinta irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷiṉ pāṭalkaḷit tiraṭṭi acciṭumpaṭi koṭuttār. ataṉi irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷiṉ pāṭal tiraṭṭu eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ aṉṟu kuṭi aracup patippakattiṉ mūlam oru ciṟu nūli veḷiyiṭṭēṉ.
atu mutal irāmaliṅka cuvamikaḷi nāṅkaḷ cuya mariyātikkāraril oruvarāka matittu vantōm. iṉṟum matittu varukiṟōm. avaratu pāṭalil eṉatu uḷḷattik kavarnta pāṭal oṉṟuṇṭu. ataṉi iṅku kuṟippiṭāmal irukka muṭiyavilli. atu varumāṟu :
aruṭpāvil viḷakku
aruḷoḷi viḷaṅkiṭa āṇava meṉumōr
iruḷaṟa eṉṉuḷat tēṟṟiya viḷakkē
tuṉpuṟu tattuvat turicelām viḷakkē nīkkinal
iṉpuṟa eṉṉuḷat tēṟṟiya viḷakkē
maya laṟa vaḻiyā vāḻvu mēṉ mēlum
iyaluṟa veṉṉuḷat tēṟṟiya viḷakkē
naṭuveḷi naṭuvē nāṭṭiya viḷakkē
karu veḷi yaṉittuṅ katiroḷi viḷaṅkiṭa
uruveḷi naṭuvē oḷitaru viḷakkē
ippāṭalē pakuttaṟivu vātiyāy irunta eṉakku viḷakkip paṟṟi eḻuta ūkkam aḷittatu.
niṟka, ceyaṟki muṟiyil oḷiyi uṟpatti ceytu atip payaṉpaṭuttum mukkiya vicayaṅkaḷiyum kuṭumpattilum, camūkattilum, vāḻkkiyilum, caṭaṅkilum, ceyaṟki oḷik kuḷḷa mukkiyattuvattiyum ataṉuṭiya aṟiya varalāṟṟiṟku nām aḷittirukkum coṟpak kavaṉattiyum cintitta pōtu, atu eṉakku mikavum āccariyamāka iruntatu.
palvēṟu nāṭukaḷilum, palvēṟu cakāptaṅkaḷilum viḷakkiṉ oḷiyip paṟṟiya karuttukaḷi viḷakkamāka eḻutap paṭṭa muṟkālak kuṟippukaḷ etuvum ituvari namakkuk kiṭṭa villi. taṟkālak kuṟippukaḷ kūṭa namakkuc carivarak kiṭikka villi. mēlum, vaṇṇa ōviyarkaḷum, uruveḻutukiṟavar kaḷum taṅkaḷatu kālaṅkaḷil toṭarcciyāy mēṉāṭṭu iravu vāḻkkiyip paṟṟi aṟinta kuṟippukaḷik koṇṭu ariya ōviyaṅkaḷ pala tīṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
tamiḻ nāṭṭilō caṅkilum, kallilum, maṇṇilum, veṇkalattilum viḷakkukaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa.
ōviyattil veḷiccattiṉ.... tōṟṟattik kāṭṭāmal iravu vāḻkkiyi vivarikka muṭiyātu.
ulāntara muṟiyil kali payiṉṟa ōviya vallunarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ uṭpuṟak kāṭciyiṉ nuṇukkatti veḷikkāṭṭat tāṅkaḷ tīṭṭiya paṭaṅkaḷil viḷakkiṉ oḷiyi naṉṟākak kāṭṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. perumpālāṉa aṟikaḷil veḷiccam tarum karuvikaḷiṉ pakutikaḷi ōviyarkaḷ kāṭṭa maṟuppatē illi. cila camayaṅkaḷil viḷakkiṟku avaciyam illāmal iruntālum oru mūliyil oru ciṟu viḷakkit tīṭṭat tavaṟuvatē illi.
maṉita camūka vaḷarcciyiṉ ārampak kaṭṭattiliruntu iṉṟu vari - atāvatu makkaḷ iṉattiṉ kāṭṭumirāṇṭi vāḻkkiyiliruntu iṉṟiya naṉi ciṟanta nākarika vāḻkkiyiṉ ucca nili vari, oḷiyum atiliruntu arumpiya akalviḷakkum vāḻkkikku vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāta ariya cātaṉamāka ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
oḷi tarum tiruviḷakku, ulakiṉ iruḷi akaṟṟi iravip pakalāka māṟṟukiṟatu. aññāṉatti akaṟṟi meyñ ñāṉatti viḷakkukiṟatu.
makkaḷiṉ iruṇṭa uḷḷattiṟku aṟivuc cuṭar ūṭṭum ñāyiṟākat tikaḻkiṟatu. vāḻkkikku, uṇarcciyum, ūkkamum, uṟcākamum ūṭṭukiṟatu. kuḷir kālattil koṭiya paṉiyāl naṭunaṭuṅkum makkaḷukku oḷi cūṭēṟṟi uṭampiṟku oru tempi uṇṭākkukiṟatu.
makkaḷukku vēṇṭiya uṇavip pakkuvappaṭutti uṭampi urañ ceyya utavukiṟatu. oḷiyē ulakil makkaḷukku uyirāka oḷirkiṟatu. makkaḷ uṭampil oḷiyiṉ veppam illā viṭṭāl avarkaḷ iṟantu viṭṭavarkaḷākak karutappaṭu kiṟārkaḷ.
eṉavē, paṇṭit tamiḻaṉ oḷiyi iṟivaṉākak kaṇṭāṉ. `cōtiyē cuṭarē, cūḻ oḷi viḷakkē eṉṟu pukaḻntu pāṭiṉāṉ. iṟivaṉi `viṟakil tīyiṉaṉ eṉṟum, `maṟiya niṉṟuḷāṉ māmaṇic cōtiyāṉ eṉṟum iṟiñciṉāṉ.
maṉita vāḻviṟku vaḷam aḷikkum makaḷi avaṉ kuṭumpa viḷakku eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. oḷiyāl iravilum iruṇṭa mēkaṅkaḷ cūḻnta pakalilum avaṉ vāḻvu cemmiyāy naṭi peṟukiṟatu. iravilum iyantiraṅkaḷil uṇaviyum, uṭiyi yum mali maliyāyc ceytu kuvikka oḷiyē utavukiṟatu.
makkaḷ muṉṉēṟṟattiṟku oḷiyē vaḻikāṭṭum viṭi veḷḷiyākat tikaḻkiṟatu. tamiḻarkaḷ mārikālattil, iruṇṭa kār mēkaṅkaḷ cūḻnta kārttikit tiṅkaḷil viḷakkīṭṭut tirunāḷi ēṟpaṭuttiṉar. aṉṟu eṅkum jekajōtiyāka viḷaṅkak kōṭāṉu kōṭi viḷakkukaḷi ēṟṟi makiḻccip perukkāl koṇṭāṭiya attirunāḷ perum poruḷ uḷḷatāka irukkiṟatu. ataṉi, aṟiñarkaḷ pukaḻvatil āccariyamilli.
vāḻkkiyuṭaṉ oṉṟiyatu viḷakku
nām iṉṟu viḷakkiṉ perumiyic carivara uṇarā viṭiṉum atu namatu vāḻvilum, `tāḻvilum namatu caṭaṅku kaḷilum, viḻākkaḷilum perum paṅku peṟṟu varukiṟatu. nām oḷiyiṉ uyarvic carivara uṇarāviṭiṉum atu, nammi aṟiyāmalē nām ataṉ uyarvi ēṟṟukkoḷḷumpaṭi cey tirukkiṟatu.
viññāṉat tuṟiyilē intiyā iṟuti niliyil niṉṟatu. attipūttāṟpōl intiyāvil oru viññāṉi eḻuntāṉ. avaṉ tamiḻnāṭṭilē piṟantu tamiḻ moḻiyit tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭu, tamiḻppaṇpāṭṭiṉ naṭuvilē talitūkkiya tiru. ci.vi. irāmaṉ. avaṉ ulakilē eṇṇaṟṟa viññāṉat tuṟikaḷ irukka, oḷiyippaṟṟi ārāynta ulakap pukaḻpeṟṟa oppaṟṟa viññāṉa mētiyāy viḷaṅkukiṟāṉ. oḷiyip paṟṟit tamiḻarkaḷ āyiram āyiram āṇṭukaḷāka ārāyntuvanta pāramparyattuvantāṉ car.ci.vi. irāmaṉiṭam oḷi ārāycciyākap pariṇamittirukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉāl atu mikiyākātu eṉṟu eṇṇukiṟēṉ.
viḷakkiṉ viḷakkamāṉa varalāṟṟiṉiṉṟē namatu tattuva cātiraṅkaḷ arumpiyuḷḷaṉa. eṉatu urikaḷi āḻntu cintikkiṟavarkaḷ viḷakkiṉ varalāṟṟiyum apiviruttiyiyum uṇaruvatōṭu ataṉ tattuvattiyum uṇarntu ittuṟiyil cīriya ārāycciyil iṟaṅkuvārkaḷ eṉṟa eṇṇattōṭē innūli eḻuta muṉintēṉ.
makkaḷ tam muṉṉōr varalāṟṟi ārāyntu avarkaḷ peṟṟa veṟṟiyiyum, tōlviyiyum naṉkuṇarntu tam vāḻkkiyil veṟṟi perumāṟu tam vāḻkki muṟikaḷi amittuk koḷvatē muṉṉēṟṟam aṭinta nāṭṭiluḷḷa aṟivuṭiya makkaḷ kaṇṭa aṉupava muṟi. āṉāl tamiḻarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟi iṉṉum cīriya muṟiyil eḻuti muṭikkavilli eṉpati nāṉ naṉkuṇarvēṉ. eṉṟālum, tamiḻarkaḷ tam nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟōṭu taṅkaḷ nāṭṭiṉ moḻi, ici, nāṭakam, nāṭṭiyam aṇi, ālayam, āṭi ilakkiyam, uṟiviṭam, kuṭi, ceruppu, camayam, kācu, viḷakku pōṉṟa vikaḷiṉ varalāṟṟiyum naṉkuṇara vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉatu pēravāvākum.
varalāṟu, vāḻa virumpum makkaḷukku nalla pāṭamākum; varalāṟu kaṣṭa kālaṅkaḷil kikoṭukkum; nām cey vatiṉṉateṉat teriyātu tayaṅki niṟkum poḻutu namakku aṟivuc cuṭarkāṭṭum. maṉitaṉ iyaṟkiyiṉ pōkkiṟkēṟpat taṉṉiyum, vāḻkki muṟiyiyum māṟṟiyamittuk koṇṭatālē vaḷam peṟṟu vāḻkiṟāṉ. avaṉ iyaṟkikku muraṇākat taṉ vāḻkkiyi māṟṟiyamikka muṟpaṭṭa poḻutellām iṭaruṇṭu vīḻntirukkiṟāṉ. ituvē avaṉatu varalāṟṟil kāṇappaṭum uṇmi.
paḻaṅkāla vāḻkki muṟikaḷiṟ cila ikkāla makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟikku ottuvarāta pōtiṉum avaṟṟi nām aṟintiruppatil tavaṟoṉṟum illi. paṇṭikkāla vāḻkki muṟikūṭac cila camayaṅkaḷil namatu uyirukku uṟutuṇiyāka niṟkum eṉpati nām maṟantuviṭak kūṭātu.
eṭuttukkāṭṭāka 1914ām āṇṭil naṭipeṟṟa airōppiya makā yuttattil oru camayam nēcatēcappaṭikaḷ pakivarkaḷiṉ tākkutalkaḷiṉāl citaṟuṇṭu pōyiṉa. orucila pōrvīrarkaḷ taṉimippaṭuttappaṭṭaṉar.
viḷakkiṉāl uyirtappiṉar
avarkaḷ oru kāṭṭil nīṇṭa nēram maṟintirukka vēṇṭiya nirpantam ēṟpaṭṭatu. avarkaḷiṭattil pōtiya uṇavilli. ciṟitaḷavē iruntatu, atuvum uṇpataṟku ukantatāka illi. paciyō avarkaḷi vāṭṭi vatittatu. avarkaḷ paccip pulliyum tiṉṟu paciyāṟat tayārāyiṉar. āṉāl avarkaḷiṭa mirunta coṟpa aḷavuḷḷa kuḷirnta uṇavic cūṭu uṇṭākkiṉāl uṇṇalām. āṉāl ati eppaṭic cūṭu uṇṭākkuvatu eṉpatē periya piraccaṉiyāka iruntatu. oruvarukkum oṉṟum puriyavilli.
avarkaḷil oruvaṉ paḻaṅkāla varalāṟṟi naṉku paṭittavaṉ. eṉavē avaṉ uṭaṉē tammiṭattil irunta takara ṭappāvi viḷakkākac ceytu viṭṭāṉ. ṭappāviṉ mūṭiyiṉmītu tuvāramiṭṭup paḻantuṇiyit tiriyākat tirittu at tuvārattil pukuttiṭappāviṉuḷḷē tammiṭamirunta mīṉ eṇṇeyiviṭṭu oru viḷakki uruvākkiviṭṭāṉ.
avaṉ taṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ ettakiya viḷakkukaḷi upayōkittu vantaṉar eṉpati varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷil paṭit tiruntāṉ. eṉavē avaṉ ippoḻutu viḷakki uruvākki ṉāṉ. ati aṭuppāka ākkit taṉṉiṭamiruntu uṇaviṟkuc cūṭu uṇṭākkiṉāṉ, cuviyuṇṭākkiṉāṉ, ellōr uyiriyum kāppāṟṟiṉāṉ.
intiyap tēciyap pōrāṭṭattil ciṟiceṉṟa tēca paktarkaḷ ciṟiyil pīṭi, curuṭṭukaḷ pukippataṟkut tiṇṭāṭi ṉārkaḷ. āṉāl varalāṟṟi naṉku paṭittavarkaḷ iruntati ṉāl kaṟkaḷik koṇṭum, kampikaḷik koṇṭum cimiṇṭut tariyil urāci neruppup poṟikaḷi uṇṭupaṇṇi atik kantit tuṇikaḷil viḻac ceytu iravil atikārikaḷukkut teriyātu pukittu iṉpantuyttu vantatu. nām kaṇṇāl kaṇṭa uṇmi - ēṉ? aṉupavittup pārtta iṉpamum kūṭa.
ciṟpac celvam
tiruviḷakku, tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ ciṟpac celvamākum. tamiḻarkaḷ ciṟpac ciṟappumikka cīriya viḷakkukaḷi vēṟu enta nāṭṭiṉariṭamiruntum peṟavilli. tamiḻnāṭṭut tiruviḷakku tamiḻaṉiṉ taṉitta paṇpāṭṭiṉiṉṟu taḷirnta taṉipperum kalik karuvūlamākum.
oru cila pāvi viḷakkum, otima viḷakkum urōmar kaḷiṭamiruntu nām peṟṟavi eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. atip pōtiya ātārattuṭaṉ maṟukka muṭiyum. eṉṟālum pāvi viḷakkum ōtima viḷakkum urōm nāṭṭu viḷakkiṉ uruvattip peṟātu tamiḻp paṇpāṭṭit taḻuviyaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟuvatāl avarkaḷōṭu nām atikam vātāṭat tēviyilli.
urōmarkaḷ kaṇṭa pāvi viḷakku ōtima viḷakkup pōṉṟavikaḷukkum tamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭa tīpalaṭcumi viḷakku, aṉṉaviḷakku ākiyavikaḷukkum atikamāṉa vēṟṟumikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kaṭal kaṭanta tamiḻ viḷakku
tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷakkukaḷ arēpiyāvilum, urōm nāṭṭi lum, kirēkka nāṭṭilum ceṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa araṇmaṉiyi yum kōyilkaḷiyum alaṅkarittuḷḷaṉa.
carittarac cuvaṭukaḷ āciyāvilum, airōppāvilumuḷḷa nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷiṉ viḷakkukaḷil patintu nīṇṭakālamāka aḻiyātiruntu vantaṉa eṉpataṟku eṇṇaṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu.
tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷakkukaḷ arāpiyarkaḷiṉ mūlam peyiṉ, ittāli mutaliya nāṭukaḷil paraviyiruntaṉa. varkkattiṉ poruṭṭut tamiḻnāṭṭiṟku vanta arāpiyarkaḷ, tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷakkit taṅkaḷ nāṭṭiṟkuk koṇṭu pōyiṉar. appāl avarkaḷiṉ celvākku airōppāvil vaṇika rītiyilum, araciyal rītiyilum paravavē tamiḻnāṭṭiliruntu kaṇṭa viḷakku nākarīkatti airōppāvilum parappiṉar.
ekiptilum, āppirikkāvilum kūṭat tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷakkup paraviyatu.
iṉṟu aṟivuṭiya makkaḷ oru nāṭṭiṉatu viḷakkiṉ taṉmiyil an nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟu maṭṭumaṉṟi maruttuvam, pūkōḷam, viññāṉam, paṭimam, kaṭṭiṭam, ōviyam, vaṇikam, kittoḻil pōṉṟa kalikaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷum piratipalip patik kāṇkiṉṟaṉar.
iru nūṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ vari, paṇṭik kālatti luḷḷa viḷakkukaḷiṉ amippilum aḻakilum oḷi tarum karuvikaḷiṉ amippilum māṟutal etuvum ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟa piraccaṉiyē eḻavilli. muṟkālattil eṅkum tīvarttikaḷ upayōkappaṭuttappaṭṭu vantaṉa.
viḷakkukaḷil eṇṇey niṟkum akal (kalayam) mikac cātāraṇamāka amikkappaṭṭiruntatu. cila viḷakkukaḷil eṇṇey niṟkum kalayam tiṟantum cila viḷakkukaḷil mūṭappaṭṭum iruntaṉa.
eṇṇey, maravittukaḷiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭatākavō mirukaṅkaḷiṉ koḻuppukaḷiliruntu ceyyappaṭṭatākavō iruntatu.
tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ tāyakam eṉṟu tolporuḷ ārāycciyāḷarkaḷum varalāṟṟu nūṟ pulavarkaḷum kūṟum cumēriya (mecapottāmiyā) nāṭṭiṉiṉṟē mutaṉ mutalil pūmiyiṉiṉṟu eṇṇey eṭukkum muṟi eḻuntatu. ak kālattil pōtiya pōkku varavuc cātaṉaṅkaḷ illā tiruntamiyāl, atikamāka aṉṉiya nāṭukaḷukkup paravavilli. āṉāl virivil kaṭaṟkarip pakutikaḷil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷukkup paraviyatāka aṟikiṟōm. ēṉeṉil akkālattil aṉṉiya nāṭukaḷōṭu kappal pōkkuvaravutāṉ iruntu vantatu. airōppāvil vaṭakku, mēṟkup pakutikaḷil patiṉeṭṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil viṟakiṟkup patilākat timiṅkalam, cīl mīṉ pōṉṟavikaḷiṉ koḻuppukaḷi liruntu ney eṭuttu upayōkikka ārampittuviṭṭaṉar. mattiyatarik kaṭaṟ pakuti kaḷil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷil paṇṭutoṭṭu oliva eṇṇeyi viḷakkerikka upayōkappaṭutti vantaṉar.
palvaki eṇṇey
tamiḻ nāṭṭilē varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉpē pacum pāliliruntu ney eṭuttu viḷakkerittu vantaṉar. iṉṟum kōyilkaḷil neyviḷakku ēṟṟuvatu ciṟanta paṇpāṭāka matikkap paṭṭatu.
piṟakutāṉ, eḷḷilirunatu ney eṭukka ārampittaṉar. eḷḷiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭa neyyi eḷney, - eṇṇey eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṉar. piṉṉar tēykāyiliruntu ney eṭuttaṉar. atit tēṅkāy ney eṉṟu kūṟātu tēṅkāy eṇṇey eṉṟaṉar.
appāl āmaṇakku vittiliruntu ney eṭukka ārampit taṉar. itu viḷakkiṟku ēṟṟatāka iruntatāl itaṉi viḷak keṇṇey eṉṟaṉar. kaṭiciyāyp puṉṉik koṭṭi, vēppaṅkoṭṭi, kuṟukkuviti pōṉṟavikaḷiliruntu ney eṭuttup puṉṉi eṇṇey, vēppeṇṇey, kuṟuk keṇṇey eṉṟu peyar cūṭṭiṉar. enta vittiliruntu ney eṭuttālum tamiḻaṉ mutalil neyyik kaṇṭa vittākiya eḷḷi maṟavātu ellāvitamāṉa vittu neykkum, eḷ eṉṉum mūlap peyari iṇittu eṇṇey eṉṉum peyari upayōkittu vantatu cintikkattakkatu.
putiya viḷakkiṉ tōṟṟam
paṇṭikkāla makkaḷ kaṇṭa neyviḷakkiṉiṉṟu uṇmiyāṉa mutal māṟṟam, āvi viḷakkukaḷ tuḷirtta piṉṉartāṉ ārampamāyiṟṟu. atu miṉ viḷakkukaḷiṉ ārampakālac cōtaṉikaḷukkup piṉṉarum - miṉ viḷakku niṟivāṉa vaḷarcci peṟumvari upayōkattil iruntu vantatu.
ulakameṅkum miṉviḷakkukaḷ tōṉṟiyatum āvi viḷakkukaḷum eṇṇey viḷakkukaḷum kākati aṭintu viṭṭaṉa. āṉāl tamiḻakattil maṭṭum anta viḷakku maṟiyātu oḷir viṭṭōṅki niṟkiṟatu. eṇṇey viḷakkukaḷ camayat tōṭu piṇikkappaṭṭiruppatāl camayaṅkaḷ aḻiyum vari, makkaḷ uḷḷattiṉiṉṟu tamiḻaṉiṉ paṇpāṭu akalumvari namatu nāṭṭil tiruviḷakkukaḷ nilittu niṟkum. iḵtaṉṟit tamiḻaṉ kaṇṭa kuttu viḷakkiṉ ciṟpa vaṭivam iṉṟu eḻunta miṉ viḷakkiṉ taṇṭukaḷilellām iṭampeṟṟu varuvatum cintikkattakkatākum. paṇṭit tamiḻar kaṇṭa tiruviḷakkum ataṉ ciṟpac celvaṅkum aḻiyātu pātukākkappaṭavēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttōṭu innūli eḻuta muṉinta nāṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭil eḻunta miṉ viḷakkukaḷil mēji viḷakkiṉ taṇṭu kaḷil namatu kuttuviḷakkiluḷḷa ciṟpa vaṭivaṅkaḷ poliyak kaṇṭu maṭṭilā makiḻcciyaṭintuḷḷēṉ. paḻam paṇpāṭṭip pātukāttu vaḷarkkum uṇarcci vaḷarumāṉāl ati eṉatu muyaṟciyiṉ muḻu veṟṟiyākak karuti iṟumpū teytuvēṉ.
kalimēti caṇmukanātaṉ
ikkaṭṭurikaḷi eḻutavum nāṉ tamiḻk kalikaḷil paṟṟukkoḷḷavum kāraṇamāy iruntavar īḻanāṭṭic cērnta kālañceṉṟa kalimēti cu. caṇmukanātaṉ avar kaḷāvar. avar iṅkilāntiluḷḷa ākpōrṭu palkalik kaḻakattil kaṭṭiṭak kali payiṉṟavar. mēlum puti poruḷ ārāycci, ōviyam mutaliya tuṟikaḷil naṉku payiṉṟu ilaṅkip putiporuḷ ārāycci utavik kamiṣaṉarāka iruntu 1953ām āṇṭu uyir nīttār.
nāṉ 1943ām āṇṭu ilaṅkikkup poruḷ tēṭum poruṭṭup pōyiruntēṉ. eṉakku araciyal tāṉ teriyum. kaliyip paṟṟik kaṭukaḷavum ārāycci illi; nāṉ koḻumpil oru accakam vittu naṭattip poruḷ tēṭi vantēṉ. ataṉāl ilaṅkiyil uḷḷa eḻuttāḷarkaḷ, pulavarkaḷ, kaliñarkaḷ pērāciriyarkaḷiṉ naṭpu eḷitil kiṭittatu.
nāṉ ilaṅkiyil kaṇṭa pēraṟiñarkaḷil iṅku kuṟip piṭattakkavar tiru.cu. caṇmukanātaṉ avarkaḷāvar. 1948ām āṇṭiliruntu avar uyirtuṟakkum vari 4 āṇṭukaḷ avarōṭu neruṅkip paḻakum vāyppu ēṟpaṭṭatu. avarkaḷ illattil cumār pattāyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa vili uyarnta kali nūṟkaḷ aṭaṅkiya oru periya nūlniliyam iruntatu.
avarkaḷiṉ kali māḷikiyil avarkaḷ tīṭṭiya pala ōviyaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avarkaḷ cēkarittu vitta cippi, kiḷiñcal, caṅku mutaliyaṉa āyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa vakikaḷ iruntaṉa.
300-kku mēṟpaṭṭa tiruviḷakkukaḷum cēkarittu vittirun tārkaḷ. karnāṭaka iciyiyum, parata nāṭṭiyattiyum, nāṭakattiyum naṉku payiṉṟiruntārkaḷ. utayacaṅkar, kōpināt pōṉṟa nāṭṭiyakkārarkaḷ avari kuruvāka matittup pōṟṟi vantaṉar.
avar tīṭṭiya ōviyaṅkaḷ iṅkilāntu, amerikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil naṭipeṟṟa kaṇkāṭcikaḷil mutal iṭam peṟṟaṉa. cila vaṇṇa ōviyaṅkaḷ iṉṟum āṇṭuḷḷa tol poruḷ kāṭci cāliyil miḷirkiṉṟaṉa. avar patiṉāṉku moḻikaḷil pāṇṭittiyam peṟṟiruntār.
tamiḻukkāka pataviyiyum tuṟanta peruntaki
avar ilaṅki nāṭṭiṉ oppaṟṟa talivar, car irāma nātaṉ avarkaḷiṉ uṟaviṉar, ulakap pukaḻ peṟṟa kalāyōki tiru. āṉantakumāracāmiyiṉ kuṭumpattilē avaratu kali vāricākat tiru.cu. caṇmukanātaṉ avarkaḷ tikaḻntārkaḷ.
āṉanta kumāracāmiyiviṭa ivarukkut tamiḻaṟivu atikam. tamiḻk kaliyip paṟṟiya ārāycciyum, ati apivirutti ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟa ārvamum atikam. ataṉāl avar, ilaṅkiyiluḷḷa cila ciṅkaḷak kali veṟiyarkaḷāṉa uyartara aracāṅka atikārikaḷiṉ kōpattiṟku āḷāṉār. eṉṟālum añcātu tam koḷkiyi oru ciṟitum viṭṭukkoṭātu aracāṅkattil tām vakittu vanta uyarnta pataviyik kūṭa utaṟittaḷḷiṉār. tamiḻ aṉṉiyiṉ taliciṟanta tavapputalva rāyt tikaḻnta tiru. caṇmukanātaṉ avarkaḷ 1953ām āṇṭil nalla iḷam pirāyattil iṉṉuyir nīttār. avaratu maṟivu tamiḻk kali ulakiṟku īṭuceyyamuṭiyāta perum naṣṭamākum.
eṉakku 62 vayatu ākiṟatu. itu vari nāṉ cantitta tamiḻk kaliñarkaḷil ivarukku oppāṉa variyō mikkāṉa variyō nāṉ cantittatē illi. ivar taṉṉalamaṟṟut tamiḻk kalit tēvikkut tam uṭal, poruḷ, āvi aṉitti yum arppaṇittuḷḷār. tamiḻk kali vaḷarat tam iṟuti mūccuḷḷa vari arumpāṭupaṭṭār. ivarit tamiḻarkaḷ niṉivil iruttal vēṇṭum.
avar arikuṟiyāy viṭṭuc ceṉṟa kalip paṇiyit toṭarntu naṭatta vēṇṭuvatu iṉṟu vāḻum tamiḻk kaliñar kaḷiṉ taliyāya kaṭaṉākum. ituvē eṉ ilaṭciyamum paṇiyum ākum.
perunalam payanta naṭpu
ik kali mētiyiṉ naṭpu eṉṉip peritum kavarntatu. tamiḻk kaliyiṉ mītu aḷavilāp paṟṟu uṇṭākkiyatu. avar uṟavē eṉṉi varalāṟu, ōviyam, paṭimam, kaṭṭiṭakkali, nāṭṭiyam, puti poruḷ ārāycci mutaliyavikaḷil ārvam koḷḷat tūṇṭiyatu.
paḻaṅkācukaḷ cēkarittal, cippi, caṅku mutaliyavikaḷ cēkarittal, tamiḻ moḻiyiyum kalikaḷiyum paṟṟi āṅkilam, pirañcu, jermaṉ mutaliya moḻikaḷil veḷivanta nūṟkaḷic cēkarittal, viḷakkukaḷ cēkarittal, paḻaṅkāla āṭikaḷ, aṇikaḷ, paṭaṅkaḷ pāttiraṅkaḷ mutaliyavikaḷic cēkarittal pōṉṟa naṟpaṇikku eṉ uḷḷattil vittiṭṭatu. curuṅkak kūṟiṉ kali ārāycciyil īṭupaṭat tūṇṭiyatu.
avarkaḷ iṟanta piṉ avarkaḷiṉ tuṇiviyāriṭa miruntu, avarkaḷiṉ viliyuyarnta 400kku mēṟpaṭṭa kalinūṟ kaḷi vilikku vāṅkiṉēṉ. avvariya nūṟkaḷē innūli eḻutap perun tūṇṭu kōlāka iruntaṉa. kali ārvam eṉ uḷḷattil eḻak kāraṇarāy niṉṟa eṉ uḻuvalaṉpar uyar 
tiru. cu. caṇmukanātaṉ avarkaḷukku innūli maṉam uvantu paṭiyal ceykiṟēṉ.
tiruviḷakku, paḻaṅkācukaḷ, nāṭṭiyam, ici pōṉṟa tamiḻk kalikaḷippaṟṟi nūlkaḷ eḻutumpaṭi palakālum tūṇṭi vantatōṭu eḻutik koṭuttāl avikaḷi uṭaṉē nūlvaṭivil veḷiyiṭat tayār eṉṟu eṉṉi aṭikkaṭi tūṇṭi vanta eṉ.ci.pi.ec. talimi iyakkunarāy iruntu kālañ ceṉṟa eṉatu arumit tōḻar pi.e. cīṉivācarāv avarkaḷi nāṉ eṉṟum maṟappataṟkilli.
tamiḻnāṭṭut tiruviḷakkukaḷ eṉṟa in nūli, ceṉṟa āṇṭu ciṅkappūr tamiḻ muracu eṉṟa taṉ cīriya nāḷitaḻiṉ ñāyiṟu malaril 9-9-62 mutal 23-12-62 vari toṭarntu ērāḷamāṉa paṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ mika aḻakāka veḷiyiṭṭatōṭu eṉatu iṟuti mūccu vari nāṉ eḻutum kalikkaṭṭurikaḷi veḷiyiṭuvatākakkūṟi eṉakku ūkkamum ākkamum aḷitta tōṭu tamiḻnāṭṭut tiruviḷakkukaḷ nūlvaṭivamāka aṉumati yum āciyum aḷitta tamiḻ muracup pattirikiyiṉ urimi yāḷarum āciriyarum eṉatu uḻuvalaṉ parumāṉa tamiḻavēḷ kō. cāraṅkapāṇi avarkaḷukkum, innūl muḻuvatiyum, tamatu palvēṟu vēlikaḷukkiṭiyē paṭittut tirutti iti virivil veḷivara ēṟpāṭu ceyta eṉatu naṇpar pāḷiyaṅ kōṭṭip pērāciriyar tiru.nā. vāṉamāmali em.ē. el.ṭi. avarkaḷukkum eṉ naṉṟi uṟittākuka.
itaṉi nāṉ virumpiya paṭi aḻakākavum, piḻikaḷiṉṟi yum acciṭṭutaviya ceṉṉi niyū ceñcuripuk havu limiṭeṭ niliyattiṟkum eṉatu maṉamārnta naṉṟi urittākuka.
tamiḻakam eṉatu ikkalippaṇikku muḻu ātaraviyum aḷikkum eṉṟum nampukiṟēṉ.
pāḷiyaṅkōṭṭi	
01.01.1964	a. irākavaṉ






tamiḻnāṭṭut tiruviḷakkukaḷ



viḷakkiṉ tōṟṟam
kāṭṭil kaṇṭa kal viḷakku
makkaḷ iṉam maṇṇulakil tōṉṟiyatum mutalil iruḷiyē kaṇṭatu. appāl pakalavaṉiyum viṇmīṉkaḷiyum, matiyiyum kaṇṭatu.
puliyip pōl, pūṉiyip pōl, āntiyip pōl iravil makkaḷ tam kaṇkaḷāl oru poruḷip pārkka muṭiya villi. āṉāl katiravaṉ oḷiyilē makkaḷ kaṇkaḷāl oru poruḷi naṉṟākak kāṇa muṭintatu. kuḷirmatiyilum aḵtillāta kālaṅkaḷilum viṇmīṉkaḷāl makkaḷ ōraḷavutāṉ pārkka muṭintatu.
kār mēkaṅkaḷ katiravaṉiyum kuḷir matiyiyum, viṇmīṉ kaḷiyum maṟittu viṭṭāl makkaḷ iruḷilē kiṭantu tuṉpuṟa vēṇṭiya niliyil iruntaṉar. maṉitaṉ ātikālattilē viḷak kiṉik kāṇavilli; cempu, pittaḷi, veṇkalam, veḷḷi, poṉ mutaliya tātup poruḷkaḷi avaṉ kaṇṭatum illi; kēṭṭatum illi. eḷḷic cekkiliṭṭu neyyi uṇṭākkum aṟivi avaṉ aṇuvaḷavum peṟavilli. ceyaṟki muṟiyil tīyi uṇṭākkum muṟi avaṉukkut teriyavē teriyātu.
ārampakālattil viṇṇil oḷirum miṉṉaliyum iṭiyi yum kaṇṭu naṭunaṭuṅkik kaṇkaḷi mūṭik koṇṭāṉ. maṉita vāḻvil avaṉukku oḷi kāṭṭum maṇiviḷakkāy ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum, viṇmīṉkaḷum viḷaṅkiṉa. maṉitaṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷum uṭalum iravil vāḻvataṟku ēṟṟaṉavāka illi. puli, ciṅkam, karaṭi pōṉṟa taṉ koṭiya virōtikaḷōṭu pōriṭa avaṉukku uṭal valimi illi. iravil tappittu ōṭimaṟintu koḷḷa avaṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷ cakti vāyntavikaḷāka illi.
eṉavē, maṉitaṉ iravil kukikaḷilum putarkaḷilum patuṅkikkiṭakka vēṇṭiyatāyiruntatu. pakalilē katiravaṉ oḷiyāl kaṇkaḷ pārkkum caktiyip peritum peṟṟiruntatāl maṉitaṉ taṉ uṇavit tēṭavum taṉpakivarkaḷāṉa vilaṅkukaḷiṭamiruntu tappittu ōṭavum, kukikaḷilum putarkaḷilum patuṅkik koḷḷa vum muṭintatu.
appāl taṭikaḷikkoṇṭum, kūriya kaṟkaḷik koṇṭum vilaṅkukaḷi etirttup pōriṭṭu aṭittum, veṭṭiyum vāḻat talippaṭṭāṉ.
aṉṟu viyattil vāḻnta makkaḷukku vāṉattuc cuṭarkaḷ ellām iṉiyaṉavāka, eḻiluṭiyaṉavāka iṉpantaruvaṉavākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷ iruḷil tuṉpamum oḷiyil iṉpamum kaṇṭaṉar.
ātiyilē eṅkum iruḷ cūḻntirunta kālattilē maṉitaṉ ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, viṇmīṉ pōṉṟavikaḷi vaḻipaṭa ārampit tāṉ. nāḷaṭivil avaṉ aṟivu vaḷara ārampittatu. nīṇṭa kālattiṟku appāl avaṉ katiravaṉāl payirkaḷ vaḷarvatiyum, uyirkaḷ tōṉṟuvatiyum, malarkaḷ malarvatiyum, maḻi poḻivatiyum, paḻaṅkaḷ paḻuppatiyum kaṇṭu ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, viṇmīṉkaḷ mutaliyavikaḷit taṉ vāḻviṟku uṟutuṇiyāka niṟkum teyvaṅkaḷāka eṇṇi vaḻipaṭa ārampittāṉ.
makkaḷ iṉam, maliyil tōṉṟiyateṉṟu oru cila uyir nūl vallunarkaḷ urikkiṉṟaṉar. oru cilar mali aṭi vāratti luḷḷa tōppilē (ētēṉ tōṭṭattil) tōṉṟiyateṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
maṉita kulattiṉ toṭṭil lemūriyāk kaṇṭam atāvatu kaṭal koṇṭa tamiḻnāṭu eṉṟu hekkal eṉṟa jermaṉ nāṭṭup pūkarpa nūl ārāycci vallunar kūṟiṉar.1 ipiṉ paṭṭūṭṭā eṉṟa ōrarapi vaḻipōkkaṉ (ki.pi. 1325 - 1354il) intiyāviṟku vantāṉ. appāl, maṉita kulattiṉ peṟṟōrkaḷāṉa ātam, havvā tōṉṟi yatu ilaṅkiyiluḷḷa civaṉaṭi pātamali eṉṟu iṉṟum ellā camayikaḷum puṉita maliyākap pōṟṟum ammalikaḷiṉ arukil pōy taṉatu mariyātiyic celuttiyatōṭu taṉatu kiyāl, puṉiyā ōviyamāka oru paṭamum tīṭṭiyuḷḷāṉ. avaṉ taṉatu nāṭkuṟippil tīṭṭiya paṭamum kuṟippum iṉṟu nūl vaṭivamāka vantuḷḷatu. atil ātam maliyum havvā maliyum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.2 ātamum havvāvum ilaṅkiyiṉiṉṟu intiyāviṟkup pālam kaṭṭi vantaṉar eṉṟum, avarkaḷ vanta vaḻiyi, ātam pālam (Adam’s Bridge) eṉṟu varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ kūṟṟu meyyāyiṉ civaṉaṭi pātamaliyē maṉita kulattiṉ toṭṭilākum eṉpatil aiyamilli.
iḵtaṉṟi makēntira maliyilum, kili maliyilum, cīṉāy maliyilum kaṭavuḷ kāṭci aḷittiruppatākak kūṟum camayak kūṟṟum, paṇṭit tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kuṟiñci nila vāḻvum, mulli nila vāḻvum mutaṉ mutalāka makkaḷ malikaḷilum, mali aṭivārattilum vāḻnta vāḻvi veḷippaṭuttuvataṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟākum.
malikaḷ mītum mali aṭivārattilum vāḻnta paṇṭiya makkaḷ, aṭarnta kāṭukaḷil peruṅkāṟṟu vīciyapōtu āṇṭuḷḷa mūṅkil pōṉṟa maraṅkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu urāyntu neruppu uṇṭāki marañceṭi koṭikaḷ erintu cāmpalāyp pōvatik kaṇṭaṉar. ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, viṇmīṉkaḷ ākiyavikaḷukku aṭutta paṭiyāka makkaḷ iṉam kaṇṭa oḷi intak kāṭṭuttītāṉ.
maṉitaṉ nākarikattiṉ mutaṟpaṭiyiṟ kāṭṭuttīyik kaṇṭa kāṭcitāṉ, maṉitaṉ nākarikattiṉ mutaṟpaṭiyil kāl vikka ārampitta kāṭciyākum. ataṟkup piṉṉartāṉ kūriya kaṟkaḷit taṉ pakivarkaḷākiya vilaṅkukaḷiṉ mītu pirayōkittu avikaḷi veṭṭikkoṉṟu veṟṟi māli cūṭa ārampittāṉ. kūriya kaṟ kaḷik koṇṭē marañceṭi koṭikaḷi veṭṭa ārampittāṉ. kāykaḷik kallāyutattālē paṟikka ārampittāṉ.
appāl kallāyutatti vilaṅkukaḷ ōṭum poḻutu vīca ārampittāṉ. akkallāyutam pūmiyil uḷḷa kalliṉ mēṟpaṭṭut tīppoṟikaḷ arumpuvati aṟintāṉ. cila camayam tāṉ vīciya kal vēṟoru kalliṉ mītu vīḻntu tīppoṟi eḻuntu arukiluḷḷa kāynta carukukaḷ mītu viḻuntu neruppu uṇṭāvati uṇarntāṉ.
piṉṉar, ceyaṟki muṟiyil tīyi uṇṭākkat talip paṭṭāṉ. cakki mukkik kaṟkaḷi oṉṟōṭoṉṟu uracum paṭi ceytu arukiluḷḷa ularnta carukukaḷil tīppoṟi vīḻntu paṟṟum paṭi ceytāṉ. ularnta iliyil paṟṟiya neruppu virivil maṟintu pōvatiyum marakkaṭṭiyil paṟṟiya tī aṇiyātu nīṇṭa nēram iruppatiyum tīppoṟi, carukukaḷ, pañcu mutaliya meṉmiyāṉa poruḷkaḷil eḷitil paṟṟik koḷvatiyum kaṇṭāṉ. kāṭṭuttīyil akappaṭṭa kiḻaṅkukaḷ cuviyāy iruppatit terintāṉ.
iti varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ makkaḷiṉ kaṟkālam eṉṟu moḻikiṉṟaṉar. makkaḷiṉ kaṟkālam arumpiya kālattilēyē makkaḷiṉ mutalviḷakkum tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu kūṟalām.
maṉitaṉ ceyaṟki muṟiyil tīyi uṇṭākka ārampit tālum viḷakki uruvākkum eṇṇam eḻa nīṇṭa kālam ceṉṟatu. miṉmiṉip pūcciyik kaṇṭatum, maṉitaṉukku viḷakki uruvākkum āci uṇṭāyiṟṟu. mutaṉ mutalākap paṉiyōli kaḷiyum marakkaṭṭikaḷiyum viḷakkāka upayōkittāṉ. appāl eṇṇey, koḻuppu mutaliyavikaḷik kaṇṭāṉ. taṉatu aṟivu mutiravē kaṟkuḻiyilum, caṅkilum, kiḷiñcalilum neyyi ūṟṟic ceṭikaḷiliruntu uṇṭākum nārkaḷit tiriyāka upayōkappaṭutta muṉintāṉ.
nāḷaṭivil aṟivu mutircciyaṭintatum maṇṇic cuṭṭu viḷakku ceyyum muṟiyik kaṇṭāṉ. maṇ viḷakkil neyyi vārttu viḷakku eriyumpaṭic ceytāṉ. piṉ eḷḷiliruntu ney eṭuttu eṇṇey eṉṟu peyariṭṭu viḷakku eriyac ceytāṉ.
ciṟitu nāḷil āmaṇakkuk koṭṭiyiliruntu ney eṭuttu viḷakkil ūṟṟi eriyac ceytāṉ. nīṇṭakālamāka immuṟi payaṉpaṭṭu vantatāl āmaṇakku ney, viḷakkeṇṇey eṉṟa peyarum peṟṟatu.
piṉṉar makkaḷ iluppi, kaṭukuviti, puṉṉik koṭṭi, vēppaṅkoṭṭi, tēṅkāy, cūriya kānti viti mutaliyavi kaḷiliruntu ney eṭuttu viḷakku ēṟṟum muṟiyik kaṇṭaṉar.
cila nāṭukaḷil timiṅkalattiṉ koḻuppiliruntu ney eṭuttu viḷakku erittu vantaṉar. tamiḻarkaḷit tavira āriyar, cīriyar, acīriyar, cumēriyar, ekiptiyar, cīṉar, kirēkkar, urōmar pōṉṟa makkaḷum eṇṇey viḷakkukaḷip payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar. iṉṟu annāṭukaḷil akaḻnteṭukkappaṭṭa paḻam poruḷkaḷuḷ pala viḷakkukaḷum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cumār āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ceyyappaṭṭa cuṭumaṇ viḷakkoṉṟu eṉṉiṭamuḷḷatu.
āticca nallūrilum arikkaṉmēṭṭilum uḷḷa paḻiya viḷakkukaḷ
iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ceyyappaṭṭa pala viḷakkukaḷ āticcanallūrilum arikkaṉmēṭṭilum kaṇṭeṭuk kappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cumār aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉuḷḷa viḷakkukaḷ mokañcatārōvilum, pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ ceyyappaṭṭa kaḷimaṇ viḷakkukaḷ acīriyā, cālṭiyā, cumēriyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum iṉṟu akaḻnteṭukkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.
maṉita camūkattiṉ vayatu vaḷarntuḷḷatu. iyaṟkiyiṉ iṉpa viḷiyāṭṭukaḷi yellām naṉkuṇara maṉita camūkam āram pittatu. nāḷaṭivil camūkattiṉ tēvikaḷ atikappaṭṭatu. iyaṟkiyik kaṇṭu payantu kiṭanta maṉitaṉ nāḷaṭivil, taṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta tēvikaḷip pūrtti ceyya, atit taṉ vayap paṭutta muṉintāṉ. atil avaṉ paṭippaṭiyāy veṟṟiyum īṭṭik koṇṭāṉ.
neruppu iravilē oḷiyi aḷittu, etirēyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ avaṉ kaṇṇukkut teriyumāṟu ceytatu. kāy, kaṉi, kiḻaṅku mutaliyavaṟṟip pakkuvappaṭuttit tiṉṉa atu peritum utaviyatu. karaṭi, puli, pāmpu mutaliya pakivarkaḷiṉiṉṟu taṉṉip pātu kāttuk koḷḷat tīyi oru karuviyāka maṉitaṉ upayōkittut taṉ vāḻvil perum veṟṟiyip peṟṟāṉ.
paṉikkālattilum, kūtir kālattilum kuḷir pōkki uṭalukku veppamūṭṭa maṉitaṉ tīyip payaṉpaṭutta naṉku terintu koṇṭāṉ. maṉita camūkam virintu vaḷarat talippaṭṭatu. eṇṇikkiyil maṭṭumalla, aṟivilum āṟṟalilum āṇmi yilum itara pirāṇikaḷiviṭac ciṟantu viḷaṅkiyatu. maṉita camūkam iyaṟkiyi aṭakki āḷa muṟpaṭṭatu. nilam, nīr, neruppu. kāṟṟu, vāṉ mutaliya aimpūtaṅkaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ taṉmikaḷiyum uṇara muṟpaṭṭatu. maṉita vāḻkki vaḷam peṟṟut tuḷirkkat toṭaṅ kiyatu. naṉiciṟanta nākarika vāḻkkiyi natta maṉitaṉ muṟpaṭṭāṉ.
nāṭōṭikaḷāy āṭumāṭukaḷi mēyttuk koṇṭu kāṭu mēṭukaḷumāyt tirintalinta maṉitarkaḷ ceḻippāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil kūṭṭam kūṭṭamāy vāḻattuṇintaṉar. itara mirukaṅkaḷippōl kukikaḷil vāḻnta maṉitaṉ paṟavikaḷippōl maraṅkaḷil kūṭukaḷ kaṭṭa muṉintu nāḷaṭivil kuṉṟiṉ ucciyilum, mēṭu kaḷilum, iṟutiyākac camapūmiyilum vīṭukaḷip kaṭṭat tuvaṅ kiṉāṉ.
vīṭukaḷ, avaṉuṭiya melliya uṭalit taṭpaveṭpa nilikaḷiṉiṉṟu maṭṭumiṉṟi, avaṉuṭiya koṭiya pakivar kaḷākiya vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum kāppataṟku ariya araṇkaḷāy viḷaṅkiṉa. eṇṇaṟṟa eṇṇey viḷakkukaḷum uṟutuṇiyāy eḻa ārampittaṉa. kalviḷakkukaḷ, kiḷiñcal viḷakkukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
maṉita vāḻkkiyiṉ varalāṟṟil pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷ paṟantōṭiṉa. veṟuṅ kaṭṭikaḷi viḷakkāka upayōkikka ārampitta maṉitaṉ caṅkilum karuṅkallilum, mākkallilum, cuṭumaṇṇilum viḷakki uruvākkiviṭṭāṉ.
tamiḻ moḻiyil kāṇappaṭum nūlkaḷil mikappaḻami yāṉatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatu tolkāppiyamēyākum. apperum kāppiyam tōṉṟiya kālam ki.mu. 1000kkum ki.mu. 600kkum iṭip paṭṭa kālam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. akkālattil tamiḻnāṭu nāṉilamākap pirintiruntatu. nāṉilattil vāḻnta makkaḷ nāṉila makkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭaṉar. nāṉilam eṉpatu kuṟiñci, mulli, neytal, marutam eṉpaṉavākum. maliyum mali cārnta iṭamum kuṟiñci eṉṟum, kāṭum kāṭu cārnta iṭamum mulli eṉṟum, kaṭalum kaṭal cārnta iṭamum neytal eṉṟum, vayalum vayal cārnta iṭamum marutam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.
	`mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal eṉac
	colliya muṟiyāṟ collavum paṭumē
eṉṟa tolkāppiyac cūttiram itaṉi uṟutippaṭuttukiṟatu.
inta nāṉila makkaḷil kuṟiñci nila makkaḷē mutaṟkuṭi eṉṟu moḻivar mūtaṟiñar. ātiyil makkaḷ maliyilē tōṉṟiṉar eṉṟu palavaralāṟṟaṟiñarkaḷ ēṟṟukkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar. uyirnūl vallunarkaḷum ati oppukiṉṟaṉar.
ātiyil araṉār makēntira maliyil kallāla marattiṉ kīḻ nālvarkaḷukku nallaṟam naviṉṟār eṉṟu civarkaḷ ēṟṟukkoḷ kiṉṟaṉar. cumārttarkaḷum iṟivaṉ imayamali mītu umiyāḷuṭaṉ irukkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. kiṟitavarkaḷ ātāmic cuvarkka lōkattiliruntu maliyilē (ilaṅkiyilēyuḷḷa civaṉaṭi pātamaliyilē) iṟakkiṉāṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itip pala mulimkaḷum ēṟṟukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar. iṟivaṉ cīṉā (cīṉ) malimītu akkiṉi vaṭivamāka mōcēkkuk kāṭci aḷittatāka yūtarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. mukammatu napi (sal) avarkaḷukku allā(h) jiprayal eṉṉum taṉ tūtar mūlamāka hirāmaliyilē tirukkur āṉiṉ mutal vārttiyic colli vittatāka ilā miyarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ itaṉāl maliyiṉ mukkiyattuvam uṟutip paṭukiṟatu.
itaṉi ātarittup pi.ṭi. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār avarkaḷ `tamiḻar varalāṟu eṉṟa āṅkila nūlil piṉvarumāṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.
`ivvulakil tōṉṟiya ātimaṉitaṉ mutalil maliyilum maliyi aṭutta veḷiyilum vāḻntirukka vēṇṭum; piṟaku koñcam koñcamākac camanilattit tēṭi mulli, neytal mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷic cērntu kaṭiciyilē maruta nilatti aṭintirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭiruppatu namatu karutti ātarippatāka irukkiṉṟatu.1
kuṟiñci nilattilē makkaḷ tōṉṟip pallāṇṭu vāḻntu appāl oru cilar mulli nilattil vantu kuṭiyēṟi irukka vēṇṭum. piṉṉar oru cila makkaḷ neytal nilattil pōyk kuṭiyēṟi irukka vēṇṭum; oru cila makkaḷ maruta nilattil kuṭiyēṟi irukka vēṇṭum.
ātiyil kuṟiñci nilattilē aṉṉiyiṉ āṭci arumpi yirukka vēṇṭum. avaḷē kuṭumpat taliviyākavum, illā ḷākavum teyvamākavum pōṟṟappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum. avaḷ iṭṭatē caṭṭamāka iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum eṇṇu kiṟēṉ. avaḷ koṟṟavi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟum nampukiṟēṉ. piṟkālattilētāṉ kuṟiñcikkut teyvam murukaṉ eṉṟum mullikkut teyvam māyōṉ eṉṟum marutattiṟkut teyvam vēntaṉ eṉṟum neytalukkut teyvam vāyuveṉṟum ēṟpaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum.
ēṉeṉil ulakilē tāy āṭci māṟit tantiyiṉ āṭci nilitta piṉṉartāṉ murukaṉ tōṉṟiyirukkavēṇṭum. murukaṉik koṟṟavi mintaṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvatē eṉatu karutti araṇ ceyvatākum. atē pōṉṟu mulli nilat teyvamākiya māyōḷ nāḷaṭivil āṇ āṭci taḷirtta kālattil māyōṉāki viṭṭatāka aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. marutanilat teyvamākiya kāḷi civaṉākiviṭṭāṉ. `cattiyāyc civamatāki eṉṉum tēvārap pāṭṭu maruta nilattilum tāy āṭci  nilaviyiruntati meyppippa tāka irukkiṟatu. atōṭu koṟṟavi putitāka arumpiya pāli nilattiṟku viraṭṭappaṭṭāḷ. avaḷatu mintaṉ kuṟiñcit teyva mākavum avaḷatu aṇṇaṉ mullit teyvamākavum avaḷatu kaṇavaṉ marutat teyvamākavum avaḷatu ēvalāḷ neytal nilat teyvamākavum piṟkālattil ākkappaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇukiṟēṉ. eṉatu karuttiṟku eṇṇaṟṟa ātāraṅkaḷ uṇṭu. avikaḷip paṟṟi iṅku vivarikka icciṟunūl iṭantarātu eṉṟu itaṉuṭaṉ niṟuttik koḷkiṟēṉ.
paṇṭit tamiḻar kaṇṭa nāṉilattilum nālu teyvaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. atōṭu kuṟavar, āyar, uḻavar, paravar eṉṟa nāṉku peyar koṇṭa makkaḷ vāḻntu vantaṉar. ivarkaḷ nālvarum tamiḻ iṉattavarkaḷēyāvar. ivarkaḷiṭattil evvita kula vēṟṟumiyum kuṭi vēṟṟumiyum nila vēṟṟumiyum illi. tīṇṭāmip pēy ivarkaḷi immi aḷavum aṇukavilli.
oru nilattil vāḻum āṇmakaṉ maṟṟoru nilattil vāḻum peṇ makaḷi maṇantu koḷvatil evvitac caṭṭamum paḻakka vaḻakkamum kuṟukkiṭuvatilli. ivarkaḷ vayiṟṟil piṟakkum makkaḷ caṇṭāḷar eṉṟō aṉumalōmar eṉṟō iḻivu paṭuttappaṭuva tilli. avarkaḷ, tāṉ vāḻum nilattiṟkuriya taliciṟanta makkaḷāy vāḻntuvara urimi peṟṟu iruntaṉar.
nāṉila makkaḷum avarkaḷ vāḻum nilattiṉ taṭpa veṭpat tiṟkēṟpa nāḷaṭivil vaḷarcci peṟa ārampittaṉar. av vaḷarcci taṉittaṉiyē tuḷirkka ārampittatu. av vaḷarcci poruḷā tāratti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatāka malarntatu. itaṟkut tolkāppiyamum piṟa caṅka nūṟkaḷum namakkuc cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa. kuṟiñci nilattilum mulli nilattilum cila pakutikaḷ ñāyiṟṟiṉ veppattāl maṇalveḷiyāka māṟiṉa. itu pāli nilam eṉap paṭṭatu. innilam piṟkālattil eḻuntatu.
kuṟiñci nilam : innilattil kalvi, kēḷvi, vīram mutaliya vaṟṟāl oppuyarvaṟṟavaṉāy viḷaṅkum āṇ makaṉ kāṉakanāṭaṉ, maliyaṉ, veṟpaṉ poruppaṉ, cilampaṉ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭu vāṉ. ivaṉē innila makkaḷiṉ talivaṉ. maṟṟiya āṇmakkaḷ kāṉavar eṉappaṭuvar. avvāṟē aṅkuḷḷa kalvi kēḷvikaḷil ciṟanta peṇkoṭiyik koṭicci eṉṟu kūṟuvar ivaḷ innilattiṉ talivaṉuṭiya illak kiḻatti; ēṉiya peṇkaḷ kuṟattiyar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvar.
ivarkaḷiṉ uṇavup poruḷ, nel, tiṉi, mūṅkil arici, kāykkaṉi  kiḻaṅkukaḷ mutaliyaṉa.
ivarkaḷ nilattiṟkuriya vilaṅkukaḷ puli, paṉṟi, yāṉi, karaṭi mutaliyavi.
puḷḷiṉam : kiḷi, mayil pōṉṟavikaḷ.
maram : akil, āram, timicu, tēkku, vēṅki mutaliyavi.
ivarkaḷ marakkompukaḷil irukkum tēṉkaḷi eṭuttu aruntiṉar. maraṅkaḷil paḻuttu utirntu kiṭakkum paḻaṅkaḷi eṭuttuccāppiṭṭaṉar. appāl paḻaṅkaḷip paṟittum kallāl eṟintum tiṉṟaṉar. nāḷaṭivil kallāl pūmiyikkiṇṭi kiḻaṅku kaḷip payiriṭṭum tiṉikaḷi vitittum payir ceyya muṟ paṭṭaṉar.
innilattiṟkuriya malar : kuṟiñciyum, kāntaḷum, vēṅkiyum cuṉikkuvaḷiyum ākum.
ivarkaḷatu ūr : ciṟukuṭiyum, kuṟicciyum, kuṟiñciyum, kuṟum poriyum, ciṟṟūrum ākum.
ivarkaḷatu teyvam, koṟṟaviyiṉ mintaṉāṉa murukaṉ āvāṉ. murukaṉ vākaṉam mayil. innilattiṟkuriya paṟi: veṟi yāṭṭuppaṟi, toṇṭakappaṟi, kuravippaṟi, muṟukiyam, tuṭi eṉap pala vakippaṭum. vākaṉam mutal paṟikaḷ vari - aṉittum avarkaḷiṉ āṭṭattiṟkuriyaṉa eṉpati nām naṉkaṟiyak kiṭak kiṉṟaṉa.
ivarkaḷatu yāḻ kuṟiñciyāḻ allatu yāmayāḻ eṉappaṭum. ivarkaḷatu paṇ : kuṟiñcippaṇ eṉappaṭum. taṟkālam vaḻaṅkum kuṟiñci eṉṉum irākam kōṭippāliyiṉ (caṅkarāparaṇattiṉ) curaṅkaḷiyuṭiyatu. ituvē tāyppaṉ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. kuṟiñci nila vāḻkkiyiṉ toṉmikku ituvē cāṉṟākum.
kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ kaṇṭa kal viḷakku
kuṟattiyar kuṟi collum nāṭṭup pāṭalkaḷum ik kuṟiñciyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭaviyākum. innila makkaḷ ēṉiya nilattiṉiṉṟu, taṅkaḷukkeṉṟu vēṟupaṭṭa taṉitta teyva mum, paṟiyum, paṇṇum yāḻum kaṇṭatip pōla, karuṅkal viḷakkiyum kaṇṭaṉar. itaṉik kūṟuvataṟkē ittuṇi viḷakkamum eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ivarkaḷ mutaṉmutalil viḷakkik kāṇum poḻutu camūkattil kal taccarkaḷ tōṉṟavilli. kal uḷiyi ivarkaḷ kaṉavilum aṟiyārkaḷ. ārampa kālattil ivarkaḷ kalviḷakkic ceyyavilli. iyaṟkiyē ivarkaḷukku viḷakkic ceytu koṭuttatu. mūṅkil maraṅkaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu uraci tīppaṟṟu vatik kaṇṭa maṉitaṉ nāḷaṭivil iru kampukaḷi palamāka uraci, tīppiṭikkac ceytāṉ. atōṭu kāṭṭuttīyiṉ ciṟu kuccik kāṭṭit tīppaṟṟiyatum viḷakkākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉāṉ.
appāl nīṇṭa nāṭkaḷukkuppiṉ iyaṟki aṉṉi makka ḷukkuk kalviḷakki uruvākkik koṭuttāḷ ati avaṉukkuk kāṇpittum koṭuttāḷ. pallāṇṭu pallāṇṭāka kāṉyāṟṟil uruṇṭu uruṇṭu vanta kaṟkaḷil cila, naṭuvil kuḻi viḻuntiruntaṉa. antak kaṟkaḷittāṉ kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ viḷakkākap payaṉ paṭutti vantaṉar.
paṇṭiyak kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭil vacitta kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ kaṇṭa intak karuṅkal viḷakkukaḷiṉ acal uruvam namakkuk kiṭikkavilli. namatu aracāṅkap putiporuḷ ārāyccit tuṟiyiṉar paṇṭu kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ vāḻnta malikaḷilum kuki kaḷilum tam ārāycci vēliyi ārampikkavilli. āṉāl vēṟu pala nāṭṭil malikaḷil vāḻntuḷḷa makkaḷ kaṇṭa kal viḷakkukaḷ kaṇṭekkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. attakiya viḷakkukaḷil oṉṟu īṇṭu veḷiyiṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. (paṭam 1 pārkka)
mulli nilam : innilattil vāḻnta ciṟanta talimakaṉ kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ, tōṉṟal, kāṉakanāṭaṉ aṇṇal eṉap paṭuvaṉ. ivaṉ innila makkaḷiṉ talivaṉ. ēṉiya āṇmakkaḷ iṭiyar, āyar eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭuvar.
innilattil vāḻnta ciṟanta talivi, maṉivi kiḻatti eṉappaṭuvāḷ. ivaḷ innilat talivaṉ maṉivi. ēṉiya peṇkaḷ iṭicciyar, āyar makaḷ, āycciyar, kōpikaḷ, kuṭattiyar, toṟuviyar eṉappaṭuvar.
innilattiṟkuriya vilaṅku : muyalum pulvāyum (ciṟumāṉum) āṭum māṭum ākum.
puḷ : kāṉaṅkōḻiyum mayilum civalum ākum.
maram : koṉṟiyum kuruntum ākum.
pū : mulliyum tōṉṟiyum piṭavum taḷavum ākum.
toḻil : āṭumāṭu mēyttal, avikaḷi pātukāttal, pāl karattal, pāl kaṭital, tayir, mōr, ney ceytal, varaku, avari, tuvari payiriṭal - akkālam uḻavu ceyyat teriyāta kālam. kallē kalappiyākap payaṉpaṭṭatu.
uṇavu : varaku, cāmi, mutiriyumām.
teyvam : māyōḷ, piṉṉar māyōṉāka māṟiviṭṭāḷ.
paṟi : ēṟukōṭpaṟi, muracu, pampi ākum.
yāḻ : mulliyāḻ (cevvaḻiyāḻ) ākum.
paṇ cevvaḻippaṇ - itaṉi mullit tīmpāṇi, iṟaṅkaṟpaṇ cātāri eṉṟellām kūṟuvar.
	`aruḷē yātalō koṭitēyiruḷvarac
	cīṟiyāḻ celvaḻi paṇṇi yāḻ tiṉ
	kāretir kāṉam pāṭiṉē māka 	(puṟam 144)
eṉṟa puṟanāṉūṟṟup pāṭal mulli nila makkaḷiṉ paṇṇiyum yāḻiyum naṉkeṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟatu. ivarkaḷatu ūr pāṭi, cēri, paḷḷi, āviram, iṭiccēri, uvaḷakam, āyppāṭi, valli, valliyam eṉappaṭum.
mulliyil ceyta kalviḷakku
kuṟiñcinila makkaḷ kaṇṭa viḷakkiṉum mullinila makkaḷ kaṇṭa viḷakku ciṟitu muṉṉēṟṟam uṭiyatāy irukkiṟatu. ivar kaḷatu viḷakku iyaṟki aṉṉi aḷitta viḷakku alla. ceyaṟki viḷakkākum. meṉmiyāṉa karuṅkallut tuṇṭi eṭuttu oru ciṟiya valuvāṉa karuṅkallik koṇṭu taṭṭittaṭṭi veḷḷi mākkalliṉ naṭuvil oru ciṟiya paḷḷam uṇṭākki appaḷḷat tilē ney vārttu viḷakkerittaṉar. mulli nila makkaḷ uruvākkiya kalviḷakkiṉ uruvam oṉṟum itaṉuṭaṉ iṇikkap paṭṭu irukkiṟatu. vācakarkaḷ kaṇṭu koḷka. (paṭam 2 pārkka)
neytal nilam : innila makkaḷ kalvi, kēḷvimikka ciṟanta āṇ makaṉic cērppaṉ, pulampaṉ, tuṟivaṉ koṇkaṉ eṉpar. ivaṉ innila makkaḷiṉ talivaṉāvāṉ. ēṉiya āṇmakkaḷ paravar, aḷavar, nuḷiyar, kaṭalar, kaḻiyar, calavar, timilar, valiyar eṉappaṭuvar.
neytal nilattiṟ kalvi, kēḷviyiṟ ciṟanta peṇ makaḷ paratti, nuḷicci eṉappaṭuvāḷ. ivaḷ innilattiṉ talivi. ēṉiya peṇkaḷ parattiyar, aḷattiyar, nuḷicciyar eṉappaṭu vārkaḷ.
puḷ : aṉṉam, aṉṟil, akaṉṟil pōlvaṉa.
toḻil : mīṉ viṟṟal, uppuc ceytal, uppu viṟṟal.
uṇavu : mīṉ viliyum uppu viliyumākum.
pū : veḷḷitaḻk kitiyum neytalumām. 
ūr : kalamēṟu paṭṭiṉam, ciṟukuṭi, pākkam.
maram : puṉṉi, ñāḻal, kaṇṭalākum. 
teyvam : varuṇaṉākum.
	varuṇaṉ mēya perumaṇal ulakamum
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟuvatu ivarkaḷatu teyvam varuṇaṉ eṉpati uṟutippaṭuttukiṟatu.
paṟi : mīṉ kōṭpaṟi, nāvāyppaṟi, cāppaṟiyākum. 
yāḻ : neytalyāḻ (viḷari yāḻ)  paṇ : viḷarippaṇ ākum.
neytal nilattil kaṇṭa caṅku viḷakku
ivar tam  nilattil  kalviḷakkik kaṇṭārkaḷilli. caṅkukaḷil neyviṭṭu erikkum caṅku viḷakkē neytal nila makkaḷ kaṇṭa viḷakkākum. ciṟu cippikaḷilum neyviṭṭu erikkum cippi viḷakku ivarkaḷ illattil iruntatu. mīṉ koḻuppi urukki neyyāka ivarkaḷ upayōkittirukkalām. ittakiya caṅku viḷakkukaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭu mīṉavarkaḷ maṭṭum kaṇṭārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkilli. cumār 10,000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar, tirāviṭar kaḷiṉ tāyakam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum cumēriyāvilum (papilōṉ allatu mecapottāmiyāvilum) caṅku viḷakkukaḷ upayōkap paṭuttappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa neytal nilatti luḷḷa caṅkuviḷakkum cumēriya nāṭṭil kaṇṭa paḻamperum caṅku viḷakkum oruppōla irukkiṉṟaṉa. (paṭam 3 pārkka)
maruta nilattil tōṉṟiya maṇ viḷakku
maruta nilam : innila makkaḷ taṅkaḷ nilattiluḷḷa kalvi, kēḷvi, aḻaku, āṇmi mutaliyavaṟṟil ciṟantu viḷaṅkum āṇma kaṉi ūraṉ, makiḻnaṉ eṉa aḻippar. ivaṉ innila makkaḷiṉ talivaṉ. ēṉiya āṇkaḷ uḻavar, kaṭivar eṉappaṭuvar. innilattil kalvi, kēḷvi, aḻaku, peṇmi mutaliyavi niṟinta peṇ makaḷik kiḻatti, maṉivi eṉpar. ivaḷ innilat taliva ṉiṉ illak kiḻatti. ēṉiya peṇkaḷ uḻattiyar, kaṭicciyar eṉappaṭuvar.
innilattiṟkuriya maram : marutam, vañci, kāñci mutaliyaṉa vākum. vilaṅkukaḷ : erumi, nīrnāy mutaliyaṉa. puḷ : nīrkōḻi, tārā.
pū : tāmari malar, ceṅkaḻunīrppū. uṇavu : cennel, veṇṇel.
ūr : pērūr, akaram, uṟiyuḷ, kirāmam, kuṭam, kōṭṭam, caraṇam, taṇṇaṭi, tāvaḷam, nakaram, nakar, nikētaṉam, niyamam, nilayam, paṭappi, paḷḷi, pāḻi, puram, puri mutaliyaṉavākum. ivikaḷ iṭattiṟkum toḻilukkum ēṟpa peritum ciṟitumāṉa palavita ūrkaḷākum.
nīr : maṉik kiṇaṟum poykiyum yāṟum.
toḻil : naṭutal, kaḷikaṭṭal, nellarital, avi kaṭā viṭutal, payirkkuk kaḷi kaṭṭal.
maruta nilam tōṉṟiya kālattil makkaḷ nākarikam tuḷirkka ārampittatu. uḻutal toḻil ūkkamum ākkamum peṟṟaṉa. innila makkaḷ uṟpattiyip perukkat tīviramāka muṉvantaṉar. innilattiṉ talimip patavi uyarttappaṭṭatu. uṟpattiyip perukka ellā nilattiṉiṉṟum makkaḷ tiraṭṭappaṭṭaṉar. teyva niliyilum māṟutalkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. ellā nilattiṉ talivaṉilum maruta nilattut talivaṉ uyarntāṉ. piṟanilat teyvaṅkaḷ ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷ ākkappaṭṭu ellāt teyvaṅkaḷukku mēlāka maruta nilat teyvamāṉa civaṉ uyarttappaṭṭāṉ. murukaṉ civaṉuṭiya makaṉāṉāṉ. tirumāl mittuṉaṉākavum, kāḷi maṉiviyākavum, vēntaṉ (intiraṉ) tēvarkaḷukku aracaṉākavum, varuṇaṉ paṇi āḷākavum, civaṉ maṉṉarmaṉṉaṉāka piṟavāyākkip peri yōṉāka muḷittāṉ.
paṟi : maṇa muḻavu, nellarikkiṇi. yāḻ: maruta yāḻ. paṇ: marutap paṇ. ippaṇ paṭumalippāliyāki iṉṟu kalyāṇi yākap peyar māṟiyirukkiṟatu.
	vēntaṉ mēya tīmpuṉal ulakamum
eṉṟa tolkāppiya cūttiram innilattiṉ teyvatti naṉku eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟatu.
nāṉilattilum innilamē mēṉilamāy makkaḷ vāḻkkikku ēṟṟa nilamāyt tikaḻntatu. innilattil pērāṟukaḷ pāyntaṉa. nañceyum, puñceyum innilattil tāṉ mikuntaṉa. ellā nilattavarkaḷukkum vēṇṭiya uṇavi innilamē koṭuttatu. eṉavē innilattilētāṉ nākarika vaḷarccikku vāyppu ēṟpaṭṭatu.
innila makkaḷtāṉ, kalappi eṉṉum iyantiratti mutaṉ mutalil kaṇṭaṉar. āḷuṭimi eṉṉum attiyāyam iṅkutāṉ ārampamāyiṟṟu. āṇṭāṉ aṭimi eṉṉum varkka muraṇpāṭu iṅku tāṉ tuḷirttatu. māṭukaḷum erumiyum uḻuvataṟku iṅkutāṉ payiṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. mutaṉ mutalāka iṅku tāṉ ariyil āṭi aṇiyum vaḻakkam ēṟpaṭṭatu.
nīṇṭa kālamāka āṭi aṇiyātu vilaṅkukaḷōṭu vilaṅkukaḷāy vāḻnta maṉitaṉiṉ uṭampil mayirkaḷ utirntu taṭpaveṭpa nilikaḷit tāṅka muṭiyātu. ilikaḷiyum, marappaṭṭikaḷiyum, tōlkaḷiyum pōrtta ārampittāṉ. appāl avikaḷi ariyil piṇikka ārampittāṉ. maruta nila makkaḷ ātiyil pulittōlkaḷi ariyil aṇintum, yāṉit tōlip pōrttiyum vantaṉar. piṉ avarkaḷ paruttiyip payiriṭavum nūṟkavum, āṭi neyyavum payiṉṟa piṉṉarē ari yil parutti āṭiyi aṇiya ārampittaṉar. innilattil veṭpa nili avarkaḷik kuṟinta aḷavu āṭikaḷiyē aṇiyac ceytatu. ivarkaḷiṉ mutalvaṉākiya civaperumāṉ innilattiṉ pirati nitiyāka pulittōli aṇintum yāṉit tōlip pōrttiyum kāḷi mītu amarntum iruppatē nalla cāṉṟāka miḷirkiṟatu.
kaṟkālam māṟi iṅkutāṉ ulōkak kālam arumpiyatu. ataṉ ciṉṉamākavē civaperumāṉ piratipalikkiṟāṉ. avaṉ arukē māṉ niṟkiṟatu. avaṉ māṭṭip paḻakki vākaṉamākak koṇṭirukkiṟāṉ. uṭukkiyum, akkiṉiyum, tiricūlamum, maḻuvum piṟa āyutaṅkaḷum karattil ēnti niṟkiṟāṉ. maṉita varkkattiṉ pakivarkaḷi nirmūlañ ceykiṉṟāṉ. yāṉiyik koṉṟu ataṉ tōlip pōrviyākap pōrttuk koṇ ṭiruppatum puliyik koṉṟu ataṉ melliya tōli ariyil tarittiruppatum aḻakaṟṟa koṭiya kaṭittara makaḷi (apamāra puruṣaṉi) kāliṉ kīḻ pōṭṭu mitittu niṟpatuvum taliyil kaṅkiyi vittiruppatum, iṭappākattil malimakaḷi vit tiruppatum, avaṉ tāṇṭavaṉākat tikaḻvatum, kiyil kamaṇ ṭalam vittiruppatum, piṟavum in nilattiṉiṉṟē tamiḻakattiṉ nākarīkam aṉittum vaḷarntaṉa eṉpatik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. civaperumāṉ maruta nilattiṉ ciṉṉam eṉṟu kūṟiṉāl atu mikavum poruttamāṉatāka irukkum. innilattiltāṉ piraputtuva camūkam tuḷirttatu. maṉṉarkaḷuṭaṉ maṇimakuṭaṅkaḷum, ceṅkōlum, māṭamāḷikikaḷum, kūṭakōpuraṅkaḷum, kōyilkaḷum, aṇi kaḷum, maṇikaḷum necavum, āṭikaḷum, āyutaṅkaḷum moḻiyum eḻuttukaḷum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum kalikaḷum eṇṇaṟṟa icik karuvikaḷum icikaḷum maruttuva nūṟkaḷum vaḷarntaṉa.
innilattiltāṉ parattiyar eṉṟa camūkam piṟantatu. maṉṉar kaḷum matimantirikaḷum, pulavarkaḷum ciṟpikaḷum, ōviyarkaḷum, maruttuvarkaḷum, aṟivāḷikaḷum iṅkutāṉ arumpiṉar. camaya neṟikaḷ iṅku tāṉ eḻuntaṉa. aṟaneṟikaḷ, oḻukkaṅkaḷ iṅku tāṉ kāl kōliṉa. itaṟku iṉṟu namatu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa.
innilamē kalikaḷukkuk kāppiṭamāka iruntatu. ellāt teyvaṅkaḷum iṅku vantu tāṉ kuṭi pukuntaṉa. pērūrum, pērilak kiyaṅkaḷum iṅkutāṉ tōṉṟiṉa.
maṇ viḷakkum, ulōka viḷakkum
innilattil kalcaṭṭikaḷ, maṇcaṭṭikaḷ, kal uruvaṅkaḷ, maṇ uruvaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. pacumiyāṉa mākkaṟkaḷik koṇṭu pāṉikaḷum caṭṭikaḷum kiviḷakku kaḷum uruvākkiṉar. atē pōṉṟu cuṭumaṇṇāṟ pāṉikaḷum caṭṭikaḷum kalayaṅkaḷum, kuṭaṅkaḷum aṭuppukaḷum viḷakkukaḷum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
kaḷimaṇṇāl takaṭukaḷ ceytu cuṭṭu ēṭukaḷāka mutaṉ mutalākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. cuṭumaṇṇāl teyva uruvaṅkaḷ iṅku ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. cuṭumāṇṇāl aḻakiya kiviḷakkukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. (paṭam 4 pārkka)
kalliṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa kuttuviḷakkukaḷum ki viḷakku kaḷum upayōkap paṭuttappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl atikamākac cuṭumaṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa ki viḷakkukaḷē upayōkap paṭuttappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
innilattiliruntutāṉ pacu neyyum, erumi neyyum eḷ neyyum (eṇṇeyum) viḷakkeṇṇeyum, piṇṇāk keṇ ṇeyum, vēppeṇṇeyum, kuṟukkeṇṇeyum tōṉṟiyatu.
kaṟkālam māṟi ulōkak kālam arumpiyatum innilatti ṉiṉṟē cempu, pittaḷi ulōkaṅkaḷil kiviḷakkukaḷ, tūkku viḷakkukaḷ, tūṇṭāmaṇi viḷakkukaḷ, tīvarttikaḷ, nili viḷak kukaḷ, kuttu viḷakkukaḷ, caraviḷakkukaḷ, tīpalaṭcumi viḷakkukaḷ, kiḷi viḷakkukaḷ, pūci viḷakkukaḷ pōṉṟa viḷakkukaḷum ivikaḷ ovvoṉṟilum palvēṟu camayak kuṟikaḷik kāṭṭum palvēṟu vitamāṉa ciṉṉaṅkaḷum viḷakkukaḷi aḻaku paṭuttuvataṟ keṉṟu palvēṟu uruvaṅkaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭa eḻil viḷakku kaḷumāka āyirakkaṇakkāṉa vitamuḷḷa viḷakkukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉar marutanilattil arumpiya cuṭumaṇ viḷakkilē kali tuḷirkka ārampittu viṭṭatu. (paṭam 5 pārkka)

muccuṭar vaḻipāṭu
tamiḻarkaḷiṉ neṟi uyarnta tattuvatti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭatu. iṟivaṉ aimpulaṉiṟkum eṭṭātavaṉ. avaṉ ellāmāy allavumāy ilaṅkupavaṉ. avaṉ piṟavātavaṉ, iṟavātavaṉ. avaṉukku ūrum illi, peyarum illi. avaṉ ulakil avatarippatē illi. avaṉik kaṇṭavar evarum illi. piramaṉ, tirumāl mutaliya tēvarkaḷ kūṭa  avaṉuṭiya aṭiyiyum muṭiyiyum kāṇa muyaṉṟu tōlviyuṟṟārkaḷ eṉṟellām kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻ maṟiyāṉatu,
	ippaṭiyaṉ inniṟattaṉ ivvaṇṇattaṉ
	ivaṉ iṟivaṉ eṉṟeḻutik kāṭṭoṇātē
eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
ippaṭi irukkat tamiḻarkaḷ tiruviḷakkit teyvamākak kumpiṭuvatu muṟiyāveṉṟu cilar viṉavukiṉṟārkaḷ akkēḷvi niyāyamāṉa kēḷviyēyākum.
tamiḻiṉam, arumpiyapoḻutē iṟivaṉiṉ māperum tattuvaṅkaḷiyellām kāṇavilli. avarkaḷ neruppu, ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ mutaliya iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷiṉiṉṟē iṟivaṉik kaṇṭaṉar. eṉavē, tattuvaṅ kaṭanta taṟparaṉikkūṭa iyaṟki vaṭiviṉaṉ eṉṟu pōṟṟukiṉṟaṉar.
ellāt tattuvaṅkaḷiyum iyaṟkiyiṉiṉṟē avarkaḷ kaṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ kaṇṭa māperum tattuvaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭi  aṉittum  iyaṟkiyiliruntē eḻuntaṉavākum. eṉavētāṉ,
	irunilaṉāyt tīyāki nīrumāki
	iyamāṉaṉāy eṟiyum kāṟṟumāki
eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu.
civa camayap periyār tiru.vi.ka. avarkaḷ,  paḻantamiḻarkaḷ iyaṟki niliyamākiya mivariyil iyaṟki yuṇavukoṇṭu, iyaṟkiyuṭiyaṇintu ulakeṉum iyaṟki nūl payiṉṟu iyaṟkiyuṭaṉ kūṭi iyaṟkiyip pāṭi iṉpa vāḻvu naṭātti vanta pōtu iyaṟkiyiṉuḷ piriviṉṟi viravinilavum aḻiyā aḻaku pulaṉāyiruttal vēṇṭum. iyaṟkiyuḷ vāḻvōrkkuk ki puṉintiyaṟṟāk kaviṉ peṟuvaṉappu eḷitil pulaṉākum eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.1
tamiḻarkaḷ ātiyil  tīyiṉiṉṟē taṅkaḷatu arumperum teyva uṇmikaḷiyellām kaṇṭamiyiṉālē tīyāṉatu tiru viḷakkākap pariṇamittatu. atu pōṟṟappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. eṉpati nām niṉivil iruttik koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
tamiḻnāṭṭu makkaḷ naṉiciṟanta nākarikatti eyti niṉṟa pōtum, avarkaḷ tam vāḻviṟkum vaḷamaḷitta neruppi maṟakkavilli. avarkaḷ ātiyil neruppik kaṭavuḷākak kaṇṭu, ataṉip payapaktiyuṭaṉ vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ. appāl paṭippaṭiyāka viḷakkiyum, ñāyiṟiyum, tiṅkaḷiyum vaḻipaṭa ārampittaṉar.2 avarkaḷ nāṭu - nakaraṅkaḷiyum, māṭamāḷikikaḷiyum, kūṭa kōpuraṅkaḷiyum, palvēṟu maṇikaḷiyum, aṇikaḷiyum, maruntukaḷiyum, ulōkaṅkaḷiyum, moḻikaḷiyum, maṟikaḷi yum, ilakkiyaṅkaḷiyum, uruvākkiya kālattum neruppiyum ñāyiṟṟiyum, tiṅkaḷiyum maṟantuviṭavilli. ivikaḷē avar kaḷiṉ oppaṟṟa teyvaṅkaḷākap pariṇamittaṉa. ivikaḷiṉiṉṟē murukaṉ, māyōṉ, civaṉ pōṉṟa perun teyvaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa.
tamiḻarkaḷiṉ varalaṟṟi nuṇuki ārāyntāl atil pala ariya uṇmikaḷ kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ, malikaḷilum atiyaṭutta kāṭukaḷilum ataṟkaṭutta vaḷam vāynta kaḷaṉikaḷ arukilum kaṭaṟkarikaḷilum vāḻntu vantaṉar.
iyaṟkiyōṭu iyaṟkiyāy iṇintu iṉpa vāḻvu eytiṉar. ārampakālattil avarkaḷ vayiṟṟup paciyāl varunta villi. vāḻkkikku vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷkaḷi eṇṇi vāṭavilli. avarkaḷ arukē paḻuttuk kuluṅkum paḻa maraṅkaḷiṉ arukē ceṉṟu vayiṟārap pucittaṉar. pakkattil paḷiṅku pōl caracaraveṉṟu ōṭum kāṉāṟṟu nīrit tārāḷamākattākan tīrum maṭṭum kuṭittu makiḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ nilattiṟ paṭuttum, nīriṟ kuḷittum, ñāyiṟu oḷiyil tirintum nalla kāṟṟi nukarntum uṭal uram peṟṟum, uḷḷa makiḻcciyuṟṟum nīṇṭanāḷ vāḻntu iṉpa vāḻvu naṭātti vantaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ camūka vaḷarcciyilē uṇavil paṟṟākkuṟi eḻuntatu avarkaḷ iṭam peyara ārampittaṉar. uḻavuttoḻiliyum ēṉiya kit toḻiliyum kaṇṭaṉar.3
iyaṟki vāḻvu naṭattiya paḻantamiḻ makkaḷ iyaṟkiyiyē iṟivaṉākak kaṇṭaṉar. ataṉ palaṉāka vāḻvil iṉpaṅ koṇṭaṉar; iyaṟki vaḻiyē iṟivaḻiyeṉa viṇṭaṉar.
olkāp perum pukaḻ vāynta tolkāppiyatti uṟṟu nōkkiṉāl makkaḷ ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, neruppu mutaliya oḷip poruḷkaḷiṉ vāyilāka iṟivaṉi vaḻi paṭṭuvantataṟku eṇṇaṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
	koṭinili kantaḻi vaḷḷi yeṉṟa
	vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ mutalaṉa mūṉṟum
	kaṭavuḷ vāḻttoṭu kaṇṇiya varumē
eṉṟa tolkāppiyac cūttirattāl, tamiḻ makkaḷ ñāyiṟu (koṭi nili), tiṅkaḷ (vaḷḷi), tī (kantaḻi) eṉṉum oḷip poruḷkaḷ vāyilāka iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ eṉpati naṉkaṟiyalām.
kantaḻi oru paṟṟuk kōṭumiṉṟit tāṉākayeḻunta tattuvaṅ kaṭanta poruḷākak koḷḷappaṭum. kantaḻi eṉpatu paṟṟuk kōṭṭi aḻippatu eṉṉum poruḷil aṅkiyik kuṟikkum eṉat tamiḻ mūtaṟiñarkaḷ viḷakka uri nalki irukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
avarkaḷ nalla uṇavi nāṭiya kālattil paṟṟākkuṟi ēṟpaṭṭatu. appoḻutu uṇavu uṟpatti ceyya āṭkaḷ atikam tēvippaṭṭatu. uṟpattik karuviyāka eṇṇappaṭṭa iliṅkam vaṇakkap poruḷāka eḻuntatu.
tamiḻ makkaḷiṭiyē muccuṭar vaḻipāṭu muḷittiruntatu eṉpataṟku eṇṇaṟṟa ēṭṭuc cāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu, makkaḷ vāḻvil muccuṭar vaḻipāṭu vaḷarntu varukiyil ñāyiṟṟiyum tiṅkaḷi yum eppoḻutum kaṇṭu vaḻipaṭutal aritāka iruntatu.
eṉavē ataṟku īṭākat tamiḻakarkaḷ tīvaḻipāṭṭi mēṟ koṇṭaṉar. ākavē tiṉakaraṉ tiṅkaḷ tī eṉṉum muccuṭarkaḷiṉ vaṭivik kuṟikkum vaṭṭam, vil, allatu piṟi, mukkōṇam pōṉṟa vaṭivamāṉa kuṇṭaṅkaḷi amittu avikaḷil tī vaḷarttuk kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar. itutāṉ muttī vēṭṭal eṉṉum camaya vaḻakkākap pariṇamittatu. cīriya makkaḷum, ācīriya makkaḷum, kaltēya makkaḷum, cumēriya makkaḷum, ekiptiya makkaḷum, mokañcatārō makkaḷum, pāracīka makkaḷum, āriya makkaḷum, pūtamakkaḷum neruppi vaṇaṅki vantuḷḷaṉar.
paṉmoḻip pulavarum tamiḻar neṟiyip paḻutaṟa āyn tuṇarntavarum namatu aṉpiṟkum matippiṟkum uriyavarumāṉa pēraṟiñar tiru. kā. cuppiramaṇiya piḷḷi avarkaḷ, tol kāppiyar kālattilē tamiḻakattil mūmmūrttikaḷum avarkaḷukku mēmpaṭṭa muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷum tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa reṉṟum mum mūrttikaḷiyē ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, tī eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭiruppa tākavum kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. (paḻantamiḻar nākarikam tiru.kā.cu. piḷḷi)
eṭuttukkāṭṭāka āciriyar tolkāppiyar kālattilēyē mummūrttikaḷ uṇṭu eṉṟa karuttu muḷikkat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. ellāk kōḷkaḷukkum naṭuviliruntu taṉatu kavarum caktiyāl avaṟṟip piṇittu niṟkiṉṟa katiravaṉuṭiya maṇṭilam koṭi nili eṉappaṭṭatu. iravil payirkaḷukku uyir tarum tiṅkaḷ maṇṭilam amutatti vaḻaṅkuvatākak karutappaṭṭu vaḷḷi eṉṟa peyar peṟṟatu. paṟṟiṉavaṟṟiyellām aḻikkiṉṟa neruppu kantaḻi eṉappaṭṭatu. katiravaṉ oḷiyāl ellām uṇṭātaliṉ cūriya maṇṭalamākiya koṭi nilikku atipati piramā eṉṟum, cantira maṇṭalattiṟku atipati kāttaṟ kaṭavuḷākiya tirumāl eṉṟum, neruppu maṇṭalattiṟku atipati aḻittaṟ kaṭavuḷākiya uruttiraṉ eṉṟum ulakaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum aṭakki niṟkum cakti maṇṭalattiṟku atipati aḻittaṟkaṭavuḷākiya uruttiraṉ eṉṟum ulakaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum aṭakki niṟkum cakti maṇṭa lattiṟku atipati kaṭavuḷ eṉṟum karutappaṭṭatu. kaṭavuḷit tutippōr mummūrttikaḷōṭu avarit toṭarpupaṭuttip pēcu talil koṭi nili kantaḻi vaḷḷi eṉṟa vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ mutalāṉa mūṉṟum kaṭavuḷ vāḻttoṭu kaṇṇiya varumē eṉṟa tolkāppiyac cūttiram eḻuntatākum eṉṟum,
katiravaṉic civattiṟku uvamiyākavum avaṉ oḷiyic caktikku uvamiyākavum vaḻaṅkiya pōtu avvoḷi paraviya iṭa mākiya viṇṇum caktikkup pērāyiṟṟu. viṇṇu eṉṟa pērē viṇṭu eṉavum viṣṇu eṉavum māṟiṟṟu. irukku vētattil viṇṇiṟ paraviya katir oḷiyē viṣṇuvākap pēcappaṭṭatu eṉavum kuṟip piṭappaṭṭa aṟivurikaḷ ārāyattakkaṉa. (tamiḻar camayam pak.48, 52, 53)
mēlum avarkaḷ `katiravaṉi iṭamākak koṇṭa civa pirāṉ vemmiyiṉāl accam viḷivikkum tantit teyva mākak karutappaṭṭapōtu taṇṇaḷiyuṭiya tāyt teyvattiṉ vaṭivam taṇṇīrākak karutappaṭṭatu. āḻamuḷḷa taṇṇīriṉ niṟam  nīlam āyiṟṟu. ātaliṉ tāyt teyvattiṉ niṟamum nīlamāyiṟṟu. cantiraṉ kuḷircci taruvatākat tōṉṟuvatāl cantiraṉ tāyiṉ aruvuruva vaṭivamākak karutappaṭuvataṟku ētuvāyiṟṟu cūriya oḷiyil tōṉṟum ēḻu niṟaṅkaḷil mutalil uḷḷatu civappum iṟutiyiluḷḷatu nīlamum. ātalāl, pāti āṇum pāti peṇṇu mākiya civa vaṭivattil āṇ pakuti cenniṟamākavum peṇ pakuti nīlaniṟamākavum koḷḷappaṭṭatu. civam eṉṟa col muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷik kuṟikkap payaṉpaṭṭapōtu eṅkum niṟintilaṅkum kaṭavuḷiṉ āṟṟalē cakti eṉappaṭṭatu. (tolkāppiyam - poruḷ atikāram cūt. 88 kā. cu. piḷḷi) eṉak kūṟiyiruppatu cintikkat takkatu.
iḵtaṉṟi ulakiluḷḷa palvēṟu makkaḷum tiṉakaraṉ, tiṅkaḷ, tī eṉṟa muccuṭar vaḻipāṭṭil īṭupaṭṭiruntārkaḷ eṉpataṟku ērāḷamāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ uḷa. ekiptiya makkaḷ amaṉ eṉṟa peya rāl cūriyaṉik kaṭavuḷāka vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar. papilōṉ makkaḷiṟ palar hapil civā eṉṟum rapu civā eṉṟum iruteyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar.1 aṅkum civā eṉṟa collukku oḷi eṉṟē poruḷ koḷḷappaṭṭatu. papilōṉ nāṭṭil nippūr eṉṉum ūril periya ñāyiṟṟuk kōyil iruntu vantatu.2
kiṟitavarkaḷ ñāyiṟṟuk kiḻamiyi oru ciṟanta nāḷākak koṇṭāṭuvatu katiravaṉ vaḻipāṭṭiliruntu eḻunta karuttē yākum. epirēya makkaḷ niṟi uvā nāṭkaḷip pōlavē ñāyiṟṟuk kiḻamikaḷilum pali celutti vantārkaḷ. eṉavē ñāyiṟṟuk kiḻamiyiṉ mutaṉmi ñāyiṟu vaḻipāṭu kāraṇamāka eḻuntatēyām.3
tiṉakaraṉ vaḻipāṭu eḻuntapiṉ tiṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭum  palanāṭu kaḷil arumpiyuḷḷaṉa. tiṉakaraṉit tūṇ vaṭivil vaḻipaṭṭa makkaḷ maravaṭivil vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. tiṉakaraṉ (ñāyiṟu), tiṅkaḷ (cantiraṉ) vaṇakkaṅkaḷ epirēya makkaḷiṭam pāl aṭṭōret vaṇakkaṅkaḷāka vaḷarntatu. nāḷaṭivil pāl (pakal) āṇ teyvamākavum aṭōret ākiya tiṅkaḷ peṇ teyvamāka vum pariṇamittaṉa. appāl ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷiṉ maṉiviyākat tiṅkaḷ kaṭavuḷ karutappaṭṭatu. āṇ peṇ taṉmikaḷiṉ ciṉṉaṅ kaḷāka ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ ākiya kaṭavuḷkaḷ eṇṇappaṭṭaṉa. cālamōṉ ñāṉi kaṭṭiya ālayattiṟku muṉ iru perun tūṇkaḷ niṟuttappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṟu pipiḷil kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. avikaḷ ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriya ciṉṉaṅkaḷ eṉṟu ārāycci yāḷarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭṭip pōlavē tī vaḻipāṭum cīriyā, acīriyā, cumēriyā, kaltēyā, ekiptu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil toṉṟu toṭṭu naṭipeṟṟu vantatu. tīkkaṭavuḷukkum kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉa. nōvāviṉ nālām cantatiyil vanta nimrōt eṉpa vaṉāl tī vaḻipāṭu naṭattappaṭṭu vantatu eṉṟu kiṟitavarkaḷiṉ vētanūlil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.1
akkiṉi vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriya aracar, cūriya kulattavar eṉṟum, tiṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriya aracar cantira kulattavar eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. cappāṉiya maṉṉar cūriya kulattavar eṉṟu iṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. atēpōṉṟu pāṇṭiya kulatta varkaḷ cantirakulam eṉṟum cōḻarkaḷ cūriyakulam eṉṟum kūṟap paṭṭu varuvatu, nām aṟintēyākum.
tiṉakaraṉ (ñāyiṟu). tiṅkaḷ (cantiraṉ), tī eṉṉum mūṉṟu oḷiyuṭiya poruḷkaḷālē tamiḻ makkaḷ paṇṭikkālattil vaḻipāṭu naṭatti vantatu pōlavē āriyarkaḷum mut tī vaḻipāṭum ōmaṅkaḷum yākaṅkaḷum ceytu vantaṉar.
ulakameṅkum oru kālattil ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, neruppu ākiya muccuṭar vaḻipāṭu paravi iruntatu. varalāṟukaḷum camaya nūṟkaḷum ataṟku iṉṟum cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa tamiḻmakkaḷ varalāṟu muccuṭar vaḻipāṭu muḻu mutal teyvatti uṇara vaḻikāṭṭuvatāka iruntatu eṉpati viḷakkamāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻnāṭṭuc camayap periyōrkaḷum ilakkiya mētikaḷum iṟivaṉiṉ oḷit taṉmiyi mikat teḷivākat tēṉoḻukum tīntamiḻil namakkut tantirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
	tūṇṭā viḷakkiṉ cuṭaraṇiyāy
eṉṟa cuntaramūrtti cuvāmikaḷiṉ mantira moḻikaḷum.
	`ñāṉac cuṭar viḷakkāy niṉṟavaṉ
	`viṟakiṟ ṟīyiṉaṉ pāliṟ paṭu neyyōṉ
	`maṟiya niṉṟuḷāṉ māmaṇic cōtiyāṉ
eṉṟa appar aṭikaḷiṉ oppilā moḻikaḷum,
	`cōti antam āyiṉum cōtiyuḷ ōr cōtiyāṉum
eṉṟa campantap perumāṉiṉ maṇimoḻiyum
	`oḷi vaḷar viḷakkē ulappilā oṉṟē
	uṇarvu cūḻ kaṭantatōr uṇarvē
	teḷivaḷar paḷiṅkut tiraḷ maṇikkuṉṟē
	cittattuṭ tittikkum tēṉē
eṉṟa tirumāḷikittēvariṉ tiruvicippāvum
	`alakil cōtiyaṉ ampalat tāṭuvāṉ
eṉṟa cēkkiḻār perumāṉiṉ cemmoḻiyum
	`cintātēvi ceḻuṅkali niyamittu
	nantā viḷakkē...... pāvāy
	vāṉōr talivi maṇṇōr mutalvi:
eṉṟu kalimakaḷ perumikūṟum maṇimēkaliyiṉ aṇikeḻu pōkkum.
	`cuṭariṟ curuṭṭum tirumūrtti
eṉṟu kūṟum cintāmaṇiyiṉ nantāp perumiyum
	`tiṅkaḷip pōṟṟutum! tiṅkaḷip pōṟṟutum!
	koṅkalar tārc ceṉṉi kuḷir veṇkuṭi pōṉṟiv
	vaṅka ṇula kaḷittalāṉ;
	ñāyiṟu pōṟṟutum! ñāyiṟu pōṟṟutum
	kāviri nāṭaṉṟikiripōṟ poṟkōṭṭu
	mēru valan tiritalāṉ
eṉṟa cilappatikāra ilakkiyac ceṟivum
	`palar pukaḻ ñāyiṟu
eṉṟu kūṟum nakkīraṉiṉ naṉmoḻiyum
	`munnīr mīmici palar toḻat tōṉṟi
	yēmuṟa viḷaṅkiya cuṭar
eṉṟu naṟṟiṇi navilum nayamoḻiyum
	`miṉṉuruvāy muṉṉuruvil vēta nāṉkāy
	viḷakkoḷiyāy muḷit teḻunta tiṅkaḷ tāṉāyp
	piṉṉuruvāy muṉṉuruvil piṇi mūppillāp
	piṟappiliyāy yiṟappataṟkē yeṇṇātu eṇṇum
	poṉṉuruvāy maṇiyuruvil pūtamintāyp
	puṉaluruvāy aṉaluruvil tikaḻuñ cōti
	taṉṉuruvāy eṉṉuruvil niṉṟa enti
	taḷir puriyum tiruvaṭiyeṉa talimēlavē
eṉṟu tirumaṅki āḻvār tiruttāṇṭakattil viḷakkoḷip perumāḷukku ariya māli cūṭṭi vaṇaṅkuvatum naṉku ārāyka.
	`cutta nirkuṇamāṉa parateyvamē
	parañcōtiyē cukavāriyē
eṉṟu tāyumāṉavar aḻakoḷirak kūṟum aruḷ moḻiyum uṟṟu nōkkaṟkuriyatu.
cutta camaraca caṉmārkka caṅka niṟuviyārum mika muṟpōk kuṭiya camaya ñāṉiyumāṉa irāmaliṅka cuvāmikaḷ,
	`viṇṇār ceñcuṭarē
		cuṭar mēviya vuḷḷoḷiyē
	taṇṇār veṇmatiyē
		yatiṟṟaṅkiya taṇṇamutē
	kaṇṇār meykkaṉalē
		civakāmap peṇ kātalaṉē
	aṇṇā tantaṉiyē
		yaruḷāramu tantaṉiyē
eṉṟum, arumaṟiyātta tirumūlar.
	`viḷakkiṉi ēṟṟi
		veḷiṉaya aṟimiṉ
	viḷakkiṉ muṉṉē
		vētaṉi māṟum
	viḷakki viḷakkum
		viḷakkuṭiyārkaḷ
	viḷakkiṉ viḷakkum
		vuḷakkavar tāmē
eṉṟu pāṭiyiruppatiyum uṟṟu nōkkuka.
ulakameṅkum mūccuṭar vaḻipāṭu paravi iruntatu. eṉṟālum ivikaḷi mikac cīrum ciṟappumāyk koṇṭāṭiyavarkaḷ tamiḻar kaḷēyāvar. tamiḻakattil taḷirtta civa, viṇava camayattārkaḷ maṭṭumiṉṟit tamiḻakattiṟku viruntāḷiyāka vanta camaṇa, putta camayaṅkaḷumkūṭa muccuṭar vaḻipāṭṭi ēṟṟiruntaṉa eṉṟu camaya ārāycciyil īṭupaṭṭa pēraṟiñarkaḷ cilar kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.
iṉṉum aṭiyil kaṇṭa camaya paktarkaḷiṉ tēṉoḻukum tīñcuvip pāṭalkaḷāl avarkaḷatu muccuṭar vaḻipāṭṭiṉ uṇmiyi maṭṭumiṉṟit tiruviḷakku vaḻipāṭṭiṉ uṇmiyi yum naṉku kāṇalām. avi vaṟumāṟu.
karuṅkaṭaliṉiṉṟu poṅki eḻum poṉṉiṟa oḷippanti yoppat takatakaveṉak kīḻtticiyil ñāyiṟu, māperum viḷakkippōl oḷiviṭṭōṅkit tikaḻkiṉṟāṉ. itu oru tīpa uccipōl tikaḻkiṟatu. immāperum tiruviḷakkiṟkuk kaṭal, ney pōl kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. pūmiyāṉatu takaḻipōl viḷaṅkukiṟatu. itip poyki āḻvār eṉṉum viṇavap periyār tam teyvīkak kaṇkaḷāl kāṇkiṟār. uṭaṉē akkāṭci avaratu nāvil paktip pāṭalāy arumpukiṟatu. atu varumāṟu :
	viyam takaḻiyāy vārkaṭalē neyyāka
	veyya katirōṉ viḷakkākac ceyya
	cuṭarāḻiyiṉ aṭikkē cūṭṭiṉēṉ colmāli
	iṭarāḻi nīṅkavē yeṉṟu.
ivvarumperum pāmālikku urikaṇṭa āciriyar, `nilam, neykkum tirikkum ātāramāka niṟkum takaḻiyākavum atāvatu akalākavum, neṭiya nilak karuṅkaṭalāṉatu eṇṇeyākavum veppamāṉa oḷikkatirkaḷiyuṭiya katiravaṉi viḷakkākavum vittuc civanta oḷic cakkarattiyuṭiya paṅkayak kaṇṇaṉ pātāra vintattilē eṉatu pāmāliyi nāṉ tuṉpak kaṭalik kaṭakkac cūṭṭiṉēṉ eṉṟu uri kūṟiyatōṭu, ippaṭippaṭṭa ettuṇiyō cūriya utayaṅkaḷip poyki āḻvār pārt tirukka vēṇṭum. pārkkum poḻutellām parantāmaṉiṉ aruṭ cōti avaratu uḷḷattilē inta iyaṟkiyiṉ eḻiliyum oḷiyiyum pārkkap pārkka aṉantacayaṉa āḻiyāṉuṭiya aḻakum oḷiyum ippaṭittāṉ irukkumō eṉṟa āccariyam poṅkiyatu. araṅkap perumāṉiṉ āḻiyi (cakkaratti) niṉi vūṭṭiya anta utaya cūriyaṉiṉ kāṭci uṭaṉē uḷḷattil pukuntu aruṭpāṭalāka ūṟṟeṭuttup poṅki eḻuntatu. avarukku iṭarāka niṉṟa tuṉpac camuttiram vilaki eṅkō māyamāy maṟintuviṭṭatu pōl tōṉṟiyatu. poykiyāḻvār iyaṟṟiya poyyāt tiru viḷakku namakku oppaṟṟa ñāṉaviḷakkākum. tuṉpa iruḷi akaṟṟi iṉpa oḷiyām iṟivaṉik kaṇṭu uvappataṟkuriya tuṇik karuviyākum eṉṟu virivuri kūṟiyuḷḷār.
ippāmāliyit taḻuviya maṟṟoru viṇava camayap pēraṟiñarākiya pūtattāḻvār.
	aṉpē takaḻiyāy
		ārvamē neyyāka
	iṉpuruku cinti
		iṭutiriyāy naṉpuruki
	ñāṉac cuṭarviḷakku
		ēṟṟiṉēṉ nāraṇaṟku
	ñāṉat tamiḻ purinta nāṉ
eṉṟu eḻiloḻuka nārāyaṇaṉukku naṟṟamiḻ māli cūṭṭi yuḷḷār. aññāṉa iruḷi akaṟṟi ñāṉac cuṭarkāṇa nalvaḻi kāṭṭuvatāka eṇṇukiṉṟārkaḷ.
payilum cuṭar oḷi mūrttip paṅkayak kaṇṇaṉ eṉṟu nāraṇaṉ naṟcuṭaroḷiyāy nantā viḷakkāy oḷirum aṟputak kōlatti namatu nammāḻvārum navilukiṉṟār.
iṟivaṉ eṇṇaṟṟa peyarkaḷāl aḻikkappaṭiṉum ava ṉukkup peyarum illi. ūrum illi. avaṉi ettaṉiyō uruvaṅkaḷāl vaḻipaṭiṉum avaṉukku uruvamē illi. avaṉukku aṭiyum illi. muṭiyum illi. avaṉuṭiya muṭiyiyum aṭiyiyum tēvāti tēvarkaḷum kāṇamuṭiyāmal pōyiṟṟu. avaṉ viṟakil aṭaṅki niṟkum tīyip pōl namatu ūṉak kaṇ kaḷāl variyaṟuttuk kāṇavum kūṟavum muṭiyāta akaṇṭa cōti yāvāṉ eṉṟa karuttic ceṅkarumpeṉat tittikkum centamiḻp pāviṉāl namakku eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟār. iṟivaṉum virumpip paṭikka viḻivatākak kūṟum tiruvācakatti yātta maṇivācakap perumāṉ,
	cōtiyē cuṭarē cūḻoḷi viḷakkē
		curi kuḻaṟ paṇamuli maṭanti
	pātiyē paraṉē pāl koḷ veṇṇīṟṟāy
		paṅkayat tayaṉum mālaṟiyā
	nītiyē celvat tirupperun tuṟiyuḷ
		niṟimalark kurunta mēviya cīr
	ātiyē yaṭiyēṉ ātarittaḻittāl
		atantuvē eṉṟaruḷāyē
eṉṟu cūṭṭiya pāmāliyiṉ karuttu makkaḷ uḷḷatti vacīkarikkat takkatākum.
tamiḻar kaṇṭa nāṉmaṟiyil oṉṟākiya tirumantiramāṉatu iṟivaṉukku inta uṭampu ōr ālayam eṉṟum uḷḷam karuvari eṉṟum teḷḷiya aṟivu peṟṟavarkaḷukku āṉmāvē pūjik kuriya civaliṅkam eṉṟum aṟivu peṟātavarkaḷik kaviḻkkum pulaṉkaḷ inta uṭampākiya tirukkōvilukku ēṟṟiya iruḷakaṟṟum aintu aṇiyāta maṇiviḷakkukaḷ eṉṟum kūṟukiṟatu. atu varumāṟu :
	uḷḷam peruṅ kōyil
		ūṉuṭampu ālayam
	vaḷḷal pirāṉārkku
		vāy kōpura vācal
	teḷḷat teḷintārkkuc
		cīvaṉ civaliṅkam
	kaḷḷap pulaṉintum
		kāḷā maṇiviḷakkē	(tirumūlar tirumantiram)

tīpat tirunāḷ
paṇṭit tamiḻakattil, iṉṟu koṇṭāṭappaṭṭu varum irāmanavami, kōkulāṣṭami, kiruṣṇa jayanti, āvaṇiyaviṭṭam pōṉṟa paṇṭikikaḷil etuvum koṇṭāṭappaṭṭu vantatākat teriyavilli, tīpāvaḷip paṇṭiki kūṭak koṇṭāṭiyatākat teriyavilli.
paṅkuṉi mātam pāratappōr naṭipeṟṟatāl ammātattil nalla kāriyaṅkaḷ etiyum ceyyārkaḷ. āṭi mātattil ciṟanta civapaktaṉāṉa irāvaṇēvaraṉ iṟantuviṭṭatāl ammātattil tirumaṇam mutaliya maṅkaḷakaramāṉa kāriyaṅkaḷic ceyyātu ammātam muḻutum tukkam koṇṭāṭi vantārkaḷ.
ti mātam tamiḻ makkaḷ ellōrum poṅkal paṇṭiki yākak koṇṭāṭi vantārkaḷ. iṉṟu tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ araciyal vātikaḷ atip pāṭṭāḷiyiṉ paṇṭiki eṉṟu koṇṭāṭi varukiṉṟārkaḷ. yār eppaṭik koṇṭāṭiṉum atu paṇṭu toṭṭu namatu nāṭṭil naṭipeṟṟu varum oru perum paṇṭiki yāka miḷirkiṟatu eṉpatil aiyamilli.
kārttikit tirunāḷ
tamiḻakattil ciṟappākap paṇṭu toṭṭut tamiḻarkaḷ koṇ ṭāṭi varum paṇṭiki tīpattirunāḷākum. itu kārttikit tirunāḷ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. itu tamiḻnāṭṭilum kēraḷattilum āntirattilum kaṉṉaṭattilum iṉṟum koṇṭāṭappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
paṇṭit tamiḻarkaḷ malic cikaraṅkaḷilē viḷakkēṟṟi viḻākkoṇṭāṭi viruntuṇṭu kaḷikoṇṭu āṭi niṟpar, itaṟkuc caṅka nūṟkaḷilē cāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu.
	`aṟumīṉ payanta aṟañcey tiṅkaḷ
	celcuṭar neṭuṅkoṭi pōla
eṉṟu naṟṟiṇi kūṟukiṟatu. ataṟku, eḻilmiku kārttiki nāḷiṉ peyarālē peṟṟa aṟañceytaṟkuriya tiṅkaḷil eṭukkappaṭṭa vicumpilē celkiṉṟa oḷiyiyuṭiya nīṇṭa viḷakkukaḷiṉ varici pōla eṉṟu uri kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
patiṇeṇ kīḻkaṇakkil oṉṟu kār nāṟpatu. itu kaṭic caṅkap pulavarkaḷil oruvarāṉa maturik kaṇṇaṉ kūttaṉār eṉpavarāl pāṭap peṟṟatu. kārkālam varuvataṟku muṉ vīṭu tirumpi viṭuvēṉ eṉṟu kātaliyiṭam kūṟiviṭṭu maṉṉaṉuṭaṉ pōrt tuṇiyāyp pōṉa kātalaṉ, varattāmatamākiviṭṭatu. kārkālam aṇmittu viṭṭatu. kātalaṉ varātatu kaṇṭu talivi mukamvāṭi, maṉam nonnu, taṉ tōḻiyiṭam taṉ maṉa varuttattik kūṟukiṟāḷ. atip pulavar piṉvarumāṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṟār.
	nalamiku kārttiki nāṭṭava riṭṭa
	talināḷ viḷakkiṟ ṟakiyuṭiya vākip
	pulamelām pūttaṉa tōṉṟic cilamoḻi
	tūtoṭu vanta maḻi
