eṉṟālum, ippoḻutu veḷḷi mulām pūciya viḷakku kaḷum `evar cilvar viḷakkukaḷum ērāḷamākap paravi varukiṉṟaṉa. ippoḻutu naṭuttara makkaḷiṭiyēyum evarcilvar viḷakkukaḷ peritum iṭam peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṟu miṉcāra viḷakku eṅkum paraviyatāl evar cilvar kuttu viḷakkiṉ eṇṇikkiyi ōraḷavu pātittirukkiṟatu eṉṟu tiriyamākak kūṟalām.
cātāraṇat tamiḻarkaḷ vīṭṭil katiravaṉ maṟintatum kuttu viḷakki ēṟṟip pūcikaḷ ceyvatupōl paṇakkārarkaḷ vīṭu kaḷil kuttuviḷakkēṟṟuvatilli. eṉṟālum avarkaḷ vīṭṭil naṭipeṟum tirumaṇattil maṇavaṟiyil kuttuviḷakku mutal iṭam peṟṟirukkiṟatu. avarkaḷ naṭattum miṉcārap poruḷ vaṇika niliyatti ārampikkum poḻutu kūṭa mutalil kuttu viḷakki ēṟṟi ārampa viḻā naṭattukiṉṟaṉar.
mulimkaḷiṉ illattil viḷakkukaḷ
civarkaḷ, viṇavarkaḷ, puttarkaḷ, camaṇarkaḷip pōl tamiḻ mulimkaḷ tiruviḷakki attuṇic ciṟappākap pōṟṟamāṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇik koṇṭiruntēṉ. ceṉṟa āṇṭu ākaṭu mātam ilaṅkikkup pōyiruntēṉ. appoḻutu eṉatu māvaṭṭattic cērnta mulim naṇpar oruvaric cantikka avaratu vaṇika niliyattiṟkuc ceṉṟiruntēṉ. avar putitāka oru niḻal paṭampiṭikkum niliyam oṉṟi ārampittu naṭattuvati aṟintu aṅku ceṉṟēṉ. anta niliyatti ilaṅki muṉṉāḷ piratama mantiri ṭaṭli cēṉanāyaka, mutaṉ mutalāka tiruviḷakkēṟṟi avvaṇika niliyatti tiṟantu vittuḷḷār. atip paṭam piṭittu niliyattil pukuntatum yāvarum eḷitil aṟiyumāṟu kaṇṇāṭic caṭṭattil pōṭṭu vittirukkiṟār. ataṉ piṉṉar eṉatu eṇṇam tavaṟu eṉpatik kaṇṭu tiruttik koṇṭēṉ.
tāmariyik kaliñarkaḷum kaviñarkaḷum ciṟpikaḷum tattuva ñāṉikaḷum pōṟṟuvatippōl aṉivarum tiruviḷakkip pōṟṟukiṟārkaḷ. tiruviḷakkup peṇkaḷiṉ pōṟṟutalukkut tāṉ uriyatu eṉṟu eṇṇukiṟārkaḷ, kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.
āṉāl, periya vētāntiyum riṣikēcam tiru. civāṉanta caravati avarkaḷiṉ cīṭarum teṉṉintiya tivviya jīvaṉa caṅkat talivarumāṉa tiru. atvitāṉanta caravati cuvāmikaḷ eṉpār, ilaṅkiyililuḷḷa mallākam eṉṉum ūriluḷḷa āṉanta vittiyācāli eṉṉumiṭattal 22-12-60il nikaḻttiya coṟ poḻivil piṉvarumāṟu kūṟiṉār.
maṅkaḷakaramāṉa kuttuviḷakkil māperum tattuvaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. ataṉ aṭippākamākiya tāmari pōṉṟa ācaṉam (pātam) pirammāvākavum ataṉ neṭiya taṇṭāṉatu tiru māl ākavum, ney ēntum akal uruttiraṉākavum, tirimukikaḷ makēvaraṉākavum, ellāvaṟṟiṟkum cikaramāka uḷḷa nuṉippākam, catācivaṉākavum, neyyāṉatu nātamākavum, tiri pintuvākavum, cuṭar tiru makaḷākavum, tīppiḻampu kalimakaḷākavum, caṅkārañ ceyyum vallamiyāṉa tī caktiyākavum, īṟṟil pañcēntiriyaṅ kaḷi naṭāttum parappiramamākavum viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. namatu camaya tattuvaṅkaḷ aṉittum orē viḷakkil kāṇum paṭiyāka namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ amittiruppatu mikap periya aṟputamēyākum (tiṉakaraṉ, koḻumpu) itaṉip paṭitta piṉṉar tāṉ tiruviḷakkup peṇkaḷiṉ pōṟṟutalukku maṭṭumē uriyateṉṟu eṇṇuvōr tamatu karutti māṟṟi āṇkaḷiṉ - ēṉ? tattuva ñāṉikaḷiṉ pōṟṟutalukkum uriyatu eṉpati uṇarvārkaḷ eṉṟu nampu kiṟēṉ. iḵtillāviṭil āṇkaḷi viṭap peṇkaḷ viḷakkiṉ tattuvatti naṉkaṟintu pōṟṟi varukiṟār eṉṟāvatu oru muṭi viṟku vara vēṇṭum.
tiru.ṭi.e. iraṅkanāyaki ammiyār, kuttu viḷakku makkaḷiṉ uṭalik kāṭṭukiṟatu. eppaṭi? paruvam varappeṟṟa āṇ peṇṇākiya iru pālarukkum nāpiyiliruntu mēl nōkkik koṭi pōla oru rēki ōṭum. iti avaravarkaḷē pārttut terintu koḷḷalām. nāḷ āka āka ikkōṭu naṉṟākap patiyum. yōkam kivarap peṟṟavarkaḷukku mūlātārattiliruntu kuṇṭaliṉiyi eḻuppi neṟṟiyilē puruva mattiyilirukkum iṭattilēyē cōtip piḻampākak kāṭṭuvārkaḷ. accōtip piḻampu kuttuviḷakku erivatu pōl irukkum. maṉitaratu iṭuppukkuk kīḻppaṭṭa pākamē kuttu viḷakkiṉ pātam. iṭuppiliruntu mēl nōkki ōṭum rēkik koṭiyē kuttu viḷakkut taṇṭu. kaḻuttukku mēṟpaṭṭa namatu taliyē kuttuviḷakkiṉ tali. puruva mattiyil cōti colippatē kuttuviḷakku erivatākum. ākavē kuttuviḷakku ēṟṟuvatiṉ artta meṉṉa? yōkiyi vaṇaṅkuvatu; ātma jōtiyi vaṇaṅkuvatu, iṟivaṉi vaṇaṅkuvatu; ñāṉam ki vara vēṇṭuvatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār.
āṇkaḷukkellām eṭṭāta intak kuttuviḷakkiṉ tattuvattip peṇkaḷ aṟintiruppataṉāl avarkaḷ tiru viḷakkup pūciyit taṅkaḷukkē contamāṉatākat taṉi urimi koṇṭāṭukiṟārkaḷ pōlum. aḵtaṉṟi avarkaḷ kuṭumpa viḷakkākavum, illaṟa viḷakkākavum, illaka viḷakkākavum iruppatāl taṅkaḷiṉ iṉamākiya kuttuviḷakkip pōṟṟu kiṟārkaḷ pōlum. iḵtaṉṟi avarkaḷ kuṭumpa peṇṇiṟku irukka vēṇṭiya aṉpu, aṟivu, uṟuti, nitāṉam, poṟumi ākiya aintu kuṇaṅkaḷiyum kuttuviḷakkiṉ aintu mukaṅkaḷum niṉip pūṭṭuvatāka nampukiṟārkaḷ. viḷakkiṉ akalil kāṇum eṇ ṇeyk kuḻiyē kuṭumpap peṇṇiṉ āḻnta uḷḷam. atiliruntu ūṭṭappeṟum aintu oḷiyip pōl kuṭumpap peṇṇiṉ aintu kuṇaṅkaḷum oruṅku kūṭi oḷirum pōtu illam iṉpa oḷi vīci nalam pala poṅkip pirakācikkiṟatu. peṇ makaḷ kuttu viḷakkiṉ mūlam peṟṟa ñāṉattil kuṭumpa viḷakkāka oḷirkiṟāḷ. `illaka viḷakkatu eṉṉum pārāṭṭukku uriyavaḷ ākiṉṟāḷ.
inta illaka viḷakkiṉāl illaṟam nallaṟamāvati uṉṉit tamiḻ maṟiyil, poyyā moḻiyār.
	illatu eṉ illavaḷ
		māṇpāṉāl uḷḷatu eṉ
	illavaḷ māṇāk kaṭi
eṉṟār. oruvaṉukku, illāḷ, illaka viḷakkāka naṟkuṇa, naṟceyalkaḷ uḷḷavaḷāka oḷirntāl avaṉukku illātatu yātu? avaḷ illāviṭṭāl avaṉukku etu uṇṭu? eṉṉum karuttup paṭa eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.
vīṭṭu viḷakkukaḷil virumpattakkatu kuttu viḷakkēyākum. paṇṭik kālattil kiviḷakkukaḷ eṇṇikkiyil atikamāka iruntirukkalām. āṉāl iṉṟu eṇṇikkiyil atikamāka eṅkum paraviyiruppatu kuttuviḷakkēyākum. itiyē namatu! peṇ makkaḷ peritum virumpip pūcikkuriyatākat tērnteṭuttiruk kiṟārkaḷ. vīṭṭiṟku aḻaku tarum viḷakkum ituvēyākum.
intak kuttu viḷakkiltāṉ namatu ciṟpikaḷiṉ kavaṉa mellām celuttappaṭṭu eṇṇaṟṟa eḻil viḷakkukaḷ uruvāk kappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
mottamākak kūṟuvatāṉāl tamiḻ nāṭṭil āyirattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa vitamāṉa aḻaku viḷakkukaḷ uṇṭu. avaṟṟil kuttu viḷakkil maṭṭum 400kku mēṟpaṭṭa vitaṅkaḷip pārkka muṭiyum. eṉṉiṭattil maṭṭum 100kku mēṟpaṭṭa kuttuviḷakkukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
inta viḷakkukaḷ kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭattil oru vita mākavum nelli māvaṭṭattil maṟṟoru vitamākavum maturi māvaṭṭattil maṟṟum oru vitamākavum tirucci māvaṭṭattil piṟitōr vitamākavum tañci māvaṭṭattil iṉṉoru vita mākavum ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu māvaṭṭattil putiyatōr vitamākavum māvaṭṭattiṟku māvaṭṭam vēṟupaṭṭa eḻiluṭaṉ miḷirkiṉṟaṉa.
kuttuviḷakkil pātam vaṭṭamākavum caturamākavum amik kappaṭṭu naṭuvil taṇṭum, cūḻa vaṭṭamāka oru paḷḷamum kāṇap paṭum. taṇṭu kuḻalākavum cilavaṟṟil kaṭṭiyākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. cila pātaṅkaḷ kaṭṭiyāṉatākavum, cila pātaṅkaḷ takaṭāṉatākavum uḷḷaṉa. cila pātaṅkaḷil mika mika arumiyāṉa pū vēlip pāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kiṭṭat taṭṭap paṇṭikkāla vaṭṭat tāmpāḷattil tīṭṭappaṭṭa aḻakiya vēlippāṭukaḷ aṉittum viḷakkiṉ pātaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pātattil kāṇappaṭum kuḻivu kaḷum neḷivukaḷum mika aṟputamāka oḷirkiṉṟaṉa.
pātattiṉ kīḻ cila viḷakkukaḷukku mūṉṟu kālkaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. nāṉku kālkaḷ uḷḷa viḷakkukaḷum uṇṭu. perum pālāṉa viḷakkukaḷ pātam pūmiyil poruntum paṭiyākavē ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. pātamāṉatu kiṭṭattaṭṭa pīṭam pōl kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. pātattiṉ mēl taṇṭiṉ aṭippākam peruttu mēlē pōkap pōkac ciṟuttu iraṇṭu aṭi, mūṉṟu aṭi, nāṉku aṭi uyaram nīṇṭu niṟkum pātattiṉ mītu niṟkum taṇṭiṉ aṭip pakkattilum aḻakiya ciṟpa vēlippāṭukaḷ ceṟintilaṅkum.  perumpālāṉa viḷakkukaḷil pātam taṉiyākavum taṇṭu taṉiyāka vum, mēṟpākam taṉiyākavum ceyyappaṭṭu mūṉṟiyum iṇikka ovvoṉṟilum tirukukaḷ amikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. taṇṭu kaḷil cila kaṭṭik kampikaḷākavum cila kuḻalkaḷākavum ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
kampi viḷakkukaḷiviṭak kāy viḷakkukaḷil vēlip pāṭukaḷ atikam. mēlē kāṇappaṭum takaḻiyum ataṉmēl kāṇap paṭum cikaramum viḷakkil vēṟṟumikaḷi kāṭṭuvaṉavāka amin tirukkiṉṟaṉa.
mēṟpakutiyiltāṉ eṇṇey niṟkum takaḻiyākiya kiṇṇam irukkum.  takaḻikku mēl taṇṭiṟku nērāka irukkum uccip pakutiyē viḷakkiṉ ciṟappāṉa pakutiyākum.
intat takaḻiyilē tiri eriyum mukam - mūkku (tiri mukikaḷ) uṇṭu. inta mukam cilavaṟṟil iraṇṭum cilavaṟṟil mūṉṟum cilavaṟṟil nāṉkum cilavaṟṟil aintum cilavaṟṟil āṟumākap perukikkoṇṭē pōkiṉṟaṉa.
kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa cila kuttu viḷakkil ittakiya mukamē illi. takaḻiyil iṣṭappaṭṭa pakutiyil iṣṭappaṭṭa eṇṇuḷḷa tirikaḷi iṭṭu viḷakku erikkalām. cila kuttuviḷakkukaḷ takaḻi maṭṭum uṭiyaṉavāka, atāvatu tāmarip pūp pōṉṟa takaḻiyi uṭiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. ataṟku mēlē ēṉiya viḷakkukaḷip pōṉṟa cikaram etuvum illi.
takaḻikku mēlē uḷḷa cikaraṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟil ūcipōl kūrmiyāyum, cilavaṟṟil vāḻippūp pōṉṟa uruvam uṭiyaṉa vāyum cilavaṟṟil piṟivaṭivam amintaṉavāyum cilavaṟṟil vaṭṭavaṭivam uṭiyaṉavāyum cilavaṟṟil takaḻikku mēl cumār oṉṟari aṅkula uyaramuḷḷa vaṭṭamāṉa kampiyum ataṟku mēl kāykaḷum cantirahāramum vikkappaṭṭaviyāyum uḷḷaṉa. (paṭam 18, 19, 20, 21A varip pārkka)
cila viḷakkukaḷil eṇṇey niṟkum takaḻikkup patilāka oru mūṭi pōṭṭa vaṭṭak kalayam allatu uḻakkup pōla amikkap paṭṭut tiriyiṭuvataṟku vēṇṭiya iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu kuḻāykaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. cila viḷakkukaḷil takaḻikkuk kīḻē vaṭṭamākak kuṭi pōṉṟum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cila viḷakkukaḷil taṇṭiṉ kīḻiruntu takaḻi vari uruṇṭik kampikaḷic cuṟṟiṉāṟpōl oṉṟiṉ mēl oṉṟākap pala uruṭṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
intak kuttuviḷakkut tāṉ pūcikkuriya puṉitap poru ḷākap pōṟṟappaṭukiṟatu. itutāṉ puṟiruḷ pōkkum puṉita oḷi viḷakku eṉṟum aka iruḷ akaṟṟum aruḷ viḷakku eṉṟum pukaḻntu ēṟṟappaṭukiṟatu. illaka iruḷ ōṭṭuvatu ittiru viḷakkēyākum. vīṭṭiṉ maṅkalap poruḷākak kuttu viḷakku matikkappaṭukiṟatu. maruḷit tarum iruḷi akaṟṟi makkaḷ makiḻcci peṟa nāḷtōṟum iti vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṟārkaḷ.
nāḷtōṟum vīṭukaḷip perukki, meḻukik kōlam pōṭṭu maṇi pōṭukiṟārkaḷ. appāl viḷakkit tuppuravākat tuṭittu, naṉṟāka umiyāl viḷakki muḻukkāṭṭi maṇiyil vikkiṟārkaḷ. muṟkālattil kōmūttiram, pacuviṉ cāṇam ākiyavaṟṟāl meḻuki mākkōlam pōṭṭu viḷakki umiyāl pāl pōla viḷakkit tuṇiyāl tuṭittu nīrāṭṭi muḻukkāṭṭu vārkaḷ. ati veṇmiyāṉa tuṇiyāl tuṭittu maṇi mītēṟṟit tiruveṇṇīṟu eṭṭiṭaṅkaḷil poṭṭāka aṇivittu ataṉmēl cantaṉam, kuṅkumam, cāntu mutaliyavikaḷāl poṭ ṭiṭuvārkaḷ. appar perumāṉiṉ aruṭpāṭṭip pāṭuvārkaḷ.
appāl ney vārppar. tiriyiṭuvar. viḷakkēṟṟuvar. pūvaṇivar. tēṅkāy, paḻam, poṅkal, cuṇṭal, aval, kaṭali, pori, muntirippaḻam, vellam mutaliyavikaḷil iyaṉṟavi kaḷip paṭittu nīr koṇṭu mūṉṟu muṟi valañ cuṟṟi vilvam, tuḷaci, pōṉṟa ilikaḷiyō allatu naṟumalarkaḷil ētēṉum oṉṟiyō koṇṭu aruccippar.
paktip pāṭalkaḷip pāṭuvar. maṇi aṭippar. mēḷa tāḷaṅkaḷ aṭippatum kuḻal ūtutal pōṉṟa iṉṉicikaḷ muḻakkuvatum uṇṭu. cāmpirāṇip pukikāṭṭuvatum karppūram kāṭṭuvatum kiviḷakku kāṭṭuvatum uṇṭu. tirumuṟi pōṉṟa aruḷ nūṟkaḷiṉ tiruppāṭalkaḷip poruḷ viḷaṅkumāṟu iṉiya kuralil tāḷattōṭu pāṭuvar. ataṉ piṉ pirārttaṉi ceytu pōṟṟi! pōṟṟi!! eṉṟu kūṟi vaṇaṅki muṭippar.
cila vīṭukaḷil viḷakku vippataṟkeṉṟu taṉi māṭam amittiruppārkaḷ. nāṭōṟum naṭi peṟum nitya pūciyit tavirat tirukkārttiki, tip poṅkal, amāvāci, purṇami pōṉṟa cila nāṭkaḷil mikac ciṟappāṉa muṟiyil cit tirāṉ ṉaṅkaḷ paṭittup pūmālikaḷ cūṭṭic ciṟappup pūci nikaḻt tuvar.
vīṭukaḷil ovvoru nāḷum kuṭumpattiluḷḷavarkaḷum iḷañciṟārkaḷum viḷakkup puci ceytu viḷakku muṉ iruntu taṅkaḷiṉ paḷḷip pāṭaṅkaḷip paṭippar.
ip pūciyil viṇavarkaḷ oru vitamākavum civarkaḷ vēṟu vitamākavum pūci naṭattuvar. civarkaḷ vīṭṭil naṭattum viḷakkup pūciyil tēvāramum viṇavarkaḷ vīṭṭil naṭattum viḷakkup pūciyil tiruvāy moḻiyum peritum iṭam peṟṟirukkum.
iṉṟu eṅkaḷ nelli māvaṭṭattil civa viṇava vēṟṟumikaḷ peritum maṟintu varukiṉṟaṉa. eṅkaḷ māvaṭṭat til uḷḷa caṅkaranārāyaṇar (ariyartta mūrtti) civarkaḷum viṇavarkaḷum vaṇaṅkum camaracat teyvamāki iru piriviṉarka ḷiṭiyēyum celvākkup peṟṟu varukiṟār. itaṉ palaṉāka ippoḻutu civarkaḷum viṇavarkaḷum koḷviṉi koṭup piṉi ceytu koḷḷa ārampittirukkiṟārkaḷ.
civar vīṭṭup peṇ viṇavar vīṭṭiṟkup pōy tiruvāymoḻi pāṭi viḷakkup pūji ceykiṟāḷ. viṇavar vīṭṭup peṇ civar vīṭṭiṟkup pōṉatum tēvāram pāṭit tirunīṟaṇintu tiruviḷakkup pūci ceykiṟāḷ.
muṟkālattil civarkaḷ vīṭṭiluḷḷa viḷakkukaḷil civa ciṉṉamum viṇavarkaḷ vīṭṭiluḷḷa viḷakkukaḷil viṇava ciṉṉamum iṭam peṟṟiruntaṉa. kiṟitavarkaḷ vīṭṭiluḷḷa viḷakkukaḷil ciluviyum, mulīmkaḷ vīṭṭiluḷḷa viḷakkukaḷil piṟiyum iṭam peṟṟiruntaṉa. ippoḻutu antac camayac ciṉṉaṅkaḷ poṟitta viḷakkukaḷik kāṇpatē aritāy viṭṭatu. appaṭi eṅkāvatu oru camayac ciṉṉam poṟitta viḷakku irun tālum makkaḷ vēṟṟumi uṇarcciyiṉṟik kūcātu vaṇaṅki vaḻi paṭukiṉṟaṉar.
pala civarkaḷ vīṭṭilē tirumāluruvamum avaratu maṉivi yar uruvaṅkaḷum poṟikkappaṭṭu, āṅkila eḻuttākiya oy, yū vaṭivam pōṭṭirukkum viḷakkukaḷ kūṭa iṭam peṟṟup pūcik kuriya puṉitap poruḷākap pōṟṟappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
viḷakkuc cakuṉam
iṉṟum tamiḻarkaḷ vīṭukaḷil oru nalla kāriyam, atāvatu tirumaṇam , vīṭu kuṭi pukutal, māṭu vāṅkutal, vayal vāṅkutal pōṉṟa nalla kāriyaṅkaḷip paṟṟip pēcik koṇṭirukkum pōtu viḷakkiṉiṉṟu poṟi (cuṭar) paṟantāl naṟcakuṉam eṉṟu makiḻcciyuṟukiṟārkaḷ.
tamiḻnāṭṭil maṭṭumiṉṟi intiyā muḻuvatilum iṉṟu 
kūṭa uṇavu uṇṇum poḻutō, tirumaṇa vicayamāy pēcik koṇṭirukkum poḻutō, naṟkarumaṅkaḷi nāṭi niṟkum poḻutō, viḷakkuk kuḷirntu (aṇintu) viṭṭāl keṭṭa cakuṉamākak karutukiṟārkaḷ. antak kāriyam tīmiyāy muṭiyum eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟārkaḷ.
paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭil oru vīraṉ pōril kāyamuṟṟu vīṭṭiṟku vantāṉ. avaṉatu maṉivi iravil kāyattukku maruntu vittuk kaṭṭi avaṉik kavaṉittu vantāḷ. avaḷukkup pala keṭṭa nimittaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. avikaḷil oṉṟu viḷakku aṇintatu. avaḷ ati taṉakku varappōkum payaṅkaramāṉa viḷivukaḷiṉ muṉṉaṟivippāka eṇṇiṉāḷ. taṉ kaṇavaṉ kuṇappaṭṭu eḻuvāṉ eṉpatiluḷḷa ellā nampikkikaḷiyum iḻantāḷ eṉpatit teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭic (nelli māvaṭṭattic) cērnta koṟkikku arukiluḷḷa māṟamaṅkalam (māṟōkkam) eṉṉum ūriluḷḷa nappacaliyār eṉṉum caṅka kālap pulavar piṉvarum pāṭṭil tokuttuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.
eṉṉi mārpiṟ puṇṇum veyya
naṭunāḷ vantu tumpiyum tuvikkum
neṭunakar varippiṉ viḷakku nillā	(puṟam 280)
intap pāṭṭiṟkup poruḷ kūṟiya uriyāciriyarkaḷ palaruṭiya karuttukkaḷiliruntum paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭilirunta pala maṉṉarkaḷum viḷakku aṇintu viṭāmal irukkumāṟu tam araṇmaṉiyil takka ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceytiruntārkaḷ eṉpati aṟikiṟōm. viḷakku iravum pakalum oyvu oḻivu iṉṟi vāṭāmaṇi viḷakkāy oḷi vīcuvatit taṅkaḷ veṟṟiyiṉ ciṉṉamāka avarkaḷ karuti vantatāka uṇarkiṟōm.
kāṟṟaṭittu viḷakku kuḷirntu (aṇintu)  viṭṭālum allatu vēṟukāraṇattiṉāl viḷakka aṇintu viṭṭālum maṉṉarukkup periya āpattu nērappōkiṟatu eṉṟu karutap paṭṭatu. itaṉi niccamiru viḷakku allatu nittiya viḷakku eṉṟu aḻittaṉar. itaṉ mūlam aracaṉ vāḻum iṭaṅkaḷil viḷakku iḷam peṇkaḷāl iravu muḻutum mikka kavaṉamākavum carva cākkiratiyākavum kavaṉittu varappaṭṭatu eṉpati aṟikiṟōm.
	kuṟuntoṭi muṉkik
		kūntalañ ciṟupuṟat
	tiravupakaṟ ceyyun
		tiṇpiṭi yoḷvāḷ
	viravuvarik kacciṟ
		pūṇṭa maṅkiyar
	neyyumiḻ curiyar
		neṭuntirik koḷī ik
	kiyami viḷakka
		nantutoṟu māṭṭa	(mullippāṭṭu 45 - 49.)
nimittikarkaḷ allatu nimittam pārppatil ciṟanta nipuṇarkaḷ maṉṉarkaḷiṉ vicēṣa viḷakkukaḷi eppoḻutum kaṇkāṇittu vantaṉar. viḷakkiṉ oḷiyum cuvāliyum īṭṭi nuṉipōl nērāka nimirntu eḻuntu erivatiliruntu āṭāmalum kuluṅkāmalum kāṟṟiṉāl karumpuḷḷikaḷ tōṉṟāmalum iruppatik kaṇṭāl maṉṉarkku nalvāḻvum veṟṟiyum eḻum eṉṟu tirupti aṭintu ati muṉṉaṟivippuc ceyvārkaḷ.
	`mimici yiṉṟi
		maṇiviḷakkup pōlōṅkic
	cemmiyi ṉiṉṟilaṅkum
		tīpiki - temmuṉiyuḷ
	vēliṉuṅ kōṭātu
		vēntaṉ maṉi viḷaṅkak
	kōliṉuṅ kōṭā koḻuntu	(nacciṉārkkiṉiyar virivuri)
	vaḷiturantak kaṇṇum
		varlantiriyāp poṅki
	yoḷi ciṟantōṅki
		varalāl - aḷi ciṟantu
	naṉṉeṟiyē kāṭṭum
		nalanteri kōlārkku
	veṉṉeṟiyē kāṭṭum viḷakku`					(puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāli - 200)
iṉṟum tamiḻakattilum tamiḻarkaḷ vāḻum ilaṅki, parmā, malāyā ciṅkappūr, cāvakam, pōrṉiyō pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum kūṭa vīṭṭil mukkiyamāṉa kāriyaṅkaḷ pēcum poḻutu vīṭṭil eṇṇeyiṉāl eriyum tiruviḷakkiṉiṉṟu poṟi eḻuntāl ati oru nalla aṭiyāḷamāka aḷaviṭukiṉṟaṉar. tāṅkaḷ karutum kāriyaṅkaḷ nalla viḷivukaḷi uṇṭākkum eṉṟu nampukiṉ ṟārkaḷ.
inta apippirāṅkaḷ vaṭa intiya makkaḷiṭam kūṭa uṇṭu eṉṟu vaṭa intiya makkaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpuḷḷa makkaḷ kūṟukiṉ ṟārkaḷ. paṇṭuḷḷa inta nimittaṅkaḷum nampikkikaḷum tamiḻar kaḷiṭiyē āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷāka nilavi varukiṉṟaṉa. ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa!

kiḷi viḷakkukaḷ
muṟkālattil tamiḻakattil vīṭukaḷil naṭipeṟum vicēṭaṅ kaḷil vīṭṭiluḷḷa kuttuviḷakkukaḷil periya viḷakkil periya tirikaḷ pōṭṭu atikamāṉa veḷiccatti uṇṭākkiṉar. appāl periya tīvarttikaḷi upayōkikka muyaṉṟaṉar. ataṉ piṟaku kiḷi viḷakkukaḷ tuḷirkka ārampittaṉa.
nāñcil nāṭṭil vāḻippū viḷakkukaḷ eṉṟu oru vaki viḷakkukaḷ upayōkappaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. anta vāḻippū viḷakkil mikap periyavikaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭuk kōyilkaḷil upayōkappaṭuttappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟiya viḷakkukaḷ piṟa māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil kuṟivāka iruntālum nelli māvaṭṭattil kaṇicamāṉa aḷavu paraviyirukkiṉṟaṉa. anta viḷakkiṉ takaḻiyil mūkku irātu.
inta vāḻippū viḷakkil iru pakkaṅkaḷilum iru kiḷi kaḷ vittuc ceyyappaṭṭa viḷakkukaḷi nāṉ kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭattilum tiruvaṉantapurattilum pārttirukkiṟēṉ.
aḵtaṉṟit tamiḻ nāṭṭil eṅkum nāṉ pārttatē illi. āṉāl ceṉṉi aracāṅkat tol poruḷkāṭci cāliyil mūṉṟu kiḷikaḷuḷḷa oru vāḻippū viḷakkip pārttiruk kiṟēṉ. intak kiḷi viḷakkiyum itupōṉṟa kiḷi viḷakku kaḷiyum vīṭṭu viḷakkil cērttuk kaṇakkiṭuvatā? allatu kōyil viḷakkil cērttuk kaṇakkiṭuvatā? eṉṟu eṉakkup pulappaṭavilli.
ivi vīṭṭiṟkum kōyilukkum ēṟṟaviyākac camikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl ivaṟṟik kōyilkaḷil eppoḻutum ēṟṟalām. vīṭṭil eppoḻutum ēṟṟavō, ellōrkkum vāṅkavō vacatippaṭātu.
āṉāl itu vīṭukaḷil vicēṣa kālaṅkaḷil ēṟṟuvataṟku ēṟṟatāka amintirukkiṟatu. ākiyāl iti vicēṣa viḷakku eṉṟu kūṟiṉāl mikap poruttamāṉatāka irukkum.
inta viḷakkil periyavikaḷ āyirak kaṇakkāṉa rūpāykaḷ peṟumatiyāṉavikaḷ.
cumār aintaṭi uyarattiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa viḷakkukaḷ kūṭa irukkiṉṟaṉa. itit tūkkuvatum oru iṭattiliruntu maṟṟōr iṭattiṟku māṟṟuvatum mikak kaṣṭamāka irukkum. atōṭu intak kiḷi viḷakkukaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil eṇṇikkiyil mikak kuṟivākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. miṉ viḷakkukaḷ perukiyatum itaṉ upayōkamum kuṟintu viṭṭatu.
cōṭaca aṉṉac caṅkilik kiḷi viḷakku
it tiruviḷakkutāṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa viḷakkukaḷil mikap periyatākum. kiḷi viḷakkukaḷilum itutāṉ mikap peritu. iti eṉatu māṇavarum kōyiṟ pūci ceypavarumāṉa cāttaṉ kuḷam tiru. cu. kilācap paṭṭar, cōṭaca aṉṉaccaṅkilik kiḷi viḷakku eṉṟār. oru ciṟpi maṅkala maṇa viḷakku eṉṟār. teṉkāci yiluḷḷa eṉatu mulim naṇpar itu mulūtu viḷakku eṉṟār.
inta māperum kiḷi viḷakku, pittaḷiyāl vārkkap paṭukiṟatu. itu ciṟanta kiḷi viḷakkiṟku oru nalla eṭuttuk kāṭṭākat tikaḻkiṟatu.
itu aintaṭi uyarattiṟku mēl irukkum. itaṉ kiḷikaḷ naṭut taṇṭiliruntu ovvoru pakkattilum oṉṟari aṭi nīṇṭirukkum. eṉavē akalam mūṉṟaṭikkumēl irukkum. itaṉ niṟi teriyavilli.
itaṉ pātam, mikak kaṉamāka mēṟtaṇṭiyum kiḷikaḷiyum tāṅkat takkatāy amikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. naṭut taṇṭu mūṉṟu pirivāka irukkiṟatu. avaṟṟit taṉit taṉiyē kaḻaṟṟi viṭa muṭiyum. ovvoru pakutiyiyum palamāka iṇippataṟkēṟṟa nīṇṭa tirukukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
ovvoru uṭpirivilum eṭṭu aḻakiya vaḷinta curuḷkaḷ uṇṭu. ovvoru curuḷum aintu akalkaḷit tāṅkik koṇ ṭirukkiṟatu. atiṉiṉṟu caṅkiliyil toṅkum akal viḷakkukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. avi ovvoṉṟilum aintu tirikaḷ pōṭṭu viḷakkēṟṟum aintu mūkkukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
ittirumaṇa viḷakkil kāṇappaṭum mūṉṟu taṭṭukaḷil uḷḷa curuḷkaḷil ovvoṉṟiṟkum 70 tirikaḷāka mottam 217 tirikaḷ eriyum māperum veḷiccattiyuṭiya oppaṟṟa periya viḷakkāka itu oḷirkiṟatu.
itaṉ ucciyil aṉṉattiṉ uṭali uṭiya tirumakaḷiṉ uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ittakiya viḷakkic ceytu muṭikka iṉṟu irāṇṭāyiram rūpāykku mēṟcellum eṉṟa eṇṇu kiṟēṉ. ovvoru viḷakkum ovvoru takaḻiyit tāṅkik koṇṭu iṟutiyil aṉṉa laṭcumiyil pōy muṭikiṟatu. itu pala makkaḷ kūṭip paṭikkumpōtu maṇaviḻāviṉ pōtum upayōkip pataṟku ēṟṟatu. ittakiya tiruviḷakkut tamiḻnāṭṭil oṉṟiraṇṭu tāṉ irukkum eṉṟu eṇṇukiṟēṉ. vēṟu nāṭṭil kiṭiyavē kiṭiyātu. (paṭam 22 pārkka)
tiruppatik kiḷi viḷakku
iṅku kāṇappaṭuvatu maṟṟum oru kiḷi viḷakkākum. ik kiḷi viḷakku mikac ciṟanta vēlippāṭuṭiyatu eṉṟu kūṟa muṭiyāviṭṭālum mika maṭṭamāṉatu eṉṟu otukkit taḷḷi viṭuvataṟkuriyatu alla.
ik kiḷiviḷakku tiruppatik kōyilukku uriyatu. itu aintaṭi uyaramuḷḷatu. itaṉ mattiyil uḷḷa tūṇ marattiṉ naṭut taṇṭippōl kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. viḷakkiṉ ēṉiya kiḷikaḷ marattiṉ kiḷikaḷ pōl naṭuttaṇṭuṭaṉ iṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. (paṭam 23 pārkka)
kiḷikaḷiṉ ovvoru nuṉiyilum kiṇṇam pōṉṟu viḷaṅkum takaḻi uṇṭu. kiḷit taṇṭukaḷil ilikaḷ pōṉṟa pala eḻil uruvaṅkaḷum tīṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. taṇṭiṉ ucciyil aḻakiya aṉṉap paṟaviyiṉ uruvam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
aṉṉam paṇṭik kālattil tamiḻakattil vārtteṭukkap paṭṭa ōr uruvattip pōṉṟu ciṟpac ciṟappuṭiyatākat tikaḻkiṟatu.
muṟkālattil vīṭukaḷil kūṭa aṉṉa viḷakkukaḷ vikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. ippoḻutu vīṭukaḷil aṉṉa viḷakkukaḷ vikkum vaḻakkam aṭiyōṭu akaṟṟappaṭṭu viṭṭatu. aṉṉam vikkum viḷakkukaḷ piramaṉ kōyilukkē uriyatu. tamiḻnāṭṭilē pirama ṉukkuk kōyil illātatāl aṉṉa viḷakkukaḷ ellāk  kōyi lilum iṭam peṟṟu viṭṭaṉa eṉṟu karuta iṭam uṇṭu.
aṉṉa viḷakkukaḷ tirumakaḷ kōyililō, tirumakaḷ arukilō iruppatu mikap poruttamāṉatākum.
murukaṉ kōyiliṉ ucciyil mayil uruvam vitta viḷakku kaḷē vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kōḻi uruvam uḷḷa viḷakkukaḷum vikkalām.
cila iṭaṅkaḷil kiḷi uruvamuḷḷa viḷakkukaḷ vikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. kiḷi ellāp peṇkaḷukkum viruppamāṉa paṟaviyāy iruppatāl ellāt tēvikaḷum kiḷiyi virumpuvārkaḷ eṉṟa eṇṇattil ellāk kōyililum kiḷi viḷakkukaḷ iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu eṇṇukiṟēṉ.
aṉṉattiṉ ciṟpa aḻaki matittu ellāk kōyilkaḷilum aṉṉa viḷakkip piṭiyamuṭaṉ vittirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamuṇṭu.
cila vaṭa intiya viḷakku ārāyccikkārarkaḷ namatu kuttu viḷakkukaḷ marattip pōṉṟu nērāṉa naṭut taṇṭiliruntu piriyum pala kiḷikaḷik koṇṭu ovvoru kiḷiyiṉ nuṉiyilum ōr akali ētti niṟkum viḷakkukaḷākac ceyyappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
mutaṉ mutalil kiviḷakkukaḷ arumpiṉa. appāl makkaḷ ēṭukaḷip paṭippataṟkō taṅkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷic ceyvataṟkō kiviḷakku uyarattil iruntāl nallatu eṉṟu eṇṇiyirukka lām. eṉavē kiviḷakki or uyaramāṉa kampilō, kallilō vittirukkalām.
appāl ak kiviḷakki nīṇṭa kampil iṇittirukka lām. oru kampiṉ mītu kiviḷakku vittu avveḷiccatti upayōkikkum avaciyam atikam ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām.
ataṉ piṟaku kuttuviḷakku ēṟpaṭṭirukkalām. kuttuviḷakkut tōṉṟi nīṇṭa kālattiṟkup piṉṉar, tiruviḻā, tirumaṇam pōṉṟa kālaṅkaḷil atikamāṉa veḷiccam tēvippaṭṭatāl kuttu viḷakkil cila kiḷikaḷ vittup periya kiḷi viḷakkukaḷ uruvākkiyirukkalām.
intak kiḷi viḷakkukaḷ nāḷā vaṭṭattil nūṟṟukkaṇak kāṉa kiḷikaḷiyum akalkaḷiyum uṭiya māperum kiḷi viḷakkākap pariṇamittirukkalām.
piṉṉar, ciṟpikaḷ tam kaṟpaṉit tiṟattāl palvēṟu vitamāka aḻakupaṭuttiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ovvoru akaliṉ mītum aṉṉam vittirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
kiḷikaḷiṉ mītuḷḷa akaliṉiṉṟu caṅkilikaḷi amittu ataṉ kīḻ akal viḷakkukaḷit toṅkaviṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.
intak kiḷi viḷakkukaḷil oru taṭṭu, iru taṭṭukaḷ mut taṭṭukaḷ nāṟṟaṭṭukaḷ amittu ovvoru taṭṭilum mūṉṟu nālu kiḷikaḷi amittu ovvoru kiḷikkum pala akal kaḷip poruttiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
intak kiḷiviḷakkukaḷil aṭukkaṭukkāyp pala kiḷikaḷi yum ovvōr kiḷiyilum pala pirivukaḷi amittu ovvor akalilum pala tirikaḷ irukkum paṭiyāka amittirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
oru viḷakkil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tirikaḷ cuṭar viṭṭu jekajōtiyāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. itit tirumaṇa viḷakku eṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.
ittakiya viḷakkukaḷ vaṭanāṭṭilum uṇṭu. itu attuṇi aḻakum, ciṟappum, kiḷikaḷum uṭiyataṉṟu. itaṉi avarkaḷ viruṭca viḷakku eṉṟa kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. vaṭa moḻiyil itu tīpa virukṣa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. itaṟkum namatu tamiḻ nāṭṭut tirumaṇa viḷakkiṟkum eḷḷaḷavum campantam kiṭiyātu.
mūṉṟaṭukku kiṉṉarik kiḷiviḷakku
tirumaṇa viḷakkip pōṉṟu intak kiṉṉari viḷakkum mikap periya viḷakkēyākum. aḻakilum ciṟpac ciṟappilum ataṟku oppāṉatēyākum. atip pōl ituvum mūṉṟu aṭukku viḷakkukaḷiyuṭiyatēyākum.
itil tirumaṇa viḷakkip pōṉṟu toṅkum caṅkili viḷakkukaḷ iṇikkappaṭavilli. eṉavē iti viṭa tirumaṇa viḷakkil atikamāṉa veḷiccattip peṟa muṭiyum.
vēṭikkiyākak kūṟuvatāṉāl iraṇṭum tirumaṇa viḷakkē yākum. āṉāl tirumaṇa viḷakku āṇviḷakku, kiṉṉari viḷakku peṇ viḷakku eṉṟu kūṟi viṭalām. tōṟṟattil itu tirumaṇa viḷakkiṉum kampīramākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. uyarattil kūṭa ativiṭa itu ciṟitāvatu uyaramākavē irukkum.
iv viḷakkup pittaḷi allatu veṇkalattāl vārkkap paṭṭatākum. itu cumār 6 aṭi uyaram irukkum. pātam vaṭṭa mākavum uṟutiyākavum ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
naṭut taṇṭu mika aḻakāka vārkkappaṭṭu eḻil peṟak kaṭiyappaṭirukkiṟatu.
ucciyil 9 mukamuḷḷa takaḻiyum, mēlē aṉṉattiṉ uṭalum maṉita mukamum uḷḷa oru uruvam poṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
iti aṉṉa laṭcumi eṉṟum kiṉṉari eṉṟum kūṟuki ṟārkaḷ. oṉṟiṉ mītu oṉṟāka mūṉṟu aṭukkukaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru aṭukkilum āṟu āṟākap patiṉeṭṭuk kiḷi kaḷum ovvoru akalāka 18 akal viḷakkukaḷum mutal iraṇṭu taṭṭilum ovvoru takaḻikkum  mēlē naṭu miyamāka oru aṉṉam vītam 18 aṉṉaṅkaḷum aḻakuṟap polikiṉṟaṉa. (paṭam 24 pārkka)
mēlē oru taṭṭiluḷḷa āṟu kiḷikaḷil ovvoru cantira hāramāka 6 cantirahāram kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṉṉam kāṇappaṭa villi. aṉṉattiṟkup patil cantirahāram kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
mūṉṟāvatu taṭṭil ēṉ aṉṉattiṟku iṭam aḷikkappaṭa villi eṉpatu pulappaṭavilli. ivviḷakkil vēlippāṭukaḷ mika naṉṟākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟpac ciṟappu pōṟṟaṟkuriya muṟiyil tiṟampaṭac ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
tamiḻakattil uḷḷa iv viḷakku mikapperiya viḷakku eṉpatu maṭṭumalla aḻakiya viḷakku eṉṟum tiriyamākak kūṟamuṭiyum.
aṉṉa vaṭiva laṭcumi viḷakku
aṉṉa vaṭiva laṭcumi viḷakku mika nuṇṇiya vēlip pāṭukaḷ niṟintatu; itu iṅku kāṭṭappaṭṭirukkum ēṉiya viḷakkukaḷiviṭa mikak kavaṉamākac ceyyappaṭṭa tiruviḷak kākum. (paṭam 25 pārkka)
namatu ciṟpikaḷiṉ ciṟanta vēlippāṭṭi eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvataṟku ittakiya viḷakkukaḷ oru nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ittakiya viḷakkukaḷil oṉṟu ceṉṉit tol poruṭkāṭci cāliyilum irukkiṟatu.
curuṇṭu mūṉṟu kiḷikaḷip pōṉṟu amikkappaṭṭu aḻakiya kālkaḷāl aṭippākam (pātam) tāṅkik koṇṭu iruppatu pōl vaḷarkkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. inta aṭippakutiyākiya pātattiṉiṉṟu iru kiḷikaḷ eḻuntu ovvoṉṟiṉ mītum eḻil tatumpum iḷam peṇ oru kiyil akal viḷakkip piṭittu niṟpatu pōl kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
pātattiṉ mēl naṭuvil iraṇṭu iṟakkikaḷiyuṭiya āṇ ciṅkam taṉ mutukiṉ mītu viḷakkiṉ naṭuttaṇṭit tāṅkik koṇṭu niṟpatu pōl irukkiṟatu. naṭuttaṇṭiṉ mēlē takaḻi kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
naṭut taṇṭiṉ mēl, piṉ pakkattiluḷḷa iru kālkaḷi ūṉṟik koṇṭu takaḻiyic ciṅkaṅkaḷ tāṅki niṟpatu pōl kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
takaḻiyiṉ naṭuviliruntu oru taṇṭu uyarntu celkiṟatu. mēlē aṉṉam  aḻakoḷirak kempīramākak kāṭci aḷikkiṟatu. aṉṉattiṉ mītu patuma pīṭattilē tirumakaḷ oyyāramāka uṭkārntu koṇṭirukkiṟāḷ.
itip pōṉṟa tiruviḷakkukaḷ pala vakiyiṉum ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṟa, mika mukkiyattuvam peṟṟa viḷakkāka matikkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. itu, tamiḻar pāṇiyil aṇi ceyyappaṭṭum maṟumalarcci peṟṟum  eḻunta ariya putu viḷakkākum.
itaṉi mika naṉṟāka, iyaṉṟa aḷavu aṉutāpa uṇarcci yōṭu alaci ārāyalām. ēṉ? ivviḷakkiṉ muḻu vaṭivamum tiruptiyaṟṟum, uvappaṟṟum irukkiṟatu eṉpatik kaṇṭupiṭikka lām.
aḻaku, oru poruḷiṉ uyarnta taṉmiyip poṟuttu amivatilli. entac cikkalāṉa paṇiyilum oru pakutikku maṟṟoru pakuti takutiyum poruttamum uṭiyatāka iruppati ārāyntē atu atika aḻaku uṭiyatu eṉṟu tīrmāṉikkappaṭa vēṇṭum. inta mūla cūttiram iṅku muḻuvatum kiyāḷappaṭṭu varukiṟatu. nām ataṉ pakutikaḷip paṟṟi avḷavākak kuṟṟam kāṇa muṭiyavilli; āṉāl atil oṉṟukku oṉṟuḷḷa toṭarpu nalla poruttam illātiruttal, takutik kuṟivu ākiyavikaḷic collittāṉ āka vēṇṭum.
iṅku kāṭṭappaṭṭa viḷakkup paṭattip pōṉṟu kālkaḷ uṟutiyuṭiyavikaḷāka iruntāl namakku evvita āṭcēpamum illi. atōṭu aṭiyil irukkum pātat taṭṭu mika mukkiyamāṉa tākum. atiltāṉ viḷakkiṉ pakuti aṉittum poruntiyiruk kiṉṟaṉa. naṭut taṇṭu ataṉ mītē oru ciṟitum cāyāmal ceṅkuttāy nimirntu niṟkiṟatu. ēṉiya viḷakkiṉ pātaṅkaḷiluḷḷa kālkaḷi viṭa ik kālkaḷ mikap poruttak kuṟivāka irukkiṉṟatu eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
intat tiruviḷakkil, ciṅkam, naṭuttaṇṭu, takaḻi ākiyavi kaḷi oru ellikkuṭpaṭutti amittatu ciṟpikaḷukku oru ciramamāṉa vēliyākum.
it tiruviḷakkiṉ taṇṭiyum takaḻiyiyum ciṅkam tāṅkik koṇṭiruppatu mika atikamāṉa vēliyākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
pātattōṭu iṇikkappaṭṭirukkum ciṅka uruvam pārvikku mika uṟutiyākavum mika mika vicirāntiyāṉatākavum kāṇappaṭa vēṇṭum. mēlum pātattiṉiṉṟu uyarntu, taṉ mītu ēṟṟappaṭṭa pārattit tāṅkum uṟutiyāṉa nili uṭiyatāyum irukka vēṇṭum. atōṭu ataṉ iru muṉ mukavāyk kaṭṭi kaḷum ataṉ kīḻē pōtiya aḷavu tiṟantum virintum kāṇappaṭa vēṇṭum.
ciṅkattiṉ mutukiliruntu eḻuntuḷḷa viḷakkut taṇṭu mēlum kīḻum irukkum ēṉiya pakutikaḷōṭu oppiṭṭup pārttāl poruttamaṟṟatāyum kuṭṭiyāṉatāyum kāṇappaṭu kiṟatu. atu viḷakkiṉ iṇippil oru pakutiyāka irukkiṟatu. āṉāl ataṉ niliyum upayōkamum mikap periya mukkiyattum vāyntavikaḷāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. ciṅkattiṉ mutukiṉ mītu iṇip paṭṭirukkum taṇṭu atikamāka ataṉ mītu nili nāṭṭappaṭṭuc ciṅkattiṉ iṟakkikaḷāl maṟikkap paṭṭiruntāl mika naṉṟāka iruntirukkum.
viḷakkiṉ aṭutta uṟuppāṉa takaḻi, kuṟaḷ vaṭivamākac ciṅkat tiṉ uruvattōṭu iṇintu ataṉ kīḻē peritum puṭittuk koṇṭirukkiṟatu eṉṟālum ataṉuṭiya mikac ciṟanta payaṉ uṟutiyāka ataṟku aṉukūlamāka irukkiṟatu.
takaḻikku mēl, uyarattil taṉatu vāli uyarttik koṇṭu oyyāramāka niṟkum ōtimap paṟavi mīṇṭum takaḻiyil irukkum eṇṇeyi uṟiñca niṟkiṟatu.
putiya tōṟṟattil amikkappaṭṭa it tiruviḷakku 5-vatu eṇ iṭappaṭṭa viḷakki muṉ mātiriyāka vittu vārttirun tāl, aḻaku poliya viḷaṅki irukkum. ciṟpikaḷiṉ uḷḷattik kavarvatāy kāṭci aḷittirukkum.
tāmari malark kiḷiviḷakku
tāmari malar viḷakku eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum kiḷi viḷakkukaḷ āṭamparamāka illāviṭṭālum, ēṉiya kiḷi viḷakkukaḷip pōl periyaṉavāka illāviṭṭālum kaccitamāka irukkum.
pātam taṇṭiyum kiḷikaḷiyum tāṅkac caktiyuṭiyatākap peritāka amippaṭṭirukkiṟatu.  uyaram nālari aṭi irukkum. oṉṟiṉ mēl oṉṟāka mūṉṟu aṭukkākak kiḷikaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
aṭittaṭṭil mutaṉ mutalāka 6 kiḷikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ov voru kiḷiyilum tāmarip pūppōl kiṇṇaṅkaḷ eṇṇey vārppataṟkāka amikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
naṭuvil muṭṭiyip pōṉṟu pokuṭu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
ivvāṟu mūṉṟu taṭṭilum 18 kiḷikaḷukkum 18 takaḻikaḷum aintu mukaṅkaḷumāka 120 tiri mukikaḷ uṇṭu.
itu 120 tirikaḷ pōṭṭu eriyat takkaṉavāka irukkiṟatu.
mēlē maṟṟa viḷakkukaḷukku iruppatupōl takaḻi illi. aṉṉap paṟavi maṭṭum aḻakāka amarntu koṇṭirukkiṟatu. tōṟṟattil ivviḷakku kaṟpūrat taṭṭu viḷakkip pōl kāṭci aḷikkiṟatu.
inta viḷakkum oru apūrvamāṉa viḷakkēyākum. iḵtaṉṟi 12 kiḷikaḷum ovvoru kiḷikkum oru takaḻiyum takaḻiyiṉ naṭuvil oru ciṟiya aṉṉamum uḷḷaviyākavē vārkkappaṭṭa pala pittaḷi, veṇkalak kiḷi viḷakkukaḷum uṇṭu. ivikaḷ eṇṇikkiyil mika mikak kuṟivu. (paṭam 26 pārkka)
iḵtaṉṟi maraviḷakku (viruṭca viḷakku) eṉṟu oru vaki viḷakku uṇṭu. itu pittaḷiyāl ceyyappaṭṭatu. kiṭṭattaṭṭa nālari aṭi uyaramuḷḷatu.
itu tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷakkukaḷil mikavum kavarcci tarakkūṭiya viḷakkākum. itaṉ taṇṭiyum amippiyum koṇṭu vaṭa moḻi nūlkaḷil `tīpa virukṣa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatāl itu, iv viḷakkiṟku oru nalla eṭuttukkāṭṭāka irukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭantarukiṟatu. itu turhām mātā kōyilil irukkum `kaṉṉiyiṉ viḷakku eṉṟa oru piracitti peṟṟa viḷakki niṉip pūṭṭukiṟatu.
naṭut taṇṭiṉiṉṟu pirintuḷḷa kiḷikaḷiṉ iṟutiyil variciyāka amintirukkum ciṟu kiṇṇaṅkaḷ 7 vitamāṉa pantiyāka 108 viḷakkiṟkuriya tirikaḷ iṭumāṟu amikkappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
aṭiyil uḷḷa pātattil nāṉku ciṟu pāmpukaḷ viḷakku kaḷukkuk kāvaṟkārarkaḷāka niṟpatupōl kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pāmpukaḷ civaperumāṉuṭiya ciṉṉamākak karutap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ittakiya viḷakkukaḷil oṉṟu civapaktarāl tiruvāṅkūric cērnta valippaḷḷic civaṉ kōyilukkuk kāṇikkiyāka arppaṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ittakiya viḷakkukaḷ iṉṟu tamiḻ nāṭṭil mika apūrvamākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
vaṅkak kali mētiyum rūpam eṉṟa āṅkilat tiṅkaḷ kali itaḻi 40 āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka naṭatti varupavarum viḷakku ārāycciyil tali ciṟanta pērāciriyarumāṉa tiru.ō.ci. kaṅkūli avarkaḷ tamatu taḷḷāta mutumip paruvattilum, kali ārāyc ciyit toṭarntu viṭātu āṟṟivarum periyārāvar. avar kaṇṭa viḷakkukaḷil, avaratu uḷḷattik kavarnta viḷakkukaḷ cila. avikaḷil inta viruṭca viḷakku oṉṟeṉa moḻikiṉṟār.
iṅkilāntu vari inta viḷakkiṉ pukaḻip paṟicāṟṟi yuḷḷār aṟiñar kaṅkūli.
ittakiya viruṭca tīpaṅkaḷ hitarāpāttilum uṇṭu. atu nām muṉṉar kāṭṭiya tirumaṇa viḷakkip pōlavē kāṇap paṭukiṟatu. itaṉ pīṭam kal allatu marattilē ceyyap paṭṭiruk kiṟatu. itu tamiḻakattil kāṇappaṭum viḷakkukaḷukku oppākak kūṟak kūṭiyataṉṟu.
iti oppiṭṭup pārkkum poḻututāṉ tamiḻakac ciṟpikaḷ aṟivum āṟṟalum, aḻaki aṟputamāka vaṭittuk kāṭṭum ariya karaṅkaḷiṉ makōṉṉatamāṉa tiṟaṉum naṉku pulaṉākiṟatu. ittakiya viḷakkukaḷil oṉṟu intiya aracāṅka veḷiyīṭākiya `intiya viḷakkukaḷ eṉṟa nūlil kūṭa iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.

tūkku viḷakkukaḷ
kirēkka nāṭṭuk kōyilkaḷil māli vēḷiyil viḷak kēṟṟappaṭṭu iravu muḻuvatum viḷakkukaḷ erivataṟkup pōtumāṉa eṇṇey nirappappaṭṭiruntatu eṉṟu herō ṭōṭṭa eṉṟa kirēkka varalāṟṟu āciriyar eḻutukiṉṟār.
ekiptil aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē makkaḷ viḷak kīṭṭu viḻā koṇṭāṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. aṅku veṇkalam, cempu mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa viḷakkukaḷ iruntaṉavām.
iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē urōm nāṭṭil oru peṇ oru kiyik kīḻē toṅkap pōṭṭuk koṇṭu oru kiyi mēlē uyarttik kiyil oru viḷakkip piṭittuk koṇ ṭiruppatu pōl ōr ulōka viḷakku iruntatām.
aṉṉa viḷakkum puṟā viḷakkum aṅku iruntatākavum kūṟu kiṟārkaḷ. kāḷiyiṉ tali, ciṅkamukam pōṉṟa paṟpala vēṭikki yāṉa vaṭivaṅkaḷil viḷakkukaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
cumēriyā, acīriyā, kaltēyā (cālṭiyā) mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil putiporuḷ ārāycciyāḷarkaḷāl akaḻnteṭukkappaṭṭa kaḷimaṇ viḷakkukaḷ oṉpatāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muntiṉavi kaḷāka irukkalām eṉṟu apippirāyappaṭu kiṉṟārkaḷ.
mokañcatārōvil aiyāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ cuṭu maṇ viḷakkukaḷ, ulōka viḷakkukaḷ mutaliyavikaḷi upayōkappaṭuttiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
poṉiṣiyarkaḷ eṇṇey viḷakkukaḷi maṭṭumalla, meḻukuvartti viḷakkukaḷiyum ki.pi. 400ām āṇṭil kaṇṭu piṭittuḷḷārkaḷ.
tamiḻarkaḷ cumār 4000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē poṉi ṣiyarkaḷōṭu vaṇikat toṭarpu koḷḷa ārampittu viṭṭaṉar; 2000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē urōmarkaḷ, kirēkkarkaḷ, arēpi yarkaḷ pōṉṟa ayal nāṭṭārōṭu vaṇikat toṭarpukaḷum araciyal toṭarpukaḷum koṇṭiruntārkaḷ.
1000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē īḻam, māppāḷam, ciṅkappūr, maliyam, cāvakam, cumattirā, tāylāntu, kampōṭiyā mutaliya veḷināṭukaḷuṭaṉ tamiḻarkaḷ vaṇikam naṭatti vantaṉar.
veḷināṭṭuk kappalkaḷ pala, aṇi aṇiyāyt tamiḻ nāṭṭut tuṟimukaṅkaḷil vantu kuvintu taṅkaḷ nāṭṭiliruntu koṇṭu vanta paṇṭaṅkaḷi iṟakki iṅkuḷḷa muttukkaḷ, cantaṉak kaṭṭi, miḷaku, āṭṭu rōmam, ēlam, kirāmpu, karuvāp paṭṭi, mayiliṟakukaḷ melliya tuṇikaḷ pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷi ēṟṟik koṇṭu pōyiṉa. itaṉāl ērāḷamāṉa poṟ kaṭṭikaḷum poṟ kācukaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭil vantu kuviyalāyiṉa. anniya nāṭṭut toṭarpiṉāl tamiḻarkaḷiṉ moḻiyum kalikaḷum maṟumalarcciyuṟṟaṉa.
ki.mu. 20ām āṇṭil pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil pēraracu celutti vanta mutukuṭumip peruvaḻuti eṉṉum pāṇṭiyaṉ urōmā puriyi āṇṭu vanta akaṭa cīsar eṉṉum maṉṉaṉiṭam taṉ araca tūtari aṉuppiṉāṉ. pāṇṭiyanāṭṭu aracatūtar, pāṇṭiyaṉ urōmac cakkaravarttikkup paricāka aḷitta pala ariya poruḷkaḷi avariṭam aḷittār. avar kaṇṭu kaḷittār.
urōmāpuri aracar pāṇṭiya nāṭṭut tūtari varavēṟṟu upacarittu avarōṭu vaṇika oppantam purintu koṇṭatōṭu pāṇṭiya aracarukkup pala ariya poruḷkaḷip paricāka aḷit tār eṉṟu ṭirāpōṉ eṉṟa varalāṟṟu āciriyar kuṟippiṭu kiṉṟār.
koṟki muttu, potikic cantaṉam, tuṟiyūrttuṇi, paḻaṉimayiliṟaku, maturi viḷakku iṉṉōraṉṉa poruṭkaḷ pala yavaṉanāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ uḷḷattip peritum kavarntaṉa. itaṉ paya ṉāka ayal nāṭṭiliruntu poṟkācukaḷ kōṭi kōṭiyāy tami ḻakattil vantu kuvintaṉa. ayal nāṭṭu makkaḷōṭu arumperum naṭpu vaḷarntatu.
pāṇṭiya nāṭṭup paṭiyil pala yavaṉa vīrarkaḷ paṇi yāṟṟiṉar. pāṇṭiya maṉṉariṉ meykkāppāḷarkaḷākap pala urōma nāṭṭup peṇkaḷ vēli pārttu vantaṉar.
maturi yarukilum koṟkiyilum kāvirippūm paṭṭi ṉattilum muciṟiyilum yavaṉarkaḷ palar kuṭiyēṟiṉar. ippaṭṭiṉaṅ kaḷil pala yavaṉar vītikaḷ iruntu vantaṉa.
iṉṟu kūṭa avarkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiyirunta pakutikaḷil ērāḷa māṉa urōm nāṭṭu, arapiya nāṭṭuk kācukaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa.
ki.pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭuvari tamiḻakattōṭu urōmarkaḷ toṭarpu atikamāka iruntataṟku annāṭṭuk kācukaḷ iṉṟum cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟaṉa. ki.pi. iraṇṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu vari cōḻarkaḷiṉ talinakarāy irunta kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattiṟku arukēyuḷḷa kāviriyāṟṟiṉ vaṭakariyil kirēkkarkaḷ kuṭi yiruntu vantaṉar. inta varalāṟṟi vaṅkāḷattup pēraṟiñarum varalāṟṟu āciriyarumāṉa tiru. irātā kumutmukarji eṉpavar intiyak kappal toḻil eṉṟa nūlil naṉku vivarittuḷḷār. atu varumāṟu
paṭṭiṉam iru pakutikaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntatu. kaṭaṟkariyi aṭuttirunta oru pakutikku maruvūrp pākkam eṉṟu peyar. maruvūrkpākkattiṉ kaṭaṟkariyarukiluḷḷa peruvaḻic cāliyiṉ irumaruṅkilum makkaḷ naṭantu cellattakka uyarnta taḷaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kaṭaṟkari arukil paṇṭakacālikaḷum māḷikikaḷum iruntaṉa. paṇṭaṅkaḷ kappali viṭṭu iṟaṅki yatum paṇṭakacāliyil iṟakki vikkappaṭum. arukē yavaṉa vaṇikarkaḷiṉ illaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. viṟpaṉikkāka yavaṉa nāṭu kaḷiliruntu arumperum paṇṭaṅkaḷellām iṟakkumati ceyyap paṭṭaṉa! iṅku palvēṟu moḻikaḷip pēcum, kaṭal kaṭantu vantuḷḷa ayalnāṭṭu vaṇikarkaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷum uḷḷaṉa.
cilappatikāram, nelnalvāṭi, maṇimēkali pōṉṟa paḻan tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kuṟippiṭum viḷakkukaḷ iṅku kuṭiyēṟiya urōmarkaḷ, kirēkkarkaḷ, poṉiciyarkaḷ pōṉṟavarkaḷāl tamiḻakattiṟkuk koṇṭu varappaṭṭu iṅku viṟpaṉi ceyyappaṭ ṭirukkalām. allatu maṉṉarkaḷukkum, māperum vaṇikar kaḷukkum paricāka aḷikkappaṭṭu irukkalām. atik kaṇṭa tamiḻnāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷ avviḷakkik kaṇṭu atil cila māṟṟaṅ kaḷuṭaṉ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ paṇpukkēṟṟavāṟu orucila tiruviḷakku kaḷi uruvākki irukkalām eṉṟu cila aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉ ṟārkaḷ.
appāl avviḷakkiṉ aḻakum ciṟpac ciṟappum makkaḷ uḷḷattik kavaravē avarkaḷ ati kōyilkaḷukku naṉkoṭi yākak koṭuttirukkalām eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu.
kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ eṉṉum cōḻa maṉṉaṉ kālattil viḷakkukaḷ mutaṉmutalākat tiruviḻāk kālaṅkaḷil maṭṭum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭu vantatāka aṟikiṟōm. itu mataccārpāṉa caṭaṅkukaḷukku ārampakālattil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭavilli! appāl tamiḻ nāṭṭil, iṉṟu tīpalaṭcumi viḷakku eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum pāvi viḷakkukaḷic ceytu camayac caṭaṅkukaḷil iṭam peṟac ceytaṉar.
urōmarkaḷ viḷakkukaḷi īmac caṭaṅkiṟku uriya poru ḷākavum teyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭuvataṟkāṉa poruḷākavum payaṉ paṭutti vantaṉar.
muṟkālattil kirēkkarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kōyilkaḷukku viḷakku kaḷik kāṇikkiyāka aḷikkum vaḻakkattik koṇṭiruntaṉar.
tīpa nāṭṭik kippaṟṟiya piṉṉar cim eṉṟa iṭatti luḷḷa appalō eṉṟa teyvattiṟku makā alekcāntar eṉṉum kirēkka maṉṉaṉ oru toṅkum viḷakkik kāṇikkiyāka aḷittāṉ. atip paṟṟip piḷiṉi eṉṉum varalāṟṟu āciriyar piṉvarumāṟu kūṟukiṟār.
purātaṉak kirēkkar, urōmar viḷakkukaḷil palvēṟu vakiyāṉa viḷakkukaḷ ippoḻutu kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. avikaḷil potuvāka akkālattiya teyvaṅkaḷiṉ tiru uruvaṅkaḷ tīṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cittiraṅkaḷē perumpālum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvaki viḷakkukaḷ ellām muṟkālattil kirēkkar, poṉi ciyarkaḷ, cumēriyarkaḷ kaṇṭa kaḷimaṇ viḷakkukaḷiyē peritum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka iṅkilāntiluḷḷa tol poruḷ kāṭci cāliyil uḷḷa maṇ viḷakkukaḷip pōl irukkiṉṟaṉa.
tūkku viḷakkit toṅku viḷakku eṉṟum kūṟuvārkaḷ. iv viḷakkiṉattic cērntatē tūṇṭāmaṇi viḷakku, nantā viḷakku, nuntā viḷakku, nontā viḷakku ākiyavikaḷellām.
tūkku viḷakku kuttu viḷakkiṉ mēṟpakutiyip pōl irukkum. āṉāl ataṉ pātam akaṉṟu peritāka irukkum. mēlē takaḻi irukkum. aintu allatu ēḻu mūkku (mukam) uḷḷatāka irukkum. mēlē ucciyil toṅka viṭuvataṟkuc caṅkili iṇikkappaṭṭirukkum.
cila viḷakkukaḷil ucciyil aṉṉam vittu aṉṉattiṉ mutukil caṅkili poruttappaṭṭirukkum. intac caṅkiliyiṉ nuṉiyil vaḷiyam māṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. atiyē kūriyiṉ mēṟpakutiyil uḷḷa vaḷiyattil māṭṭiviṭuvārkaḷ.
intat tūkku viḷakkip perumpālum kaṭikaḷilum kōyilkaḷilumē upayōkappaṭuttuvārkaḷ. vīṭukaḷil perum pālum upayōkappaṭuttuvatu illi. (paṭam 30 pārkka)
iḵtaṉṟi aṭiyil pātamum ataṟku mēlē eṇṇey niṟkum akalum - eṇṇeyk kiṇṇamum uṭiyatāy akaliṉ iru pakkamum ari vaṭṭamāka uruṇṭik kampiyō tiruvāci pōṉṟa aḻakiya ciṟpa vaṭivāṉa piṟiyō irukkum.
cila viḷakkil, naṭuvē uyarnta kumiḻum ataṉ mītu aṉṉam niṉṟu koṇṭu iruppatupōṉṟa amippum irukkum. aṉṉattiṉ mutuku, piṟiyit toṭṭu niṟkum. ataṉ mītē caṅkili poṟuttappaṭṭirukkum.
tūkku viḷakkil vēṟu pala ariya ciṟpa nuṭpaṅkaḷ potin tiruppatāka eṉakkut teriyavilli. intat tūkku viḷakkiṉ kālamum muṭintuviṭṭatu. eṉṟālum cila paḻam peru nakaraṅkaḷil uḷḷa vaṇikarkaḷ iti oru maṅkalac ciṉṉamāka matittu upayōkittu varukiṟārkaḷ.
miṉ viḷakkukaḷ iruntālum tūkku viḷakki upayōkittu varuvatil oru mukkiyamāṉa viṣayam aṭaṅkiyirukkiṟatu. eṉatu nelli māvaṭṭattip pōṉṟa cila iṭaṅkaḷil atuvum maḻik kālaṅkaḷil aṭikkaṭi miṉ viḷakkukaḷ aṇintu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila kaṭikaḷilum vīṭukaḷilum aintu nimiṣam pattu nimiṣam cila camayam kāl maṇi, arimaṇi - ēṉ? oru maṇi nērattiṟku mēlum iruḷāy viṭukiṟatu. cila camayam nāḷ muḻuvatum miṉcāra viḷakku erivatilli. appoḻutu tūkkuviḷakku mika upayōkamāka irukkiṟatu.
tūkku viḷakkup pōṭṭiruppavarkaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷip pōl mikavum caṅkaṭap paṭuvatilli.
mēlum cila kaṭikaḷil cuvāmikaḷiṉ paṭaṅkaḷiṉ muṉṉar tūkku viḷakkup pōṭṭiruppatu avarkaḷukku mika upayōkamāy irukkiṟatu. miṉ viḷakku niṉṟatum meḻukuvarttikaḷit tēṭukiṟa varkaḷō maṇṇeṇṇey viḷakkukaḷit tēṭukiṟavarkaḷō illi eṉṟu colla muṭiyātu.
tūkku viḷakkup pōṉṟu viḷaṅkum tūṇṭāmaṇi viḷakkum aṭiyil tiriniṟkum mūkkum (mukamum) mēlē eṇṇey niṟkum tēṅkāy pōṉṟa kalayamum, ataṟkumēl mēṟkūriyil māṭṭat takka caṅkiliyum uṭiyatāy irukkum. (paṭam 28 pārkka)
cila viḷakkukaḷ aṭiyil kiviḷakku pōṉṟa takaḻiyum mēlē vaṭṭamāṉa - eṇṇey tēṅki niṟkum kalayamum ataṟkumēl kuttu viḷakkut taṇṭip pōl kāyum vaḷivum, kuḻivum vittu ataṟkumēl aṉṉamum vittuc ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. aṉṉat tiṉ mutukiṉ mītu vaḷiyam poruttappaṭṭirukkum. av vaḷiyattiliruntu pittaḷic caṅkilikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭuc caṅkili yiṉ nuṉiyil kokkiyum māṭṭiyirukkum.
itil palvēṟu vittiyācamāṉa viḷakkukaḷ uṇṭu. it tūṇṭāmaṇi viḷakki viṭṭu upayōkattiṟkup perumpālum upayōkippatilli. kōyilil mūlap paṭimaṅkaḷiṉ arukilum vīṭukaḷiluḷḷa pūci aṟiyilum iti atikamākap payaṉpaṭutti varukiṟārkaḷ.
jōti vaṭivamāṉa iṟivaṉi eppoḻutum oḷivaṭiva mākavē tericikka vēṇṭum eṉpatu makkaḷiṉ eṇṇam. iṟivaṉ canniti iruḷ aṭintuviṭṭāl tam vāḻvum iruḷ aṭintu viṭum; tam illamum mūtēvi aṭintu viṭum eṉpatu tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ nampikki.
mēlum cītēvi allatu tirumakaḷ eppoḻutum oḷi yirukkum iṭattiyē nāṭuvāḷ. mūtēvi iruḷ cūḻnta iṭattiyē nāṭuvāḷ eṉṟu eṇṇuvatāl iravu muḻuvatum vīṭṭil viḷak kerivatu tamiḻarkaḷ aṉirum varavēṟkum oru nalla ceyal; maṅkaḷakaramāṉa oru kāriyam.
kuḻanti piṟantatum evvaḷavu ēḻiyāṉālum oru nāṟpatu nāḷikkāvatu viṭiviḷakku vittu varuvatu iṉṟum nam nāṭṭil kaṇṭu varum campavamākum.
intat tūṇṭāmaṇi viḷakkiṉ mēluḷḷa kalayattil pōtiya aḷavu eṇṇey vārttu aṭiyiluḷḷa mūkkil (mukattil) tiriyiyum pōṭṭu viḷakkip poruttiviṭṭāl viḷakku viṭiyum vari eriyum. appāl eṇṇey vārttut tiriyum  pōṭṭāl maṟunāḷ vari eriyum.
ivvāṟu eṉṟum aṇiyāta tūṇṭāmaṇi viḷakkiyē nām nantā viḷakku eṉṟu nayampaṭa navilkiṟōm. inta nantā viḷakku namatu vīṭukaḷilum kōyilkaḷilum iravum pakalum oru viṉāṭiyum aṇiyātu oḷi ceytu koṇṭu niṟkiṟatu. intap paḻakkam tamiḻnāṭṭil maṭṭumalla, tamiḻarkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiyuḷḷa īḻa nāṭṭilum, malāyā nāṭṭilum, pōrṉiyō, piji, antamāṉ mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum iṉṟaḷavum iruntu varukiṟatu.
tūkku viḷakkukaḷil kūṭa palavitam uṇṭu. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ paṇ pāṭṭiṟkum pārampariyattiṟkum ēṟpap paṟavikaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ, caṅkukaḷ, teyvaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa palavita uruvaṅkaḷi iṇittu namatu ciṟpikaḷ ciṟpac ciṟappumikka cīriya tūkku viḷakkukaḷi uruvākkiyuḷḷārkaḷ. (paṭam 29 pārkka)
it tūkku viḷakkukaḷ palakaṇi, katavukaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku mēlēyum vaḷivukaḷilum muṟṟaṅkaḷilum maṇṭapaṅkaḷiṉ naṭuvilum caṅkilikaḷil toṅkaviṭappaṭṭirukkum.
makkaḷ kūṭum maṇṭapaṅkaḷiṉ naṭuvē veḷiccattiṟku maṭṭu miṉṟi aḻakiṟkākavum ivikaḷ upayōkappaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
it tūkku viḷakku vakiyic cērnta nantā viḷakkiṉ ciṟappu nāḷ muḻuvatum eriyum tirikku eṇṇey aḷikkattakka oru perum kalayam amikkappaṭṭiruppatēyākum.
ivvita viḷakku palavita icik karuvikaḷiṉ kōviyic cittirikkum oru ōviyampōl kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
ittūkku viḷakkukaḷi oru periya icik kaliñaṉ viruppiṟku ēṟpap palvēṟu vitamākac ceytiruppatākak karutap paṭukiṟatu. oru vēḷi, ulaka iciyil avaṉ uṉṉata niliyip peṟa viḷakku avaṉukku niṉivu ūṭṭiyirukkalām. eṉavē avaṉ ericuṭari ēṟṟum ceyalip paricuttamāṉa ceyalāka eṇṇi naṭantirukkalām. allatu icik kaliñaṉ kaliyulakil mīṇṭum oḷiviṭum ōr eri cuṭarāy ulakam ēṟṟum nipuṇaṇāy viḷaṅkum nōkkattip piratipalippatāy ittakiya viḷakkukaḷi uruvākkiyirukkalām.
iṅku, iṉṉoru putiya viḷakku kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atil tam kuṟukki vaḷittu iru pātaṅkaḷum talimēl irukka iru karaṅkaḷālum tūkku viḷakkip piṭittu niṟkum kūttāṭip peṇṇiṉ kaṭṭumatāṉa uṭal vaṉappu namatu ciṟpiyiṉ karuttik kavarntatiṉāl avvuruvi vittu inta viḷakki uruvākki yirukkalām.
ip peṇ uruviṉ mutukil oru nīṇṭa muḷiyeḻuppi ataṉ mītu caṅkilikaḷip piṇittu uyarē toṅka viṭuvataṟku ēṟṟavāṟu ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ittakiya uruva amippu nāṭṭiya nūṟkaḷil naṉku kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
viḷakkuc ceyyum kali maṟṟoru nuṇkaliyiṉ ciṟappi yum eṭuttuk kāṭṭum vaṇṇam amippaṭṭirukkiṟatu. aṭiyil tūkku viḷakkiṉ takaḻiyum ataṉmītu oru peṇṇiṉ vaṭivamum amippaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
maṟṟiya viḷakkukaḷil kāṇappaṭum aṉṉattiṟkup patilāka iṅku āṭaṟ peṇṇiṉ uruvam tīṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ap peṇṇiṉ uruvam cātāraṇamāṉa oru peṇ uṭkārntiruppatu pōṉṟō, niṉṟu koṇṭiruppatu pōṉṟō tīṭṭātu, nāṭṭiyam āṭum naṅkiyiṉ uruvamākak kāṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
āṉāl inta nāṭṭiya uruvattiyum cātāraṇamāka ellōrum pārttirukkiṉṟōm.
parata nāṭṭiyattiṟku, apinayam mutukelumpākum. apinayam nāṉku pirivāka pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. avi āṅkīkam, vājīkam, ākāriyam, cātvīkam eṉappaṭum.
āṅkīka apinayam, aṅkaṅkaḷ, pirattiyāṅkaṅkaḷ, upāṅ kaṅkaḷ, karaṇaṅkaḷ, aṅkāhāraṅkaḷ, muttirikaḷ ākiyavi kaḷāl pāvaṅkaḷi vivarikkum mukaṅkaḷil kāṭṭum kuṟi kūṭa apinayattilē aṭaṅkum.
āṅkīka apinayam mūvakippaṭum. atāvatu mukājam (mukak kuṟippu) cāriyā (uṭaliṉ nili atāvatu karaṇaṅkaḷ, aṅkā haraṅkaḷ ākiyavikaḷ) ceṭṭā (aṅkaṅkaḷ upāṅkaḷ piratti yāṅkaṅkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷ) eṉṟu nāṭṭiya nūṟkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
iṅku kūṟappaṭum karaṇaṅkaḷ 108 vakippaṭum. ivikaḷ citamparattiluḷḷa āṭavallāṉ kōpuraṅkaḷil kūṭa - ēṉ? - tiru vaṇṇāmali aruṇācalēcuvarar kōyilil kūṭac cittirikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
inta nūṟṟeṭṭuk karaṇaṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉa kaṅkāvatāraṇa karaṇaniliyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu ivvuruvam amik kappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
ippeṇṇiṉ uruvil parata nāṭṭiya nūlil kāṇappaṭum āṭaṟ peṇṇiṉ ilaṭcaṇam oṉṟum kāṇappaṭavilli. kaḻikkūttāṭip peṇṇiṉ uruvampōl kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
ittakiya viḷakkukaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil carvacātāraṇamākak kāṇappaṭavilli. eṉṟālum teṉṉintiya viḷakkukaḷ eṉṟu intiya aracāṅkap piracurap pakutiyārāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa oru nūlil ivviḷakkiṉ uruvam poṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ataṉ kīḻ oru nāṭōṭip peṇṇiṉ uruvam poṟikkappaṭṭa oru tūkku viḷakkum irukkiṟatu. itaṉ uyaram 28 ceṇṭi mīṭṭar. itu teṉ intiyāvil 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṟuru kuṟippu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itu pōṉṟa viḷakkukaḷik kāṇpatu aritiṉum aritu.  (paṭam 32 pārkka)
nantā viḷakkukaḷ tamiḻakattil maṭṭu miṉṟi īḻa nāṭṭilum atikam uṇṭu. ēṉ? - cāvakattil kūṭa nantā viḷakkukaḷ uṇṭu.
aṟiñar ō.ci. kaṅkuli avarkaḷ intiya kali itaḻ eṉṟu laṇṭaṉil iruntu veḷivanta oru ciṟanta kalip pattirikiyil 1916ām āṇṭil 17vatu malariṉ 136-vatu itaḻil oru ariya kaṭṭuriyi veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār. atil cāvakattiluḷḷa oru kiṉṉari viḷakkiṉ uruvappaṭattip piracurittuḷḷār. atu veṇ kalattil ceyyappaṭṭatu. 20 aṅkula uyaram uḷḷatu.
kiṉṉari mātēvarkkup paṇipuriyum tēvarkaḷil oruvar. ivar maṉita uṭalum paṟaviyiṉ kālkaḷum iṟakkikaḷum uṭiya oru aticayat tōṟṟam uṭiyavar. it teyvam intiyap paṇpāṭṭi ṉiṉṟē piṟantatu.
intiyarkaḷiṉ camayac celvākkāl cāvakattiṟkum iv vuruvam koṇṭu cellappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. inta aticaya uruvatti vittu oru tūkku viḷakku uruvākkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. intak kiṉṉari ici ulakic cērntatu. eṉavē kiṉṉari eppoḻutum oru icik karuviyi ēnti niṟpatu pōlavē kāṭciyaḷikkum. cila camayam āṇ uruvamuṭiya kiṉṉararkaḷōṭum kāṭciyaḷikkum.
kiṉṉarit tūkku viḷakku pārvikku aḻakāka illā viṭṭālum ciṟanta vēlippāṭuṭiyatu. intak kiṉṉari taṉ iru kikaḷilum akali ēntit tiruviḷakkup paṇi purikiṟatu. mukam peṇ vaṭivam uṭiyatu. iṟakkikaḷ virintu uyarntu niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ataṉ mutukil kuḻi ceyyappaṭṭu atil eṇṇey tēṅki niṟkumpaṭi ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. taliyiṉ piṉ puṟamum vāliṉ piṉ puṟamum iruntu kampikaḷ vaḷittu ataṉ mēlē caṅkili kōrkkappaṭṭu toṅka viṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
inta viḷakkum mika apūrvamāṉa viḷakkēyākum. intiyavil nāṉ eṅkum inta viḷakkik kaṇṭatē illi. eṉṟālum itu cāvakap paṇpāṭṭiliruntu arumpiyatu illi eṉpatu eṉatu karuttu maṭṭumaṉṟi, viḷakku ārāycciyil īṭupaṭṭa intiyak kaliñarkaḷiṉ ēkōpitta apiprāyamum ākum.
iḵtaṉṟi, kōyilkaḷiṉ karuvari vāyilil veḷiccattiṟ kākavum aḻakiṟkākavum caraviḷakku pōṭṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. vāyiliṉ mēlum iruppakkaṅkaḷilum irumpuk kampiyiṉ mēl aṭukkaṭukkāy iṭiyiṉṟi tūkku viḷakkip pōṉṟu ciṟu viḷakkukaḷip piṇittirukkiṟārkaḷ. iravil ivviḷakkukaḷ karuvaṟiyiṉ vāyilil aḻakuṟa miḷirkiṉṟatu. (paṭam 27 pārkka)

tūṇ viḷakkukaḷ
tūṇ viḷakkukaḷ iruvakippaṭum.  oṉṟu kōyilkaḷil cumār 20 aṭi uyarattil ulōkattāl ceyta pala aṭukkukaḷā lāṉa tūṇ viḷakku. maṟṟoṉṟu kaṭaṟkariyil toṉṟu toṭṭuk kalaṅkaḷ, ticiyaṟintu celvataṟkēṟṟa kalaṅkari viḷakkākum.
tūṇ viḷakkukaḷit tamiḻ nāṭṭil kāṇpatu aritu. āṉāl kēraḷa nāṭṭiluḷḷa periya kōyilkaḷilellām ittūṇ viḷakkukaḷik kāṇalām kēraḷa nāṭṭiṉ talinakarāy viḷaṅkum tiruvaṉantapurattiṟkuc ceṉṟa āṇṭu ceṉṟiruntēṉ.
nāṉ eṉatu iḷamippirāyattilum ikkōyilukku ceṉ ṟirukkiṟēṉ. eṉṟālum aṉṟu kāṇāta pala putiya viṣayaṅkaḷi ippoḻutu kaṇṭēṉ. ēṉ eṉil nāṉ ippoḻutu kalik kaṇṇōṭu kōyili nōkkiṉēṉ. pala ariya viṣayaṅkaḷi aṟintēṉ.
cātāraṇamākak kēraḷa nāṭṭuk kōyilkaḷil uḷḷatu pōl tiruvaṉantapurattilum muṉ vāyilil marattiṉāl aminta taṭṭutaṭṭāṉa oru tampam  irukkiṟatu. itutāṉ koṭimaram. maṟṟoru pakkattil koṭimaram pōl ulōkattāl ceyta pala aṭukkukaḷiyuṭiya viḷakkiyum ati vippataṟkākavum viḷak kēṟṟuvataṟkākavum vikkappaṭṭuḷḷa periya mara ēṇiyiyum kaṇṭēṉ.
karuvaṟiyic cuṟṟilumuḷḷa pirakārattil cuvar pōṉṟu kuṟukkum neṭukkumāka maraccaṭṭaṅkaḷ amittu avaṟṟil āyirakkaṇakkāṉa, pittaḷi allatu irumpu viḷakkukaḷip poruttiyirukkiṟārkaḷ.
ivviḷakkukaḷellām eṟṟappaṭṭirukkum pōtu nāṉ pārkkavilli. eṉṟālum uṟcavakālattil ivi aṉittiyum poruttiṉāl eṅkum jeka jōtiyāy viḷaṅkum eṉṟu kaṟpaṉi ceytu koṇṭēṉ.
iṅku laṭcatīpam eṉṟu oru uṟcavam naṭipeṟukiṟatu. oruvēḷi ataṟkākattāṉ kōyilic cuṟṟi ittuṇi viḷakku kaḷi vittirukkiṟārkaḷ pōlum eṉṟu eṇṇik koṇ ṭēṉ. eṇṇippārttāl niccayam ilaṭcam viḷakkukaḷ irukkum eṉṟu tiriyamākak kūṟalām.
tiruvaṉantapurattiṟku aṭutta paṭiyākac ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkum kōvilkaḷil oṉṟu kuruvāyūriluḷḷa kuruvāyūr appaṉ kōvi lākum. ikkōyiliṉ uṭpukuntāl koṭi marattiṟku aṭuttāṟ pōl paṉi pōṉṟu nīṇṭu niṟkum tūṇ viḷakku kampīramāka niṟkiṟatu. atiyoṭṭi nīṇṭa ēṇiyum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. (paṭam 33 pārkka)
kōyilil pukum poḻutē karuvaṟiyiluḷḷa tirumāliṉ vaṭivaḻaku coṭṭum aṟputamāṉa tōṟṟam makkaḷ uḷḷattik kavarntu viṭukiṟatu. vivarikka muṭiyāta ōr iṉpattik kuruvāyūr appaṉiṉ tiru uruvam aḷittu itayatti nirappiviṭukiṟatu.
kōyil pirakārattil kūttu ampalam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum araṅkattil purāṇaṅkaḷ paṭippatuṇṭu. cila camayaṅkaḷil kūttu kaḷum naṭipeṟum. kūttu eṉpatu katikaḷi naṭittuk kāṭṭum nāṭakamēyākum. itu vārtti etuvum iṉṟi uṇarcci pāvaṅ kaḷālē naṭittuk kāṭṭum oru aruṅkali.
mēlum, annāṭṭiluḷḷa kōyilkaḷil naṭipeṟum vaḻi pāṭṭil oru taṉittaṉmi uṇṭu. tirumāl, civaṉ eṉṟa vēṟṟumiyiṉṟi iṟivaṉi vaṇaṅki, appaṉē eṉṟu aṉpu poṅka vaṇaṅki vaḻipaṭukiṉṟārkaḷ.
malināṭṭuk kōyilkaḷ ellām paṇṭiya tirāviṭac ciṟpatti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, ceṅkallum cuṇṇām pum kalantu kaṭṭappaṭṭa kōyilkaḷ ākum. caṅkakālak kōyil kaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu eḻuppappaṭṭaṉa vākum.
iṉṟiya tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kaṟkōyilkaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu kaṭṭappaṭṭa kōyilkaḷi kāṇpatu aritu. āṉāl avi tirāviṭac ciṟpamuṟiyil aṉucarittē kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa vākum.
kēraḷam paṇṭiya tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ oru aṅkamāka miḷirntatu eṉpataṟku eṇṇaṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu. eṉṟalum tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa vīṭukaḷilum kōyilkaḷilum kāṇappaṭum vitam vitamāṉa ulōka viḷakkukaḷi iṅku atikam kāṇa muṭiyavilli. eṉṟālum oru cila apūrvamāṉa ulōka viḷakku kaḷik kāṇamuṭiyum.
periya kōyilkaḷil koṭi marattiṟkarukil periya tūṇ viḷakkukaḷ iruppatip pōl ciṟiya kōyilkaḷil cumār 5 aṭi uyarattil periya veṇkala viḷakkum atē aṭippaṭiyil ciṟiya vaṭivil aṭukkaṭukkāyt tīpattaṭṭu pōṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭa tiruviḷakkum kēraḷa nāṭṭil uḷḷatu.
tiruvañcik kuḷattil uḷḷa kōyilil ēḻu aṭukkuḷḷa oru ciṟu tūṇ viḷakku uḷḷatu. itil kamuku pōṉṟa oru naṭuttaṇṭum aṭiyiliruntu aṭukkaṭukkāy oṉṟiṉ mēl oṉṟāka ēḻuvaṭṭa māṉa viḷakkukaḷum uḷḷaṉa. aṭiyil uḷḷa akaliviṭa nuṉiyil uḷḷatu caṟṟu ciṟitāka uḷḷatu. itu muḻuvatum veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ivviḷakku kōyiliṉuḷ pukuntavuṭaṉ mutalil kāṭciyaḷikkiṟatu.
iṅku cēramāṉ perumāḷ pūci ceyta āṭavallāṉiṉ uruvam irukkiṟatu. cēramāṉ perumāḷ kutiri ēṟikkoṇṭum cuntarar yāṉi ēṟikkoṇṭum kilāyattiṟkuc celvatu pōl oru ciṟpam irukkiṟatu.
iḵtaṉṟic cēramāṉ perumāḷ nāyaṉār tam kālattil citamparattiliruntu cila tēvatācikaḷi aḻittu ik kōyi lukkup paṇiviṭi ceyyum paṭi ēṟpāṭu ceytārām. avarkaḷiṉ iṉattavarkaḷ iṉṉum aṅku iruntu varukiṟārkaḷ.
kēraḷattiṉ maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷil āṇkaḷ tām pūciyiṉ pōtu cuvāmikku viḷakku eṭukkiṟārkaḷ. āṉāl tiruvañcik kuḷattuk kōyilil maṭṭum tamiḻakattip pōl tēvatācikaḷ viḷakku eṭukkiṟārkaḷ.
tamiḻnāṭṭut tirukkōyilkaḷil muṟkālattil tīpamum taṭṭum eṭukkum urimi taḷip peṇkaḷākiya tēvatācikaḷukkē uṇṭu.
	aṭṭatik kumpukaḻum ālavāyc cokkarukkut
	taṭṭeṭukkum taḷiyilāḷ
eṉṟa viṟaliviṭu tūtukkaṇṇi itaṉi uṟutippaṭuttuvataṟku uriya cāṉṟākum.
it tūṇ viḷakkukaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil amikkappaṭṭa kuttuviḷakkukaḷ aḻakuṟa amikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉ aḻakiṉi eṇṇic ciṉimāp paṭaṅkaḷil kūṭap puttar ciliyiṉ muṉ ittakiya viḷakkukaḷi vittu oru peṇ vaḻipaṭuvatu pōl cittarikkappaṭṭiruntatu.
kēraḷattil kāṇappaṭum mikapperiya tūṇ viḷakkip pōṉṟa oṉṟit tamiḻ nāṭṭil maṭṭumalla - intiyāvil eṅkum kāṇa muṭiyavilli. intiya aracāṅka veḷiyīṭāka 1961ām āṇṭil veḷivanta intiya viḷakkukaḷ eṉṟa nūlilō, `viḷakkiṉ varalāṟu eṉat tiru. rāpiṉcaṉ avarkaḷāl 1939ām āṇṭu kirēṭ piriṭṭaṉil veḷiyiṭap peṟṟa oru oppaṟṟa nūlilō ittakiya viḷakkukaḷik kāṇa muṭiyavilli. eṉavē intat tūṇ viḷakkē ulakil uyarnta viḷakku eṉṟu kūṟiṉum poruntum.
tūṇ viḷakku cumār 20 aṭi uyarattil pittaḷiyāl ceyyappaṭṭa oru oppaṟṟa viḷakkākum. ivviḷakkukaḷ kalliṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa oru pīṭattiṉ mītu ēṟṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. itic cuṟṟi mukkōṇa vaṭivamāka mūṉṟu kampikaḷāl iṇikkappaṭṭa oru pīṭamum ataṉ arukē oru ēṇiyum vikkappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa.
ivviḷakkiṉ naṭuttaṇṭic cuṟṟi cumār 12-kku mēṟpaṭṭa takaḻikaḷ vaṭṭavaṭivamāka amikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. atil eṇṇey vārttut tiriyiṭṭu viḷakkēṟṟukiṟārkaḷ.
iv viḷakkiluḷḷa 12- akalkaḷilum 12-paṭi eṇṇeyāvatu vārkkappaṭa vēṇṭum. ivviḷakkukaḷiṉ aṭiyil iruppavi vicālamāyum mēlē cellac cellac ciṟuttum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iravil ivviḷakki ēṟṟi viṭṭāl corkkappaṉi pōṉṟu viḷakku niṟkum pirakāram muḻuvatum veḷiccantarum. 
ivviḷakku, kōyiliṉ pirakārattil iruppatāl atikamāṉa kāṟṟu aṅkup puka iṭamilli. ciṟiya kāṟṟiṟkellām iv viḷakku aciyātu. naṉṟāka eriyum maḻi vantāl viḷakku eriyātu. itu vari maḻiyāl ivviḷakku pātikkappaṭāmal iruppataṟku evvita ēṟpāṭum ceyyappaṭavilli.
itaṟku aṭuttapaṭiyākac ciṟantu viḷakkum tirumaṇa viḷakkip pōṉṟa ciṟiya tūṇ viḷakku kōyiliṉuḷḷē iruppatāl ati maḻiyō kāṟṟō acikka muṭiyātu.
ivviḷakkukaḷ periyaviyāka iruppiṉum ciṟpikaḷ ivaṟṟi aḻaku oḷiṟat tiṟampaṭac ceytirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
kēraḷattiṟkup paṇṭik kālattil yavaṉarkaḷiṉ toṭarpu atikam iruntatākat terikiṟatu. eṉavē kirēkka nāṭṭut toṅku viḷakkup pāṇiyil ceyyappaṭṭa viḷakkukaḷ cila kēraḷattil uṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
tiruvāṅkūriṉ kalikaḷum kippaṇikaḷum eṉṉum peya ruṭaṉ kalkattā palkalik kaḻakattiṉ intiyak kalippakutiyil, pērāciriyarāy viḷaṅkum ṭākṭar ṭellākirāmariṣ, ṭākṭar jē. eyc. kaciṉ, kēraḷa aracāṅkap puti poruḷ ārāyccit tuṟi iyakkunar tiru.ār. vācutēva poṭuvāl ākiyōr cērntu eḻuti, 1948ām āṇṭu laṇṭaṉ rāyal intiya cociṭṭiyum tiruvāṅkūr aracāṅkamum kūṭṭākac cērntu veḷiyiṭṭa ariya nūlil kirēkka viḷakkukaḷiṉ pāṇiyil aminta oru viḷakkil, kīḻē takaḻiyum ataṉmītu kutiriyum kutiri mītu cavāri ceyyum oru teyva uruvamum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kutiriyiṉ taliyiṉiṉṟu caṅkili kōrkkappaṭṭuk kūri yiṉ mukaṭṭil toṅka viṭappaṭuvataṟkēṟṟa muṟiyil ceyyap paṭṭa toṅku viḷakkākum itu. āṉāl avvāciriyarkaḷil evarum kūṭa inta māperum tūṇviḷakku mēṉāṭṭu pāṇiyil ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṟu kūṟavilli.
mēlum ivviḷakku uruvil periyatākak kāṇappaṭiṉum carvacātāraṇamāy namatu vīṭukaḷilum kōyilkaḷilum kāṇap paṭum kuttuviḷakkiṉ aṭippaṭiyilē ceyyappaṭṭatākum. ivviḷakku muḻuvatum orē vārppil ceyyappaṭavilli. ceyva til ciramamum atikam. eṉavē cātāraṇamāka namatu nāṭṭil ceyyappaṭum kuttu viḷakkik pōl pala tuṇṭukaḷāka vārttu oṉṟāka iṇikkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟē nampukiṟēṉ.
ivviḷakku malināṭṭiṟ keṉṟē uruvākkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamuṇṭu. kaṭalil uḷḷa kappalkaḷ kalaṅkari viḷakkiṉ mūlam pāti terintum tuṟimukatti aṟintum celvatip pōl malināṭṭil malikaḷil aṅkoru vīṭum iṅkoru vīṭumāyt taṉittu vāḻupavarkaḷ kōyilil viḷak kēṟṟiyatum avveḷiccatti nōkkit tattam kuṭiciyiliruntu kōyilukku varavē inta viḷakku mikavum payaṉ taruvatākumeṉṟu karutukiṟēṉ.
itē amippil - cumār 5 aṭi uyarattil vārkkappaṭṭa kōyil viḷakkukaḷ kēraḷattil maṭṭumalla; kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭattilum kāṇak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. itip pōṉṟa viḷakku oṉṟu ceṉṉit tolporuḷ kāṭci cāliyilum irukkiṟatu. (paṭam 34 pārkka)
itē amippil ceyyappaṭṭa ciṟu kuttu viḷakkukaḷ kēraḷattilum kaṉṉiyākumari māvaṭṭattilum uḷḷaṉa. inta viḷakkukaḷ vīṭṭu viḷakkic cērntavi.
ivikaḷ veṇkalattāl ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. cumār 2½ aṭi uyaram irukkum. kīḻē pātamum ataṉ naṭuvil viḷakkut taṇṭum taṇṭic cuṟṟi vaṭṭa vaṭivamāka 4 allatu 5- takaḻikaḷum uḷḷaṉa.
taṇṭil ceyyappaṭṭa ciṟpa vēlippāṭukaḷ viḷakkiṉ ciṟappi uyarttik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. itu aḻakut tiruviḷakkukaḷil oṉṟākak karutappaṭṭu varukiṟatu.
ivvita viḷakkukaḷi nelli, maturi, irāmanātapuram, tirucci, kōvi, tañci mutaliya māvaṭṭaṅkaḷil ituvari nāṉ kaṇṭatēyilli. ceṉṉi nakaril ittakiya viḷakkukaḷ kāṇak kiṭappatāl oru vēḷi ceṅkalpaṭṭu māvaṭṭattil atikam kiṭittālum kiṭikkalām. ivviḷakkil ovvoru takaḻiyilum iṣṭamāṉa aḷavil tirikaḷi iṭṭu viḷakkup poruttalām pōlum.
iṅku kāṭṭappaṭṭirukkum ovvoru takaḻiyilum 10, 12 tirikaḷ cuṭar viṭṭu erivatu pōl kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vīṭukaḷil ivvaḷavu tiriyiṭṭu viḷakkup poruttamāṭṭārkaḷ.
nelli māvaṭṭattil vīṭukaḷil 4 mukamuḷḷa viḷakkut tāṉ peritum upayōkikkiṟārkaḷ. itil nāṉku tirikaḷ tāṉ eriyum.
maturi māvaṭṭattil 5 mukamuḷḷa viḷakkukaḷ upayōkap paṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil 6 mukaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. 4 mukaṅkaḷ āṟumuka nayiṉāriyum kuṟippiṭuvatāka makkaḷ karutukiṉṟārkaḷ.
ivviḷakkukaḷ vīṭukaḷil nāḷtōṟum upayōkappaṭuva tilli. vīṭukaḷil makkaḷ atikamākak kūṭum viṣēca tiṉaṅkaḷil maṭṭum upayōkikkappaṭukiṟatu. nāḷ tōṟum upayōkittāl atika eṇṇey celavākum.
kalaṅkari viḷakku
tamiḻar kaṇṭa viḷakkukaḷil periyatu kalaṅkari viḷakkē yākum. itu iṉṟu kēraḷāvil uḷḷa kōyilkaḷil kāṇappaṭum tūṇ viḷakkiviṭa uyarntatu. āṉāl ivviḷakku kōyilukkup payaṉpaṭum viḷakku alla; itu kaṭalil payaṇañ ceyyum kappalukkup payaṉpaṭum viḷakku.
kappali ōṭṭum mālumi tolivil varum poḻutē ivviḷakkiṉ oḷiyik kaṇṭu kari cēravum cariyāṉa pātiyil marakkalattic celuttavum tuṇi niṟkum viḷakku itu. iṉṟu itaṉit tīpatampam eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.
paṇṭiyat tamiḻ makkaḷ kāliṟ pirivatōṭu kalattiṟ pirintum āyirakkaṇakkāṉa mil tūram āḻ kaṭalit tam marak kalattiṉāl tāṇṭi vaṇikam ceytu poruḷīṭṭi vantaṉar. aṉṟu tiri kaṭalōṭiyum tiraviyam tēṭu eṉpatu avarkaḷiṉ aṉṉi mārkaḷ avarkaḷukku iṭṭa āṇi.
	muḻaṅku kaṭaṟanta viḷakku katirmutta
		maram pōḻn taṟutta kaṇṇē rilaṅkuvaḷip
	paratar tanta palvēṟu kūlamiruṅkaḻic ceru viṟ
		ṟīmpuḷi veḷḷuppup parantōṅku varip piṉ
	vaṉkit timilar koḻumīṉ kuṟiiya
		tuṭik kaṭṭuṉi yal viḻumiya nāvāy
(ma. kāñci 11 : 315 - 323)
eṉṟu kūṟum maturik kāñciyiṉ pāṭal, tamiḻar kaṭṭiya aḻakiya kappalkaḷil vaṇikarkaḷ alikaṭalukkum añcātu ayal nāṭu kaḷukkup pōntu muttu caṅku vaḷiyalkaḷ, tāṉiyaṅkaḷ, uppu, pāci, pavaḷam patamiṭappaṭṭa mīṉ mutaliyavikaḷi viṟṟup poruḷīṭṭi vantati aṟivuṟuttukiṟatu.
kaṭal kaṭantu ayal nāṭukaḷiliruntu paṇṭaṅkaḷik kalaṅ kaḷil ēṟṟik koṇṭu vanta, virintu ōṭavalla kutirikaḷum, kīḻ nāṭṭiliruntu maraṅkalaṅkaḷil vanta uṇavup poruḷkaḷum kaṭārattiṉiṉṟu kalaṅkaḷil ēṟṟi vanta kaṇakkaṟṟa poruḷkaḷum piṟavum tamiḻ nāṭṭu vaṇikatti alaṅkarittaṉa.
	nīriṉ vanta nimi parip puraviyum
	......    ......    .....
īḻattuṇavum kāḻakat tākkamum
	ariyavum periyavum neriya īṇṭi
	vaḷantali mayaṅkiya naṉantali maṟuku
eṉṟa paṭṭiṉappāliyiṉ pākkaḷum iṉṉum naṟṟiṇi cilappatikāram mutaliya nūṟkaḷiṉ pākkaḷum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kappal vāṇipatti eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻ nāṭṭil koṟki, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam, nākappaṭ ṭiṉam, māmallapuram, muciṟi, uvari, kāyal pōṉṟa tuṟimukaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. iṅku cuṅkac cāvaṭikaḷum paṇṭakacālikaḷum yavaṉac cērikaḷum kalaṅkari viḷakkukaḷum iruntaṉa.
kariyil kaṭali oṭṭi vāṉuṟa vaḷarntu, paṉimaram pōl ēṇikkum eṭṭā uyarvuṭiyaṉavāyk kallum cuṇṇamum kalantu kaṭṭappaṭṭa māṭattiṉ mītu kalaṅkari viḷakkup poruttap peṟṟiruntatu.
iravil āḻkaṭalil añcātu cellum kappalkaḷ kari kaḷiyum tuṟimukaṅkaḷiyum pātiyiyum ticikaḷiyum aṟintu kuṟippiṭṭa iṭatti aṭivataṟkāka āṅkāṅkē pala kalaṅkari viḷakkukaḷik kaṭṭiyiruntaṉar.
	vāṉa mūṉṟiya matali pōla
	ēṇi cāttiya ēṟṟaruñ ceṉṉi
	viṇpora nivanta vēyā māṭat
	tiraviṉ māṭṭiya vilaṅku cuṭar nekiḻ
	uravunī raḻuvat tōṭu kalaṅkariyum,
eṉṟu paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟāṉa perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭi kūṟukiṟatu.
eṉavē tamiḻakattil kaṭaliṟ cellum kappalkaḷukkut tuṟimukam irukkum iṭatti iraviṟkāṭṭakkūṭiya kalaṅkari viḷakkukaḷ iruntatāka aiyamaṟat terikiṟatu. ōṭu kalaṅkariyum eṉpataṟku uriyāciriyarkaḷ kaṭalil cellum kalaṅkaḷi aḻikkum viḷakku eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
ilaṅku nīr varippiṟ kalaṅkari viḷakkamum eṉṟu cilappatikāram kūṟuvatu kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattiluḷḷa varalāṟṟup pukaḻ peṟṟa kalaṅkari viḷakkik kuṟippatākum.
kaṅkuṉ māṭṭiya katiroṇ cuṭar, eṉṟu naṟṟiṇi nayantu kūṟuvatu tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa pala tuṟimukaṅkaḷil kalaṅkari viḷakkukaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpati valiyuṟuttukiṉṟatu.
tamiḻar kaṇṭa kalaṅkari viḷakku alakcantiriyāvil tālami cōtar amitta veḷicca vīṭṭiṟkup paṉṉeṭuṅ kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭatu. inta oḷik kōpuraṅkaḷiṉ tuṇi koṇṭu tamiḻarkaḷ iravil ciṉṉañciṟiya ōṭaṅkaḷi maṭṭumaṉṟip peṉṉam periya vaṅkam eṉṉum kuṉṟaṉiya kalaṅkaḷiyum ulakiṉ nāṉā ticikaḷukkum ōṭṭi vantaṉar.
kalam eṉṟa vārttiyē eṭṭuttoki nūṟkaḷil kappal eṉṟa poruḷil vaḻakkappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. tamiḻarkaḷ ciṉṉañciṟu ki viḷakkiṉiṉṟu māperum kalaṅri viḷakku variyum paṉṉeṭum kālattiṟku muṉpē kaṇṭuḷḷaṉar. (paṭam 36 pārkka)
kēraḷa nāṭṭiṉ kōyil pirakāraṅkaḷil palakic caṭṭaṅkaḷ aṭittu atil ulōkattāl ceyyappaṭṭa ciṟu viḷakkukaḷip poṟuttiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. periya kōyilkaḷil ittakiya tīpac caṭṭaviḷakkukaḷ āyirakkaṇakkāka irukkalām. ciṟiya kōyil kaḷil kūṭa cumār 4-āyirattiṟku mēl irukkalām. itu periya viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil maṭṭum ēṟṟappaṭum. (paṭam 35 pārkka)

pāvi viḷakku
tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷakkukaḷ aṉittum kali aḻaku vāyn tavikaḷ tāṉ. āṉāl pāvi viḷakkuttāṉ taliciṟanta amcaṅkaḷ palavum peṟṟa viḷakkākum. pāvi viḷakki, tiru viḷakku nācciyār eṉṟum tīpa laṭcumi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattilē pāvi viḷakkukaḷ tamiḻakattil iruntaṉa eṉpataṟkuc caṅka nūṟkaḷil eṇṇaṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ippoḻutu namakkuk kiṭattirukkum pāvi viḷakkukaḷ mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattil ceyyappaṭṭavikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkilli.
pāvi viḷakkuttāṉ eḻiliṟ ciṟanta viḷakkāka miḷirkiṟatu. namatu varalāṟṟi eṭuttukkāṭṭum ariya eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ilaṅkukiṟatu. namatu nākarikatti, namatu eṇṇaṟṟa aṇikaḷiṉ perumiyi eṭuttukkāṭṭum vāṉ maṇiyāyt tikaḻkiṟatu. (paṭam 36 pārkka)
eṭuttukkāṭṭākat tañci māvaṭṭattic cērnta vittīvaraṉ kōyililum tañcāvūr pirakatīcuvarar kōyililum, tiruviṭi marutūr, tirukkōkarṇam kōyilkaḷilum kāṇap paṭum tīpalaṭcumi viḷakkukaḷi āḻntu ārāyntu pārkkalām.
pāviyiṉ kaṭṭumatāṉa uṭalamippē oru taṉi aḻakuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. poṉmalar nāṟṟa muṭittu eṉṟa mutumoḻipōl aḻaku poliyum tiruuruvil maṇikaḷ tikaḻum aṇikaḷ, aḻakukku aḻaku ceytāṟ pōṉṟilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
taliyiṉ muṉpakkam neṟṟic cuṭṭiyum cūriyap pirapiyum, cantirap pirapiyum, cūṭāmaṇiyum piṉpuṟattil irākkoṭi, caṭi, nākam, tirukuppū mutaliya aṇikaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. irāk koṭiyic cuṟṟi aḻakāyt tāḻampūkkaḷ aḻakuṟap poṉṉili pōṟceyyappaṭṭut tali alaṅkārattic ciṟappikkiṉṟaṉa.
kātukaḷilē tōṭukaḷ kaviṉuṟa oḷirkiṉṟaṉa. kīḻē cimikkikaḷ oyyāramāka ūcalāṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉum kātil karṇap pūkkaḷum, muruku, pūṭi, kuruṭu mutaliya aṇikaḷum mūkkil mūkkutti, pullākku pōṉṟa aṇikaḷum maṇikaḷ patittu ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
kaḻuttil pūrāṉ aṭṭiki, poṭṭuc caram, karuka maṇi, nellikkāy māli, muttuc caram, kōtumi māli, picciyarumpu māli ākiyavikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
puyattil vaṅki, aṭiyil kuñcalattuṭaṉ pācip pantu, kiyil pāṭli (pāṭṭal), kaṅkaṇam, tōṭā, viralkaḷil mōtiram, neḷivu pōṉṟa aṇikaḷ tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.
ciṟṟiṭiyil ariñāṇum oṭiyāṇamum kālkaḷil pāṭakam, kolucu, pātaracam taṇṭiyum kāl viralkaḷil miñci (kālāḻi) pillaṇi, pīli pōṉṟa aṇikaḷum alaṅkāramāy ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
valat tōḷil paccik kiḷi taṉ pavaḷavāyuṭaṉ oyyāramāka uṭkārntirukkiṟatu. tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷilē kiḷikaḷ talivikkut tuṇiyāka iruntu avaḷuṭiya iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷik kēṭpatum koñcu moḻi pēcuvatum iṅku cittarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ciṟpi aḻakiya paṭimattilē kiḷiyi eḻiloḻukum ēntiḻiyiṉ tōḷil amarntu pēcuvatip pōl taṉ kittiṟaṉ aṉittum viḷaṅkac ciṟappuṭaṉ ceytuḷḷāṉ.
kōyilkaḷil uḷḷa caṉṉitikaḷil nāḷtōṟum kāli mutal iravu vari akal viḷakkit tam iru karaṅkaḷilum ēntiya vāṟu aḻakuṟa niṉṟu koṇṭirukkiṟāḷ tīpalaṭcumi.
viḷakku oru viḷakkan tāṅki eṉṟu tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ varṇippatu pōl kāṭci aḷittu niṟkiṟāḷ it tīpalaṭcumi.
ippāvi viḷakkukaḷil iṭiyil aṇivittirukkum āṭikaḷiṉ naṭuvil pala kōṭukaḷum vaḷivukaḷum pala vaṇṇat til tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. viḷimpukaḷil palvēṟu vitamāṉa pū vēlip pāṭukaḷ kaṇkavar vaṉappuṭaṉ aḻaku ceykiṉṟaṉa. muntāṇi kaḷiṉ vēlippāṭu peṇkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷiyum karuttiyum kavarvaṉavākum. pāviyiṉ muṉtāṇi mika nīḷamāka iruntāl kāṟṟaṭittu muntāṇi kikaḷil ēntiyirukkum akal viḷakkil viḻuntu tīppaṟṟi viṭakkūṭātu eṉpati aṟintu ciṟpi, muntāṇi yiṉ nīḷattik kuṟukki atit tōḷiṉ mītu pōṭṭiruppatu pōl ceytirukkiṟāṉ.
ippāvi viḷakkil kāṇappaṭum aṇikaḷ, cilappatikāram, peruṅkati neṭunalvāṭi mutaliya nūṟkaḷil kāṇappaṭum nakikaḷi niṉivūṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
urōmarkaḷiṉ pāvi viḷakkukaḷil cila, tam mārparukil, viḷakkut tiriyi vittirukkum oru peṇṇiṉ uruva amippi uṭiyaṉavāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. itip paṟṟi virivāka eyc. pi. vālṭṭa eṉṉum aṟiñar piriṭṭiṣ tolporuṭ kāṭci cāliyil uḷḷa kirēkkar, urōmar ākiyavarkaḷuṭiya viḷakku kaḷiṉ paṭṭiyal eṉṉum nūlil naṉku vivarittuḷḷār.
ekiptiliruntu kiṭitta maṟṟoru viḷakkum iṅku uḷḷatu. atil muṟkāla ekiptiyarkaḷiṉ aciri eṉṟa teyva uruvam tīṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. atil viḷakkit tāṅki niṟkum pāvi yiṉatu iṭuppiṉ kīḻppākam nīṇṭu tikaḻkiṟatu. iḵtaṉṟic cila veṇkala viḷakkukaḷil kali aḻakukaḷ miṉṉukiṉṟaṉa.
oru viḷakkil kippiṭikkup patilāka iru karaṭumuraṭāṉa korintiyaṉ tūṇkaḷāl tāṅkappaṭṭa vil vaḷivu maṇpaṭat tiṉ uṭpuṟamum, olimpik tēvatikaḷiṉ maṉṉaṉāṉa cīya uruvamum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. maṟṟoru viḷakkil piṉpuṟam kip piṭikkup patilākac cātāraṇamāṉa tūṇkaḷiṉ mēl uḷḷa vil vaḷivu maṇṭapattiṟku aṭiyil iratattiṉ mītu ēṟic cellum kipel eṉṉum kaṭavuḷiṉ vaṭivam kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
inta viḷakkukaḷil oṉṟukūṭat teyva uruvattit tavirttu vēṟu uruvam poṟikkappaṭṭatākak kāṇappaṭavilli. itaṉ mūlam tamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa eḻil viḷakkukaḷ tīpalaṭcumi viḷakkukaḷ inta yavaṉa viḷakkukaḷiliruntu eḻuntaṉa eṉṟu eṇṇuvataṟku eḷḷaḷavum iṭamilli eṉṟu viḷakku ārāycciyil ciṟantu viḷaṅkum kalāmēti ō.ci. kaṅkuli kuṟippiṭuvatu uṟṟu nōkkaṟ kuriyatu.1
āṉāl, iṅkilāntiluḷḷa tol poruḷ kāṭci cāliyiluḷḷa viḷakkukaḷip pōṉṟu teyva vaṭivam tīṭṭap peṟṟa pāvi viḷakkukaḷi urōmar, kirēkkar pōṉṟa yavaṉa vaṇikarkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkuk koṇṭu vantu tāṅkaḷ vāḻnta uṟiviṭaṅkaḷil upayōkittirukkalām. avikaḷil cilavaṟṟit tamiḻarkaḷukku viṟṟirukkalām. allatu intap pāvi viḷakku kaḷip paṟṟit tamiḻarkaḷukkuk kūṟiyirukkalām.
ituvari tol poruḷ kāṭci cāliyiṉarālum taṉip paṭṭa aṟiñarkaḷālum cēkarikkappaṭṭirukkum yavaṉarkaḷiṉ viḷakkukaḷil ciṟappāṉa viḷakku, kikaḷil akali ēnti niṟkum pāvi viḷakkēyākum. atilum pāviyar karaṅkaḷil ēnti niṟkum kiṇṇamē ciṟappuṭiyatākum.
āṉāl, tamiḻnāṭṭil kāṇappaṭum tiruviḷakku nācci yāriṉ uruvatti uṟṟu nōkkiṉ atil ciṟappāṉa pakuti tiri irukkum kiṇṇantāṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkilli. ēntiḻi ki kaḷil ēnti niṟkum eḻil oḻukum akalum avaḷ mītu aṇi ceyyum ariya aṇikaḷum aḻakiya āṭiyum tōḷiṉ mītu tōṉṟum kiḷḷiyum avaḷ niṟkum pīṭamum aḻakuk kaliyiṉ mutal iṭattic cuvīkarittuk koḷḷat takkaviyākak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
avaḷ karaṅkaḷil ēnti niṟkum kiṇṇamum tiriyiṭum mukamum aḻakuk kaliyiṉ iraṇṭāvatu iṭattit tāṉ peṟattakkaṉatāy  viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
eṉavē, tamiḻakam tanta pāvi viḷakkiṉ amippil, pārkkum paṇpum polivum eḻilum karuttum tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ paḻam peru nāṭṭil arumpiya ciṟpak kaliyiṉ viḷivēyākum. atu yavaṉarkaḷiṭamiruntu iravalākavō, kaṭaṉākavō, kalik kaḷavākavō peṟṟa karuttalla eṉpatu uṟuti. itit tamiḻ nāṭṭuc ciṟpa viṟpaṉṉarkaḷum veḷināṭṭuc ciṟpa mētikaḷum oruṅkē oppuk koḷkiṉṟaṉar.
mēlum yavaṉavarkaḷiṉ pāvi viḷakkukaḷum oḷi viḷakku kaḷum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ tīpalaṭcumi viḷakku, aṉṉa  viḷakku ākiyavi kaḷiṉ amippukaḷiliruntu muṟṟilum vēṟupaṭṭaṉavākum. āṉāl namatu turatirṣṭam, yavaṉarkaḷiṉ pāvi viḷakkōṭu oppiṭṭup pārkkap paḻaṅkālattil tamiḻakattil ceyyappaṭṭa tīpalaṭcumi viḷakkō aṉṉa viḷakkō ituvari namakkuk kiṭikkavilli.
kirēkkar, urōmar mutaliyōrkaḷiṉ viḷakkukaḷ karut taḻakō, kaliyaḻakō, niliyaḻakō, aṇiyaḻakō etuvumiṉṟi apakkuvamākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ātik kirēkkarkaḷiṉ  veṅkalap paṭimaṅkaḷil uḷḷa ciṟpa nuṭpaṅkaḷum tiṟamiyum perumiyum itaṟkum atikamākavē kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. ituvari kaṇṭupiṭitta teṉṉintiya viḷakkukaḷiyum kirēkkar urōmar viḷakkukaḷiyum oppiṭṭu ārāyntu oru kōṭpāṭṭi evarum vakuttuviṭa muṭiyātu.
āṉāl kuṟukiya kaṇṇōṭṭaṅ koṇṭa pala airōppiya ārāycciyāḷarkaḷ cintiyātu piṉvarum muṭiviṟku vantu viṭukiṟārkaḷ.
tamiḻnāṭṭup paṭima uṟpatti muṟikaḷellām kirēkkar urōmar mutaliya yavaṉa nāṭṭup paḻamperumuṟikaḷiṉiṉṟu puttuyir peṟṟu eḻuntaṉa. tamiḻakac ciṟpikaḷ mēṉāṭṭuc ciṟpa nuṭpaṅkaḷi ayalnāṭukaḷōṭu tamiḻakam naṭattiya vaṇikattiṉ payaṉākak koṟki pūmpukār pōṉṟa tuṟi mukaṅkaḷil aṟiyum vāyppip peṟṟaṉar: kirēkkac ciṟpak kaliyiṉiṉṟu kān tārattil tuḷirtta putta ciṟpam pōṉṟu urōma, kirēkkap paṭima muṟiyip piṉ paṟṟit tamiḻ nāṭṭup paṭima muṟi putiya vaḷarcciyum putiya vāḻvum peṟṟatu eṉṟu mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ palar muṭivu kaṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar.
tirumāliṉ aṉantacayaṉat tōṟṟattil kūṭa ayal nāṭṭuk karuttōṭṭam kalantirukkiṟatu eṉṟu viṉceṇṭ mit eṉṟa mēṉāṭṭu varalāṟṟu nūṟpulavar varikiṉṟār.
mēlum airōppiya ciṟpikaḷ aḻakiṉ niliyip paṟṟik koṇṭuḷḷa maṉōpāvaṅkaḷ inta aṉanta cayaṉam eṉṉum karuttuppaṭa eḻuti teṉṉintiyak kali vaḷarcciyi mika nācukkāy iḻittuk kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. ṭākhōmil tēcīyat tolporuḷ kāṭci  cāliyiṉ eṇṭimiyaṉ eṉṟa pakutiyi luḷḷa kirēkkar, urōmar paṭimaṅkaḷōṭu teṉṉintiyap paṭimaṅ kaḷil kāṇappaṭum orumippāṭukaḷi vittē inta muṭipik koṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ. tīpalaṭcumi viḷakku tamiḻ nāṭṭuc ciṟpa muṟikaḷil muḷitteḻunta cīriyavikaḷil oṉṟalla - mikac cātāraṇamāṉavikaḷil oṉṟākum. tamiḻnāṭṭut tīpalaṭcumi viḷakkukaḷukku oppuvami collakkūṭiya viḷakkukaḷ vaṭa intiyāvil kūṭak kāṇa muṭikiṟatu.
iṟutiyākak kūṟiya inta muṭipu mikipaṭak kūṟiyatu; vaṭa intiyāvil teṉṉintiyāviluḷḷa viḷakkukaḷip pōṉṟa viḷakkukaḷ uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. itu muṟṟilum tavaṟāṉa apippirāyamākum. vaṭa intiyāvil tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷak kiṟku iṇiyāṉa viḷakkukaḷē illi.
kāciyil aṉṉapūraṇā ālayattil oru viḷakku uḷḷatu. atil tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷakkil kāṇappaṭum aḻakiyō amip piyō kāṇavē muṭiyātu. mēlum avviḷakkum tamiḻakattil ceyyappaṭṭu tamiḻnāṭṭup paktar oruvarāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatākum.
itu maṭṭumalla, kāciyiluḷḷa vicuvanātar - vicālāṭci kōyilil uḷḷa pittaḷi veṇkalap paṇṭaṅkaḷ, viḷakkukaḷ palavum tamiḻnāṭṭilēyē ceyyappaṭṭavi eṉṟu kūṟac cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpiruntē tamiḻarkaḷ kilāya maliyōṭum, mokañcatārōvōṭum, kāciyōṭum toṭarpu koṇṭiruntaṉar. kilāya maliyiliruntu kal koṇṭu vantu cili ceyvatu nīṇṭakālamākat tamiḻarkaḷiṉ vaḻakkamāka iruntu vantirukkiṟatu.
tamiḻnāṭṭu viḷakkukaḷil cila, īḻa nāṭṭil kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl attīvil paḻam perum pāvi viḷakku oṉṟu kūṭa iṉṟu vari kāṇappaṭavilli. aṉittulaka ōviyap pulavar nantalālpō avarkaḷ tīṭṭiya putiya intiyap paḷḷi eṉṉum  uṇarcciyūṭṭum vaṇṇa ōviyam, aṉittulakak kaviñarākak karutum iravintiranāt tākūriṉ arum perum kavitiyic cittarippatākum. āṉāl nantalāl pō avarkaḷ tīṭṭiya ōviyattil oḷirum viḷakku, tamiḻnāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷ tīṭṭiya ōviyattiṉ piratipalippākavē tikaḻkiṟatu. inta apippirāyatti aṟiñarkaḷ palarum ātarikkiṉṟaṉar.
kācim pacāriluḷḷa makarājā maṉīntira cantira nanti avarkaḷ cērttuḷḷa paḻiya viḷakkukaḷil oṉṟu kiṭṭattaṭṭat tamiḻ nāṭṭu viḷakkukaḷiṉ vaṭivi uṭiyatākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ivviḷakku irācaputaṉattiliruntu koṇṭu varappaṭṭatākum.
tamiḻakattil iṉṟu cātāraṇamākak kāṇappaṭukiṟa paktiyi niṉivūṭṭum pāvi viḷakkukaḷiviṭa rātā - kōpi uruva viḷakku viṇava uṇarcciyi ūṭṭuvippatāka irukkiṟatu eṉṟu vaṭa nāṭṭu viṇava aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟārkaḷ.
cāvakattil paṭimaṅkaḷ
cāvaka nāṭṭiluḷḷa paṭimaṅkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiluḷḷa paṭimaṅ kaḷōṭu mika neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭiyaṉavāyk kāṭciyaḷik kiṉṟaṉa. cāvakattil kāṇakkiṭakkum viḷakkukaḷit tamiḻakatti luḷḷa viḷakkukaḷōṭu oppiṭṭup pārppatu uḷḷattiṟku uvaki ūṭṭuvatāka uḷḷatu. ulāntā nāṭṭiluḷḷa leyṭaṉ ricca etṉō kirāpik tolporuḷ kāṭci cāliyil uḷḷa mika aḻakāṉa mūṉṟu cāvaka nāṭṭu viḷakkukaḷi iṅku nām kuṟip piṭāmal viṭṭu viṭamuṭiyavilli.
aṅkuḷḷa kiṉṉari viḷakku tamiḻ nāṭṭu viḷakkukaḷōṭu oppuvami kūṟattakkatāka illi. eṉṟālum cātirōktam uṭiyatum karuttu āḻam uṭiyatum uṟutiyāṉatumāy viḷaṅku kiṟatu. ivviḷakkukaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭup pāvi viḷakkiṉiṉṟu eḻunta karutti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu amikkappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟalām. āṉāl inta viḷakkiṉ mūlak karuttukkaḷum kaṟpaṉic ciṟappum itaṟkē contamāṉatu; kaṭaṉ vāṅkappaṭṭa taṉṟu eṉṟu evarum kūṟamuṭiyātu.
iṅku tīṭṭappaṭṭa cāvaka viḷakkukaḷil maṉita uruva muḷḷavikaḷum uṇṭu. āṉāl avi tamiḻakattiluḷḷa eḻil viḷakkukaḷiṉ - tīpa laṭcumi viḷakkukaḷiṉ - tōṟṟatti uṭiyaṉa vāka illi.
āṉāl aṟiñarkaḷ palarum tamiḻ nāṭṭu viḷakkukaḷ cāvakac ciṟpikaḷukku evvita uṇarcciyiyum tūṇṭi viṭṭiruppatākak karuta muṭiyātu eṉṟu kōṭiṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. iti nām ēṟṟuk koḷḷa muṭiyavilli; kaṇṭippāka maṟuttē kūṟa vēṇṭiyatāka irukkiṟatu.
 mēlum cāvaka viḷakku iṟakkikaḷiyuṭiya oru miruka uruvatti uṭiyatāka irukkiṟatu. itu cāvakattiṉ contamāṉa ciṟpa nuṭpatti eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvatāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. iṟakkiyiṉ eḻil nalam ciṟpac ciṟappu, ciṅkaḷa nāṭṭuc ciṟpa muṟiyi ottirunta pōtilum paṟavi uruvattik kēntiramākak koṇṭa taṉmiyi ārāyntāl tamiḻ nāṭṭuc ciṟpattōṭō ciṅkaḷa nāṭṭuc ciṟpat tōṭā oṟṟumi uṭiyatāka illi eṉṟu ciṟap viṟpaṉṉarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.
ciṅkaḷam, cāvakam, puṭpakam, maliyam, tāylāntu, kaṭāram, kampōcakam mutaliya nāṭukaḷic cōḻar, pāṇṭiyar, pallavar pōṉṟa tamiḻnāṭṭu maṉṉarkaḷ veṉṟu aṅku taṅkaḷ camayatti yum kōyilkaḷiyum nākarikattiyum  āṭciyiyum paṇpāṭṭi yum nilināṭṭit taṅkaḷ ciṟpatti vaḷarttu vantaṉar. aṉṟu avarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭuc ciṟpa aṭippaṭiyil ākkiya arumperum kaṟṟaḷikaḷum paṭimaṅkaḷum ciṟpaṅkaḷum icikaḷum kavitikaḷum ōviyamum iṉṟum poṉṟātu kāṭci aḷittuk koṇṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa.
taṉināyaka aṭikaḷār tūtu
jÄHf¤â‹ jt¥ ngwhf¤ njh‹¿a ngu¿P® kiw¤âU lh¡l® jÅehaf mofŸ v«.V.v«.È£., pi.ec.ṭi. avarkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ tūtarākac cāvakam, malināṭu, ciṅkapuram pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷukku 1955il ceṉṟār. aṅku maṟintu varum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ nākarikattiyum kaliyiṉ ciṟappiyum, ciṟpa vaḷattiyum viḷakkiṉ ciṟappiyum kaṇṭu aṭikaḷār terivittuḷḷa karuttu cintikkattakkatākum.
pāvi viḷakkē paḻantamiḻarkaḷ kaṇṭa viḷakkukaḷil pavittiramāṉatu; aḻakil mikkatu. atu paktiyiṉ ciṉṉamāka oḷirkiṟatu. varalāṟṟuk karuvūlamāka miḷirkiṟatu. tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ ciṟpac ciṟappiṟku oru ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭāka viḷaṅkukiṟatu. namatu makkaḷ muṟkālattil peṟṟirunta nākarikattiṟku or nakalākat tikaḻkiṟatu.
pāvi viḷakkukaḷ vīṭṭu viḷakkalla; kōyil viḷakkākum. ivikaḷ tiruvaṟiyiṉ arukē mūlavarukku iru maruṅkiṉum niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻnāṭṭup peṇ orutti irukikaḷilum akal viḷakkoṉṟi ēnti niṟpatu pōl ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ivikaḷ kallilum cempilum ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ulōkat tāl ceyyappaṭṭa pāvi viḷakkē ciṟpa aḻaku poliyat tikaḻkiṟatu.
intap pāvi viḷakkiṉ karaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum kiṇṇam pōṉṟa akalil neyvārttu tiriyiṭṭu eriyumāṟu ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷatu.
ivvulōka viḷakkukaḷ ceyvatil ciramam atikam uṇṭu. paṇamum mikac celavākum. intap pāvi viḷakkukaḷ ciṟcila vēṟupāṭukaḷuṭaṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa periya kōyilkaḷi lellām kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivviḷakkukaḷil perumpālum peṇ vaṭivamākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pāvi eṉṟa peyarē peṇṇi uṇarttuvatāl peṇ viḷakkāka itaṉik kaṇṭatu niyāyamēyākum. āṉāl oru cila  kōyilkaḷil āṇ pāvi viḷakkukaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cila āṇkaḷ taṅkaḷukku nōy tīrntāl cuvāmikku ivvita māṉa viḷakkik kāṇikkiyākac celuttukiṟēṉ eṉṟu nērcci ceytu koṇṭirukkalām. avarukku nōy tīrntum taṉ vaṭivil (āṇ vaṭivil) oru viḷakkuc ceytu kōyilukku arp paṇittirukkalām allatu ciṟpi, ellāk kōyilum peṇ vaṭivam vāynta pāvi viḷakkuttāṉē irukkiṟatu - ēṉ? - nām āṇvaṭivamāṉa pāvi viḷakkic ceytu vikkakkūṭātu eṉṟu eṇṇi ittakiya viḷakki uruvākkiyum irukkalām.
ellā ūrkaḷilum  - ēṉ - kaṇapati āṇ vaṭivamāka iruk kiṟār eṉpatip paṟṟi oru ciṟpiyiṉ cintaṉiyil oru piraccaṉi eḻuntatu; uṭaṉē avaṉ peṇ vaṭivil oru kaṇapatiyi - kaṇēcaṉiyi uruvākkiviṭṭāṉ. yāṉi mukamum tutikkiyum iruntālum mārpil iru koṅkikaḷ kāṇumpaṭi ceytu viṭṭāṉ. iti iṉṟum cucīntiram kōyilil kāṇalām. oru vēḷi intac ciṟpiyiṉ kūṭṭatti cērntavarkaḷ pāvi viḷakki āṇvaṭivilum amittirukkalām eṉṟu eṇṇu kiṟēṉ.
namatu nāṭṭil eṇṇaṟṟa eḻil oḻukum pāvi viḷakkukaḷ uṇṭu. tirunelvēli nelliyappar kōyililum, maturi mīṉāṭci cuntarēcuvarar kōyililum, tañci māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa puḷḷirukkum vēḷūr eṉṟu kūṟum vittīvaraṉ kōyililum uḷḷa vittiya nāta cuvāmi cannitāṉattilum ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu māvaṭṭattic cērnta tirumullivāyil mācilāmaṇīvarar kōyililum, tiruviṭimarutūr jōtirmakāliṅka mūrttiyiṉ cannitiyilum, tirukkuṟṟālam kuṟṟālanātar kōyililum, tirukkō karṇam (putukkōṭṭip pakkattiluḷḷatu) kōkarṇēcuvarar - pirakātāmpāḷ cannitiyilum, tañcip peruvuṭiyār cannitiyi lum kāṇappaṭum pāvi viḷakkukaḷ mika aḻaku vāynta viḷakku kaḷākum.
tirunelvēli nelliyappar - kāntimati ammaṉ kōyi lilum, cucīntaram tāṇumālayaṉ kōyililum, tiruvaṉantapuram patmaṉāmacuvāmi kōyililum, maturi mīṉāṭci - cuntarēcuvarar kōyililum uḷḷa kaṟṟūṇkaḷilē eṇṇaṟṟa pāvi viḷakkukaḷ cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. it tūṇkaḷil cilavaṟṟil pāvikaḷ viḷakkēnti niṟpataṟkup patilāka āṇ vaṭivaṅkaḷum cila viḷakku kaḷi iru karaṅkaḷil ēntik koṇṭu niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
aḻakiṟkē uriya āparaṇaṅkaḷuṭaṉ aṉicca malar pōṉṟa aḻakiya karaṅkaḷil viḷakki ēnti niṟkum pēraḻakiṉi inta āṇ viḷakkil kāṇa muṭiyavilli. mēlum āṇ vaṭivaṅkaḷ tāṅki niṟkum viḷakkup paṭimattil āṭiyum aṇiyum aḻaku payappaṉa vāka evarum karuta muṭiyātu.
pāvi viḷakkukaḷilē tāmari pōṉṟa mukamum kārmēkam pōṉṟa kūntalum aḻakiya caṭiyum taliyilum, kātilum, mūkki lum, kaḻuttilum pujattilum, kikaḷilum viralkaḷilum kāṇap paṭum palvēṟu vitamāṉa navamaṇikaḷ patitta poṉṉāparaṇaṅ kaḷum veḷḷi āparaṇaṅkaḷum pala vaṇṇa niṟam vāynta paṭṭāṭikaḷum avikaḷiṉ aḻakiya viḷimpilum muṉtāṉi yilum tīṭṭappaṭṭa aḻakiya kōṭukaḷum ilikaḷum āṭiyiṉ uṭalil kāṇappaṭum pūṅkottukaḷum koṭikaḷum paṭṭi kaḷum, mārpil kaṭṭappaṭṭa pūṅkaccikaḷum piṟavum aḻakukku aḻaku ceyvatu pōl peṇṇiṉ iyaṟki aḻaki mēlum uyarttuvaṉavāka irukkiṉṟaṉa. āṇ viḷakkilō intak kali amcam ciṟitum kāṇappaṭavilli.
kōvilkaḷilum tolporuḷ kāṭcicālikaḷilum kāṇappaṭum pāvi viḷakkukaḷ paḻimiyāṉatākak kāṇappaṭa villi eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ apippirāyam cariyākavō tappākavō irukkalām. āṉāl pāvi viḷakku kaḷiṉ uruva amippu paḻaṅkāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kāṇak kiṭak kiṉṟaṉa.
ivviḷakkukaḷ cumār eṇṇūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē ceyyappaṭṭuk kōyilkaḷukku nivantamāka aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpataṟku ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ maṭṭumalla, kalveṭṭuc cāṉṟu kaḷum uḷa.
muṟkālattil tamiḻakattil intira viḻā eṉṉum oru paṇṭiki ciṟappākak koṇṭāṭappaṭṭu vantatu.
ivviḻā irupatteṭṭu nāṭkaḷ mikavum āṭamparamākavum kōlākalamākavum koṇṭāṭappaṭṭu varukiyil cōḻa maṉṉaṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ, cōḻa makkaḷukku,  avarkaḷiṉ illaṅ kaḷic cuttañ ceytu - alaṅkāram ceytu vītikaḷil pāvi viḷakkukaḷi variciyāka vittu intira viḻāvic ciṟappākak koṇṭāṭumāṟu kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ eṉṟum, ivaṉuṭiya kālam ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭu eṉṟum aṟikiṟōm.
pāvi viḷakkiṉ perumi paḻantamiḻ ēṭukaḷilellām parantu kiṭakkiṉṟatu eṉṟālum iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭic ciṟappittuk kūṟum neṭunal vāṭiyil pāvi viḷakkiṉ uruva amippukaḷip paṟṟiya karuttukkaḷit tamiḻarkaḷ, urōmarkaḷ, kirēkkarkaḷ pōṉṟa veḷināṭṭāriṭamiruntu peṟṟut tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ paṇpukkēṟṟavāṟu uruvākkiyuḷḷārkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇuvataṟku ēṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
āṉāl avarkaḷ veḷināṭṭu viḷakkik kaṇṭatum anta vaṭivil iṅkum ceytu koṇṭārkaḷ eṉṟu colluvataṟ killi. yavaṉa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vanta pāvi viḷakkiṟkum, ōtima viḷakkiṟkum evvita campantam illātatupōl tamiḻnāṭṭil ceyta viḷakkukaḷ - tamiḻar paṇpāṭṭiṟku ēṟṟāṟpōṉṟu ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
	yavaṉa riyaṟṟiya viṉimāṇ pāvi neycorintu
	parūut tirikoḷ iya kuruuttali nimireri
eṉṟa neṭunal vāṭiyiṉ pāṭṭiṟku, yavaṉarkaḷāl tam kittiṟaṉ viḷaṅka mika nuṇṇiya vēlippāṭukaḷōṭu aḻakuṟa amikkap paṭṭa aṇaṅkukaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ kikaḷil neyyit tāṅki niṟkum kiṇṇattiluḷḷa tiriyiliruntu eḻum ceñcuṭariṉiṉṟu veḷiccam pirakācikkum eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷappaṭum. itaṉiṉṟu yavaṉarkaḷiṭam aḻakiya pāvi viḷakkukaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatu naṉku aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu.
aimperum kāppiyaṅkaḷil oṉṟāka viḷaṅkukiṟa maṇi mēkali, terukkaḷum, vīṭukaḷum tōraṇaṅkaḷāl aḻaku ceyyap paṭṭu makkaḷ kūṭum māperu maṇimaṇṭapattil poṟkuṭaṅkaḷum aṟputap pāviviḷakkukaḷum pāṅkuṟa vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. atu varumāṟu
	tōraṇa.............. tōmaru kōṭṭiyum
	pūraṇakumpamum polam pālikikaḷum
	pāvi viḷakkum palavitam parappumiṉ	(maṇi : 43 : 45)
tiruviḻāk kālaṅkaḷil alaṅkārattiṟkup pāvi viḷakku kaḷ vikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟum avipoṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟum kikaḷil akal viḷakkukaḷi ēnti niṟpatu pōl amikkappaṭṭavi eṉṟum mullippāṭṭu kūṟukiṟatu.
	pāvi viḷakkiṟ parūuc cuṭar aḻala
eṉṟum,
	kiyami viḷakkam	(mulli - 1: 49)
eṉṟum atu kūṟukiṟatu.
cilappatikāramāṉatu, intira viḻā pūmpukāril naṭipeṟu kiyil terukkaḷiyellām alaṅkarittu maṅkalam poritta makaratōraṇaṅkaḷ vāyiltōṟum viḷaṅka, vītikaḷellām taṇṇīr niṟinta poṟkuṭaṅkaḷum, pālikip poruḷkaḷum patumi viḷakkukaḷum poṟkoṭikaḷum, veṇpoṟ tāmari kaḷum niṟintu vilaṅkiṉa. eṅkum naṟumaṇa nāṟṟam vīciyatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. atu varumāṟu :
	tōraṇam niliiya, tōmaṟu pacumpoṉ
	pūraṇa kumpattup polinta pāliki,
	pāvi viḷakkup pacumpoṉ paṭāki,
	tūmayirk kavari cuntarac cuṇṇattu
	mēviya koḷki vītiyil ceṟintu āṅku					(cilappatikāram 152 : 156)
iḵtaṉṟi, namatu viḷakkukaḷil aṉṉam pōṉṟa paṟavikaḷi vittuc ceyyum karuttukkaḷ yavaṉariṭamum iruntaṉa eṉṟu eṇṇa iṭamuṇṭu.
eṭuttukkāṭṭāka,
	........................................... yavaṉar
	ōtima viḷakkiṉ uyarmicik koṇṭa
	vikuṟu mīṉiṟ piyakat tōṉṟu
	nīrppeyaṟ ṟelli	(perum 11 : 316 - 319)
eṉṟa perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭi eṉṉum paḻantamiḻ pāṭṭil, yavaṉarkaḷiṉ marakkalaṅkaḷiluḷḷa kūmpukaḷil kaṭṭap peṟṟa cittira vēlippāṭaminta aṉṉa viḷakkukaḷ aṇipeṟa oḷirnta vivaraṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
